===== api/bootstrap/app.php ===== (90 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Http\Middleware\EnsureFacilityIsActive;
use App\Http\Middleware\ResolveFacility;
use Illuminate\Auth\AuthenticationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\ModelNotFoundException;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Exceptions;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Middleware;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Sanctum\Http\Middleware\EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful;
use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\HttpExceptionInterface;

return Application::configure(basePath: dirname(__DIR__))
    ->withRouting(
        web: __DIR__.'/../routes/web.php',
        api: __DIR__.'/../routes/api.php',
        commands: __DIR__.'/../routes/console.php',
        health: '/up',
    )
    ->withMiddleware(function (Middleware $middleware): void {
        $middleware->alias([
            'facility.active' => EnsureFacilityIsActive::class,
            'facility.resolve' => ResolveFacility::class,
        ]);

        $middleware->api(prepend: [
            EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful::class,
        ]);
    })
    ->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $exceptions): void {
        $exceptions->shouldRenderJsonWhen(
            fn (Request $request) => $request->is('api/*') || $request->expectsJson(),
        );

        $exceptions->render(function (ValidationException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'validation.failed',
                    'message' => 'Les données envoyées sont invalides.',
                    'fields' => $e->errors(),
                ],
            ], 422);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (AuthenticationException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'auth.unauthenticated',
                    'message' => 'Authentification requise.',
                ],
            ], 401);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (ModelNotFoundException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'resource.not_found',
                    'message' => 'Ressource introuvable.',
                ],
            ], 404);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (HttpExceptionInterface $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'http.'.$e->getStatusCode(),
                    'message' => $e->getMessage() ?: 'Erreur serveur.',
                ],
            ], $e->getStatusCode());
        });
    })->create();

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

<?php

use App\Models\User;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Defaults
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option defines the default authentication "guard" and password
    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    |
    */

    'defaults' => [
        'guard' => env('AUTH_GUARD', 'web'),
        'passwords' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_BROKER', 'users'),
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    | which utilizes session storage plus the Eloquent user provider.
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | Supported: "session"
    |
    */

    'guards' => [
        'web' => [
            'driver' => 'session',
            'provider' => 'users',
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User Providers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    | providers to represent the model / table. These providers may then
    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    |
    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    |
    */

    'providers' => [
        'users' => [
            'driver' => 'eloquent',
            'model' => env('AUTH_MODEL', User::class),
        ],

        // 'users' => [
        //     'driver' => 'database',
        //     'table' => 'users',
        // ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Resetting Passwords
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | These configuration options specify the behavior of Laravel's password
    | reset functionality, including the table utilized for token storage
    | and the user provider that is invoked to actually retrieve users.
    |
    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    |
    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    |
    */

    'passwords' => [
        'users' => [
            'provider' => 'users',
            'table' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TABLE', 'password_reset_tokens'),
            'expire' => 60,
            'throttle' => 60,
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Password Confirmation Timeout
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may define the number of seconds before a password confirmation
    | window expires and users are asked to re-enter their password via the
    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
    |
    */

    'password_timeout' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_TIMEOUT', 10800),

];

    |

            'lock_table' => env('DB_CACHE_LOCK_TABLE'),
        ],

            'lock_path' => storage_path('framework/cache/locks'),
        ],

            'persistent_id' => env('MEMCACHED_PERSISTENT_ID'),
            'sasl' => [
                env('MEMCACHED_USERNAME'),
                env('MEMCACHED_PASSWORD'),
            ],
            'options' => [
                // Memcached::OPT_CONNECT_TIMEOUT => 2000,
            ],
            'servers' => [
                [
                    'host' => env('MEMCACHED_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
                    'port' => env('MEMCACHED_PORT', 11211),
                    'weight' => 100,
                ],
            ],
        ],

        'redis' => [
            'driver' => 'redis',
            'connection' => env('REDIS_CACHE_CONNECTION', 'cache'),
            'lock_connection' => env('REDIS_CACHE_LOCK_CONNECTION', 'default'),
        ],

        'dynamodb' => [
            'driver' => 'dynamodb',
            'key' => env('AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID'),

            'stores' => [
                'database',
                'array',
            ],
        ],

    /*

    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    'prefix' => env('CACHE_PREFIX', Str::slug((string) env('APP_NAME', 'laravel')).'-cache-'),

    /*

    'serializable_classes' => false,

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Session Driver
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines the default session driver that is utilized for
    | incoming requests. Laravel supports a variety of storage options to
    | persist session data. Database storage is a great default choice.
    |
    | Supported: "file", "cookie", "database", "memcached",
    |            "redis", "dynamodb", "array"
    |
    */

    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'database'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Lifetime
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session
    | to be allowed to remain idle before it expires. If you want them
    | to expire immediately when the browser is closed then you may
    | indicate that via the expire_on_close configuration option.
    |
    */

    'lifetime' => (int) env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),

    'expire_on_close' => env('SESSION_EXPIRE_ON_CLOSE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Encryption
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option allows you to easily specify that all of your session data
    | should be encrypted before it's stored. All encryption is performed
    | automatically by Laravel and you may use the session like normal.
    |
    */

    'encrypt' => env('SESSION_ENCRYPT', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session File Location
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When utilizing the "file" session driver, the session files are placed
    | on disk. The default storage location is defined here; however, you
    | are free to provide another location where they should be stored.
    |
    */

    'files' => storage_path('framework/sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Connection
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" or "redis" session drivers, you may specify a
    | connection that should be used to manage these sessions. This should
    | correspond to a connection in your database configuration options.
    |
    */

    'connection' => env('SESSION_CONNECTION'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Table
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" session driver, you may specify the table to
    | be used to store sessions. Of course, a sensible default is defined
    | for you; however, you're welcome to change this to another table.
    |
    */

    'table' => env('SESSION_TABLE', 'sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cache Store
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using one of the framework's cache driven session backends, you may
    | define the cache store which should be used to store the session data
    | between requests. This must match one of your defined cache stores.
    |
    | Affects: "dynamodb", "memcached", "redis"
    |
    */

    'store' => env('SESSION_STORE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Sweeping Lottery
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Some session drivers must manually sweep their storage location to get
    | rid of old sessions from storage. Here are the chances that it will
    | happen on a given request. By default, the odds are 2 out of 100.
    |
    */

    'lottery' => [2, 100],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may change the name of the session cookie that is created by
    | the framework. Typically, you should not need to change this value
    | since doing so does not grant a meaningful security improvement.
    |
    */

    'cookie' => env(
        'SESSION_COOKIE',
        Str::slug((string) env('APP_NAME', 'laravel')).'-session'
    ),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Path
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | The session cookie path determines the path for which the cookie will
    | be regarded as available. Typically, this will be the root path of
    | your application, but you're free to change this when necessary.
    |
    */

    'path' => env('SESSION_PATH', '/'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Domain
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value determines the domain and subdomains the session cookie is
    | available to. By default, the cookie will be available to the root
    | domain without subdomains. Typically, this shouldn't be changed.
    |
    */

    'domain' => env('SESSION_DOMAIN'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTPS Only Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | By setting this option to true, session cookies will only be sent back
    | to the server if the browser has a HTTPS connection. This will keep
    | the cookie from being sent to you when it can't be done securely.
    |
    */

    'secure' => env('SESSION_SECURE_COOKIE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTP Access Only
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will prevent JavaScript from accessing the
    | value of the cookie and the cookie will only be accessible through
    | the HTTP protocol. It's unlikely you should disable this option.
    |
    */

    'http_only' => env('SESSION_HTTP_ONLY', true),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Same-Site Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines how your cookies behave when cross-site requests
    | take place, and can be used to mitigate CSRF attacks. By default, we
    | will set this value to "lax" to permit secure cross-site requests.
    |
    | See: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Headers/Set-Cookie#samesitesamesite-value
    |
    | Supported: "lax", "strict", "none", null
    |
    */

    'same_site' => env('SESSION_SAME_SITE', 'lax'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Partitioned Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will tie the cookie to the top-level site for
    | a cross-site context. Partitioned cookies are accepted by the browser
    | when flagged "secure" and the Same-Site attribute is set to "none".
    |
    */

    'partitioned' => env('SESSION_PARTITIONED_COOKIE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Serialization
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value controls the serialization strategy for session data, which
    | is JSON by default. Setting this to "php" allows the storage of PHP
    | objects in the session but can make an application vulnerable to
    | "gadget chain" serialization attacks if the APP_KEY is leaked.
    |
    | Supported: "json", "php"
    |
    */

    'serialization' => 'json',

];


===== api/database/factories/AppointmentFactory.php ===== (62 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

class AppointmentFactory extends Factory
{
    protected $model = Appointment::class;

    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'facility_id' => Facility::factory(),
            'patient_id' => Patient::factory(),
            'practitioner_id' => User::factory(),
            'created_by' => User::factory(),
            'scheduled_at' => Carbon::now()->addDays(random_int(1, 30)),
            'duration_minutes' => 30,
            'status' => 'scheduled',
            'reason' => 'Consultation de suivi',
            'notes' => null,
        ];
    }

    private function randLetters(int $length): string
    {
        $letters = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= $letters[random_int(0, strlen($letters) - 1)];
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randDigits(int $length): string
    {
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= (string) random_int(0, 9);
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randPhone(): string
    {
        return '+229'.$this->randDigits(8);
    }

    private function randFrom(array $items): string
    {
        return $items[array_rand($items)];
    }
}

<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\Drug;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;

class DrugFactory extends Factory
{
    protected $model = Drug::class;

    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => 'Médicament '.$this->randLetters(3).' '.random_int(100, 1000).'mg',
            'form' => $this->randFrom(['comprimé', 'sirop', 'injectable', 'gélule']),
            'dosage' => random_int(1, 10).'mg',
            'unit' => $this->randFrom(['boîte', 'flacon', 'ampoule']),
            'is_active' => true,
        ];
    }

    private function randLetters(int $length): string
    {
        $letters = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= $letters[random_int(0, strlen($letters) - 1)];
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randDigits(int $length): string
    {
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= (string) random_int(0, 9);
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randPhone(): string
    {
        return '+229'.$this->randDigits(8);
    }

    private function randFrom(array $items): string
    {
        return $items[array_rand($items)];
    }
}

            'phone' => $this->randPhone(),
            'email' => 'contact'.$this->randDigits(4).'@clinique.test',
            'is_active' => true,
        ];
    }

    public function inactive(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn () => ['is_active' => false]);
    }

    private function randLetters(int $length): string
    {
        $letters = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= $letters[random_int(0, strlen($letters) - 1)];
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randDigits(int $length): string
    {
        $result = '';
        for ($i = 0; $i < $length; $i++) {
            $result .= (string) random_int(0, 9);
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function randPhone(): string
    {
        return '+229'.$this->randDigits(8);
    }

    private function randFrom(array $items): string

        return $items[array_rand($items)];

===== api/database/factories/UserFactory.php ===== (46 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * @extends Factory<User>
 */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    /**
     * The current password being used by the factory.
     */
    protected static ?string $password;

    /**
     * Define the model's default state.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'email_verified_at' => now(),
            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
     */
    public function unverified(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ]);
    }
}

===== api/database/seeders/BillingPermissionsSeeder.php ===== (31 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class BillingPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('billing.view');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('billing.manage');

        $map = [
            'admin' => [$view, $manage],
            'comptable' => [$view, $manage],
            'medecin' => [$view],
        ];

        foreach ($map as $roleName => $permissions) {
            $role = Role::findOrCreate($roleName);
            $role->givePermissionTo($permissions);
        }

        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}

===== api/database/seeders/FacilitiesSeeder.php ===== (72 lignes, 2 Ko)
<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class FacilitiesSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('facilities.view', 'web');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('facilities.manage', 'web');

        Role::findByName('admin', 'web')->givePermissionTo([$view, $manage]);

        foreach (['medecin', 'infirmier', 'secretaire', 'laborantin', 'pharmacien', 'comptable'] as $roleName) {
            Role::findOrCreate($roleName, 'web')->givePermissionTo($view);
        }

        $csm = Facility::firstOrCreate(
            ['code' => 'CSM'],
            [
                'name' => 'Clinique Saint-Michel',
                'city' => 'Cotonou',
                'address' => 'Zone résidentielle, Cotonou',
                'phone' => '+229 21 00 00 00',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

        $href = Facility::firstOrCreate(
            ['code' => 'HREF'],
            [
                'name' => 'Hôpital de Référence',
                'city' => 'Porto-Novo',
                'address' => 'Avenue de la République, Porto-Novo',
                'phone' => '+229 21 00 00 01',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

        $admin = User::where('email', 'admin@hospiflow.local')->firstOrFail();
        $admin->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$csm->id, $href->id]);

        if (! $admin->current_facility_id) {
            $admin->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $csm->id])->save();
        }

        $secretary = User::firstOrCreate(
            ['email' => 'secretaire@hospiflow.local'],
            [
                'name' => 'Secrétaire Démarrage',
                'password' => 'hospiflow_sec',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );
        $secretary->assignRole('secretaire');
        $secretary->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$csm->id]);

        if (! $secretary->current_facility_id) {
            $secretary->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $csm->id])->save();
        }
    }
}

===== api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php ===== (55 lignes, 2 Ko)
<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class RolesAndPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

        $matrix = [
            'users.manage' => ['admin'],
            'patients.view' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'infirmier', 'secretaire'],
            'patients.manage' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'secretaire'],
            'appointments.manage' => ['admin', 'secretaire', 'infirmier'],
            'lab.results.manage' => ['admin', 'laborantin'],
            'pharmacy.manage' => ['admin', 'pharmacien'],
            'billing.manage' => ['admin', 'comptable'],
            'reports.view' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'comptable'],
        ];

        foreach (array_keys($matrix) as $permissionName) {
            Permission::findOrCreate($permissionName, 'web');
        }

        foreach ($matrix as $permissionName => $roleNames) {
            foreach ($roleNames as $roleName) {
                Role::findOrCreate($roleName, 'web')
                    ->givePermissionTo($permissionName);
            }
        }

        Role::findByName('admin', 'web')
            ->syncPermissions(array_keys($matrix));

        $user = User::firstOrCreate(
            ['email' => 'admin@hospiflow.local'],
            [
                'name' => 'Administrateur HOSPIFLOW',
                'password' => 'hospiflow_admin',
                'phone' => null,
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

        $user->assignRole('admin');
    }
}

===== api/routes/api.php ===== (90 lignes, 5 Ko)
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AppointmentController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AuditLogController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AuthController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ConsultationController;
use App\Http\Controllers\FacilityController;
use App\Http\Controllers\InvoiceController;
use App\Http\Controllers\LabRequestController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PatientController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PharmacyController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ReportController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

// --- Public ---
Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json(['status' => 'ok']));

Route::post('/auth/login', [AuthController::class, 'login'])
    ->middleware('throttle:5,1');

// --- Authentifié (Sanctum) ---
Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {

    // Auth (pas de facility requise)
    Route::get('/auth/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
    Route::post('/auth/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);

    // Établissements (pas de facility active requise — on peut switcher)
    Route::get('/facilities', [FacilityController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/facilities', [FacilityController::class, 'store']);
    Route::patch('/facilities/{facility}', [FacilityController::class, 'update']);
    Route::post('/facilities/{facility}/switch', [FacilityController::class, 'switch']);
    Route::post('/facilities/{facility}/users', [FacilityController::class, 'attachUser']);

    // --- Routes métier (facility active requise) ---
    Route::middleware('facility.active')->group(function () {

        // Patients
        Route::get('/patients', [PatientController::class, 'index']);
        Route::post('/patients', [PatientController::class, 'store']);
        Route::get('/patients/search', [PatientController::class, 'search']);
        Route::get('/patients/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'show']);
        Route::patch('/patients/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'update']);

        // Rendez-vous
        Route::get('/appointments', [AppointmentController::class, 'index']);
        Route::post('/appointments', [AppointmentController::class, 'store']);
        Route::get('/appointments/practitioners', [AppointmentController::class, 'practitioners']);
        Route::get('/appointments/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'show']);
        Route::patch('/appointments/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'update']);

        // Consultations
        Route::get('/consultations', [ConsultationController::class, 'index']);
        Route::post('/consultations', [ConsultationController::class, 'store']);
        Route::get('/consultations/{consultationId}', [ConsultationController::class, 'show']);
        Route::patch('/consultations/{consultationId}', [ConsultationController::class, 'update']);

        // Laboratoire
        Route::get('/lab-requests', [LabRequestController::class, 'index']);
        Route::post('/lab-requests', [LabRequestController::class, 'store']);
        Route::get('/lab-requests/{labRequestId}', [LabRequestController::class, 'show']);
        Route::patch('/lab-requests/{labRequestId}', [LabRequestController::class, 'update']);
        Route::post('/lab-requests/{labRequestId}/result', [LabRequestController::class, 'storeResult']);

        // Pharmacie
        Route::get('/pharmacy/drugs', [PharmacyController::class, 'drugs']);
        Route::post('/pharmacy/drugs', [PharmacyController::class, 'storeDrug']);
        Route::get('/pharmacy/stock', [PharmacyController::class, 'stock']);
        Route::post('/pharmacy/stock', [PharmacyController::class, 'storeStock']);
        Route::get('/pharmacy/dispensations', [PharmacyController::class, 'dispensations']);
        Route::post('/pharmacy/dispensations', [PharmacyController::class, 'dispense']);
        Route::get('/pharmacy/dispensations/{dispensationId}', [PharmacyController::class, 'showDispensation']);

        // Facturation
        Route::get('/invoices', [InvoiceController::class, 'index']);
        Route::post('/invoices', [InvoiceController::class, 'store']);
        Route::get('/invoices/{invoiceId}', [InvoiceController::class, 'show']);
        Route::patch('/invoices/{invoiceId}', [InvoiceController::class, 'update']);
        Route::post('/invoices/{invoiceId}/payments', [InvoiceController::class, 'pay']);

        // Audit logs (Vague 2 - Lot B)
        Route::get('/audit-logs', [AuditLogController::class, 'index']);

        // Rapports
        Route::get('/reports/overview', [ReportController::class, 'overview']);
        Route::get('/reports/revenue', [ReportController::class, 'revenue']);
        Route::get('/reports/stock', [ReportController::class, 'stock']);
    });
});

===== api/tests/Feature/AppointmentTest.php ===== (64 lignes, 2 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

beforeEach(function () {
    [$this->user, $this->facility] = authUser(['patients.view', 'appointments.manage']);
    $this->patient = Patient::factory()->create(['facility_id' => $this->facility->id]);
    $this->practitioner = User::factory()->create(['current_facility_id' => $this->facility->id]);
    $this->practitioner->facilities()->attach($this->facility->id);
});

test('store creates an appointment', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/appointments', [
            'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $this->practitioner->id,
            'scheduled_at' => Carbon::now()->addDays(3)->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'duration_minutes' => 30,
            'reason' => 'Consultation de suivi',
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(201)
        ->assertJsonPath('appointment.status', 'scheduled')
        ->assertJsonPath('appointment.duration_minutes', 30);
});

test('store rejects appointment in the past', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/appointments', [
            'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $this->practitioner->id,
            'scheduled_at' => Carbon::now()->subDay()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(422);
});

test('store rejects conflicting appointment', function () {
    $start = Carbon::now()->addDays(3)->setTime(10, 0, 0);

    Appointment::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'practitioner_id' => $this->practitioner->id,
        'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
        'scheduled_at' => $start,
        'duration_minutes' => 30,
        'status' => 'scheduled',
    ]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/appointments', [
            'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $this->practitioner->id,
            'scheduled_at' => $start->copy()->addMinutes(15)->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'duration_minutes' => 30,
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(422);
    expect($response->json('error.message'))->toContain('Conflit');
});


===== api/tests/Feature/AuditLogTest.php ===== (96 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Patient;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

beforeEach(function () {
    [$this->user, $this->facility] = authUser(['patients.view', 'patients.manage', 'reports.view']);
});

test('creating a patient produces an activity log', function () {
    Activity::query()->delete();

    $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/patients', [
            'first_name' => 'Aminata',
            'last_name' => 'Soglo',
            'phone' => '+22997000001',
        ])
        ->assertStatus(201);

    $logs = Activity::where('log_name', 'patient')->get();
    expect($logs)->toHaveCount(1);

    $log = $logs->first();
    expect($log->event)->toBe('created');
    expect($log->subject_type)->toBe(Patient::class);
    expect($log->properties['attributes'])->toHaveKey('first_name', 'Aminata');
});

test('updating a patient records only the changed fields', function () {
    Activity::query()->delete();

    $patient = Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'first_name' => 'Jean',
        'last_name' => 'Dupont',
        'phone' => '+22991000001',
    ]);

    $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->patchJson('/api/patients/'.$patient->id, [
            'phone' => '+22991000002',
        ])
        ->assertStatus(200);

    $logs = Activity::where('log_name', 'patient')
        ->where('event', 'updated')
        ->get();

    expect($logs)->toHaveCount(1);
    $log = $logs->first();
    expect($log->properties['old'])->toHaveKey('phone', '+22991000001');
    expect($log->properties['attributes'])->toHaveKey('phone', '+22991000002');
});

test('audit-logs endpoint requires authentication', function () {
    $this->getJson('/api/audit-logs')->assertStatus(401);
});

test('audit-logs endpoint returns logs for the current facility', function () {
    Activity::query()->delete();

    // Crée un patient (déclenche un log)
    Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/audit-logs');

    $response->assertStatus(200)
        ->assertJsonStructure([
            'activity_logs',
            'pagination' => ['current_page', 'per_page', 'total'],
        ]);

    // Vérifie qu'au moins 1 log est retourné (pas juste la structure)
    expect($response->json('activity_logs'))->not->toBeEmpty();
});

test('audit-logs endpoint filters by subject_type', function () {
    Activity::query()->delete();

    Patient::factory()->create(['facility_id' => $this->facility->id]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/audit-logs?subject_type=Patient');

    $response->assertStatus(200);
    $data = $response->json('activity_logs');
    expect($data)->not->toBeEmpty();
    foreach ($data as $log) {
        expect($log['subject']['type'])->toBe(Patient::class);
    }
});


===== api/tests/Feature/AuthTest.php ===== (92 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;

beforeEach(function () {
    $this->facility = Facility::factory()->create();
    $this->user = User::factory()->create([
        'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
        'password' => Hash::make('password123'),
        'is_active' => true,
        'current_facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);
    $this->user->facilities()->attach($this->facility->id);
});

test('login returns a token with valid credentials', function () {
    $response = $this->postJson('/api/auth/login', [
        'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
        'password' => 'password123',
    ]);

    $response->assertStatus(200)
        ->assertJsonStructure([
            'token',
            'user' => ['id', 'name', 'email'],
        ]);

    expect($response->json('token'))->toBeString()->not->toBeEmpty();
});

test('login fails with invalid credentials', function () {
    $response = $this->postJson('/api/auth/login', [
        'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
        'password' => 'wrong-password',
    ]);

    $response->assertStatus(422)
        ->assertJsonPath('error.code', 'validation.failed');
});

test('login fails with inactive account', function () {
    $this->user->update(['is_active' => false]);

    $response = $this->postJson('/api/auth/login', [
        'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
        'password' => 'password123',
    ]);

    $response->assertStatus(403);
});

test('login is rate limited after 5 attempts', function () {
    for ($i = 0; $i < 5; $i++) {
        $this->postJson('/api/auth/login', [
            'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
            'password' => 'wrong-password',
        ]);
    }

    $response = $this->postJson('/api/auth/login', [
        'email' => 'test@hospiflow.dev',
        'password' => 'wrong-password',
    ]);

    $response->assertStatus(429);
});

test('me returns the authenticated user', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/auth/me');

    $response->assertStatus(200)
        ->assertJsonPath('user.id', $this->user->id)
        ->assertJsonPath('user.email', 'test@hospiflow.dev');
});

test('me requires authentication', function () {
    $this->getJson('/api/auth/me')->assertStatus(401);
});

test('logout invalidates the current token', function () {
    $token = $this->user->createToken('spa')->plainTextToken;

    $response = $this->withHeader('Authorization', 'Bearer '.$token)
        ->postJson('/api/auth/logout');

    $response->assertStatus(200);
    expect($this->user->tokens()->count())->toBe(0);
});

===== api/tests/Feature/InvoiceTest.php ===== (75 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Invoice;
use App\Models\Patient;

beforeEach(function () {
    [$this->user, $this->facility] = authUser(['billing.view', 'billing.manage']);
    $this->patient = Patient::factory()->create(['facility_id' => $this->facility->id]);
});

test('full invoice lifecycle: draft → issued → paid', function () {
    // 1. Créer une facture (draft)
    $createResp = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/invoices', [
            'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
            'items' => [
                ['label' => 'Consultation', 'quantity' => 1, 'unit_price' => 5000],
                ['label' => 'Médicament', 'quantity' => 2, 'unit_price' => 1500],
            ],
        ]);

    $createResp->assertStatus(201);
    $invoiceId = $createResp->json('invoice.id');
    expect($createResp->json('invoice.status'))->toBe('draft');
    expect($createResp->json('invoice.total_amount'))->toBe(8000);
    expect($createResp->json('invoice.number'))->toMatch('/^FAC-\d{5}$/');

    // 2. Émettre la facture
    $issueResp = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->patchJson('/api/invoices/'.$invoiceId, ['status' => 'issued']);

    $issueResp->assertStatus(200);
    expect($issueResp->json('invoice.status'))->toBe('issued');

    // 3. Payer partiellement (5000 sur 8000)
    $payResp = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/invoices/'.$invoiceId.'/payments', [
            'amount' => 5000,
            'method' => 'cash',
        ]);

    $payResp->assertStatus(200);
    expect($payResp->json('invoice.paid_amount'))->toBe(5000);
    expect($payResp->json('invoice.remaining_amount'))->toBe(3000);
    expect($payResp->json('invoice.status'))->toBe('issued');

    // 4. Payer le reste (3000)
    $payResp2 = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/invoices/'.$invoiceId.'/payments', [
            'amount' => 3000,
            'method' => 'mobile_money',
        ]);

    $payResp2->assertStatus(200);
    expect($payResp2->json('invoice.status'))->toBe('paid');
    expect($payResp2->json('invoice.remaining_amount'))->toBe(0);
});

test('cannot pay more than remaining amount', function () {
    $invoice = Invoice::factory()->issued()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'patient_id' => $this->patient->id,
        'total_amount' => 5000,
    ]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/invoices/'.$invoice->id.'/payments', [
            'amount' => 10000,
            'method' => 'cash',
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(422);
    expect($response->json('error.message'))->toContain('reste à payer');
});


===== api/tests/Feature/PatientTest.php ===== (104 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Patient;

beforeEach(function () {
    [$this->user, $this->facility] = authUser(['patients.view', 'patients.manage']);
});

test('index requires authentication', function () {
    $this->getJson('/api/patients')->assertStatus(401);
});

test('index returns empty list for new facility', function () {
    $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/patients')
        ->assertStatus(200)
        ->assertJsonPath('patients', [])
        ->assertJsonStructure(['facility' => ['id', 'code'], 'patients']);
});

test('store creates a new patient with local code', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/patients', [
            'first_name' => 'Jean',
            'last_name' => 'Dupont',
            'date_of_birth' => '1990-05-15',
            'gender' => 'male',
            'phone' => '+22991000001',
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(201)
        ->assertJsonPath('patient.first_name', 'Jean')
        ->assertJsonPath('patient.last_name', 'Dupont')
        ->assertJsonPath('patient.facility_id', $this->facility->id);

    $code = $response->json('patient.local_code');
    expect($code)->toMatch('/^P-'.strtoupper($this->facility->code).'-\d{4}$/');
});

test('store validates required fields', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/patients', []);

    $response->assertStatus(422)
        ->assertJsonPath('error.code', 'validation.failed')
        ->assertJsonStructure(['error' => ['code', 'message', 'fields']]);
});

test('store requires patients.manage permission', function () {
    [$userWithoutPerm, $facility] = authUser(['patients.view']);

    $response = $this->actingAs($userWithoutPerm, 'sanctum')
        ->postJson('/api/patients', [
            'first_name' => 'Jean',
            'last_name' => 'Dupont',
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(403);
});

test('search finds patient by first name (portable LOWER LIKE)', function () {
    Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'first_name' => 'Aminata',
        'last_name' => 'Soglo',
        'phone' => '+22997000001',
    ]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/patients/search?q=aminata');

    $response->assertStatus(200);
    expect($response->json('results'))->toHaveCount(1);
    expect($response->json('results.0.first_name'))->toBe('Aminata');
});

test('search returns empty for no match', function () {
    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->getJson('/api/patients/search?q=zzzznotfound');

    $response->assertStatus(200);
    expect($response->json('results'))->toBe([]);
});

test('update can clear a field with explicit null (Vague 1 fix)', function () {
    $patient = Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'first_name' => 'Jean',
        'last_name' => 'Dupont',
        'phone' => '+22991000001',
    ]);

    $response = $this->actingAs($this->user, 'sanctum')
        ->patchJson('/api/patients/'.$patient->id, [
            'phone' => null,
        ]);

    $response->assertStatus(200);
    expect($response->json('patient.phone'))->toBeNull();

    $patient->refresh();
    expect($patient->phone)->toBeNull();
});


===== api/tests/Feature/PolicyTest.php ===== (141 lignes, 5 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\DrugLot;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Invoice;
use App\Models\LabRequest;
use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;

beforeEach(function () {
    // Créer un user avec facility courante
    $this->facility = Facility::factory()->create();
    $this->otherFacility = Facility::factory()->create();

    $this->user = User::factory()->create([
        'current_facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);
    $this->user->facilities()->attach($this->facility->id);
});

// Helper : donne une permission à un user
function givePerm(User $user, string $perm): void
{
    Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => $perm]);
    $user->givePermissionTo($perm);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PatientPolicy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
test('PatientPolicy: user without permission cannot view patients', function () {
    expect($this->user->can('viewAny', Patient::class))->toBeFalse();
});

test('PatientPolicy: user with patients.view can view patients', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'patients.view');
    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('viewAny', Patient::class))->toBeTrue();
});

test('PatientPolicy: user cannot update patient from other facility', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'patients.manage');

    $otherPatient = Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->otherFacility->id,
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('update', $otherPatient))->toBeFalse();
});

test('PatientPolicy: user can update patient from same facility', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'patients.manage');

    $patient = Patient::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('update', $patient))->toBeTrue();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AppointmentPolicy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
test('AppointmentPolicy: user without appointments.manage cannot create', function () {
    expect($this->user->can('create', Appointment::class))->toBeFalse();
});

test('AppointmentPolicy: user with appointments.manage can create', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'appointments.manage');
    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('create', Appointment::class))->toBeTrue();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// InvoicePolicy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
test('InvoicePolicy: cannot update paid invoice', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'billing.manage');

    $invoice = Invoice::factory()->paid()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('update', $invoice))->toBeFalse();
});

test('InvoicePolicy: can update draft invoice', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'billing.manage');

    $invoice = Invoice::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'status' => 'draft',
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('update', $invoice))->toBeTrue();
});

test('InvoicePolicy: cannot cancel invoice with payments', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'billing.manage');

    $invoice = Invoice::factory()->issued()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
    ]);
    $invoice->payments()->create([
        'amount' => 5000,
        'method' => 'cash',
        'received_by' => $this->user->id,
        'paid_at' => now(),
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('cancel', $invoice))->toBeFalse();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// LabRequestPolicy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
test('LabRequestPolicy: cannot update completed lab request', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'lab.requests.manage');

    $labRequest = LabRequest::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'status' => 'completed',
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('update', $labRequest))->toBeFalse();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DrugLotPolicy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
test('DrugLotPolicy: cannot dispense from empty lot', function () {
    givePerm($this->user, 'pharmacy.manage');

    $lot = DrugLot::factory()->create([
        'facility_id' => $this->facility->id,
        'quantity' => 0,
    ]);

    expect($this->user->fresh()->can('dispense', $lot))->toBeFalse();
});

===== api/tests/Unit/PatientLikeHelperTest.php ===== (25 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\PatientController;

test('portableLike produces LOWER LIKE LOWER pattern', function () {
    $controller = new PatientController;
    $reflection = new ReflectionClass($controller);
    $method = $reflection->getMethod('portableLike');
    $method->setAccessible(true);

    $result = $method->invoke($controller, 'first_name');
    expect($result)->toBe('LOWER(first_name) LIKE LOWER(?)');
});

test('likePattern escapes special characters', function () {
    $controller = new PatientController;
    $reflection = new ReflectionClass($controller);
    $method = $reflection->getMethod('likePattern');
    $method->setAccessible(true);

    expect($method->invoke($controller, 'aminata'))->toBe('%aminata%');
    expect($method->invoke($controller, 'a%b'))->toBe('%a\\%b%');
    expect($method->invoke($controller, 'a_b'))->toBe('%a\\_b%');
});


===== api/tests/Pest.php ===== (44 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Tests\TestCase;

pest()->extend(TestCase::class)
    ->use(RefreshDatabase::class)
    ->in('Feature');

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Helpers globaux
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

/**
 * Crée un utilisateur authentifié avec une facility active et les permissions données.
 */
function authUser(array $permissions = [], ?Facility $facility = null): array
{
    $facility = $facility ?? Facility::factory()->create();

    /** @var User $user */
    $user = User::factory()->create([
        'current_facility_id' => $facility->id,
    ]);

    $user->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$facility->id]);

    // Crée les permissions si elles n'existent pas encore
    foreach ($permissions as $perm) {
        Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => $perm]);
    }

    if (! empty($permissions)) {
        $user->givePermissionTo($permissions);
    }

    return [$user, $facility];
}

===== api/AGENTS.md ===== (40 lignes, 1 Ko)
# HOSPIFLOW — Conventions projet

> Ce fichier remplace le bootstrap Laravel Boost (Vague 1).

## Stack

- Laravel 13 (API REST), PHP 8.3+, Sanctum (auth), Spatie Permissions (RBAC).
- Base : SQLite en dev, PostgreSQL en prod.
- Frontend SPA séparé (hors de api/).

## Architecture

- Multi-tenant par établissement : toute requête métier scopée via currentFacility().
- Un contrôleur = un module (M00→M10).
- Permissions : patients., appointments., consultations., lab., pharmacy., billing., reports., facilities.

## Conventions

- Messages utilisateur en français, code en anglais.
- Validation dans `$request->validate([...])` (Form Requests en Vague 2).
- Toujours répondre en JSON. Erreurs via `abort(4xx, 'message FR')`.
- Pas d'appel `request()` global dans les closures de transaction.

## Base de données

- Portabilité SQLite/PostgreSQL obligatoire : pas de ilike, to_char, extract.
- Utiliser LOWER(col) LIKE LOWER(?) et calculer les dérivés en PHP.
- Index sur facility_id, patient_id, status, scheduled_at.

## Sécurité

- Jamais de `exists:users,email` ni de message permettant d'énumérer les comptes.
- Routes protégées par auth:sanctum (sauf /login).
- Chaque contrôleur vérifie currentFacility()->is_active.

## Tests

- php artisan test avant chaque commit.
- Chaque contrôleur : Feature test happy path + 403 + 422.

## About Laravel

Laravel is a web application framework with expressive, elegant syntax. We believe development must be an enjoyable and creative experience to be truly fulfilling. Laravel takes the pain out of development by easing common tasks used in many web projects, such as:

- [Simple, fast routing engine](https://laravel.com/docs/routing).
- [Powerful dependency injection container](https://laravel.com/docs/container).
- Multiple back-ends for [session](https://laravel.com/docs/session) and [cache](https://laravel.com/docs/cache) storage.
- Expressive, intuitive [database ORM](https://laravel.com/docs/eloquent).
- Database agnostic [schema migrations](https://laravel.com/docs/migrations).
- [Robust background job processing](https://laravel.com/docs/queues).
- [Real-time event broadcasting](https://laravel.com/docs/broadcasting).

Laravel is accessible, powerful, and provides tools required for large, robust applications.

===== scripts/m04/backend.mjs ===== (444 lignes, 14 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 (2/4) : backend — migration, modèle, contrôleur, routes
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { API, ok, fail, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Migration ---
  const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
  const migrationExists = fs
    .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .some((f) => f.includes('create_appointments_table'));

if (migrationExists) {
    ok('Migration déjà présente');
  } else {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
      path.join(migrationsDir, timestamp + '_create_appointments_table.php'),
      String.raw`<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('appointments', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('patient_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('practitioner_id')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('created_by')->nullable()->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete();
            $table->timestamp('scheduled_at');
            $table->unsignedSmallInteger('duration_minutes')->default(30);
            $table->string('status', 20)->default('scheduled');
            $table->string('reason', 500)->nullable();
            $table->text('notes')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->index(['facility_id', 'scheduled_at']);
            $table->index(['practitioner_id', 'scheduled_at']);
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('appointments');
    }
};
`
    );
    ok('Migration appointments créée');
  }

// --- Modèle Appointment ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'Appointment.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class Appointment extends Model
{
    public const STATUSES = ['scheduled', 'confirmed', 'done', 'cancelled', 'no_show'];

protected $fillable = [
        'facility_id',
        'patient_id',
        'practitioner_id',
        'created_by',
        'scheduled_at',
        'duration_minutes',
        'status',
        'reason',
        'notes',
    ];

protected $casts = [
        'scheduled_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

public function facility(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Facility::class);
    }

public function patient(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Patient::class);
    }

public function practitioner(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'practitioner_id');
    }

public function scopeInFacility($query, Facility $facility)
    {
        return $query->where('facility_id', $facility->id);
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Contrôleur AppointmentController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'AppointmentController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

class AppointmentController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$query = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->orderBy('scheduled_at');

$date = $request->query('date');

if ($date) {
            $query->whereDate('scheduled_at', $date);
        }

return response()->json([
            'appointments' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($a) => $this->payload($a)),
        ]);
    }

public function practitioners(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$users = $facility->users()
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get(['id', 'name']);

return response()->json([
            'practitioners' => $users->map(fn ($u) => [
                'id' => $u->id,
                'name' => $u->name,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'practitioner_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:users,id'],
            'scheduled_at' => ['required', 'date', 'after:now'],
            'duration_minutes' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:5', 'max:480'],
            'reason' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'scheduled_at.after' => 'La date du rendez-vous doit être dans le futur.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'practitioner_id.exists' => 'Praticien inconnu.',
        ]);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

$practitioner = User::find($data['practitioner_id']);

if (! $practitioner->isMemberOf($facility)) {
            abort(422, 'Ce praticien n\'appartient pas à votre établissement.');
        }

$start = Carbon::parse($data['scheduled_at']);
        $duration = $data['duration_minutes'] ?? 30;

if ($this->hasConflict($facility, $practitioner->id, $start, $duration)) {
            abort(422, 'Conflit d\'agenda : le praticien a déjà un rendez-vous sur ce créneau.');
        }

$appointment = Appointment::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $practitioner->id,
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'scheduled_at' => $start,
            'duration_minutes' => $duration,
            'status' => 'scheduled',
            'reason' => $data['reason'] ?? null,
        ]);

return response()->json(
            ['appointment' => $this->payload($appointment->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

public function show(Request $request, int $appointmentId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($appointmentId);

return response()->json([
            'appointment' => $this->payload($appointment),
        ]);
    }

public function update(Request $request, int $appointmentId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($appointmentId);

$data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['sometimes', 'string', 'in:' . implode(',', Appointment::STATUSES)],
            'scheduled_at' => ['sometimes', 'date', 'after:now'],
            'duration_minutes' => ['sometimes', 'integer', 'min:5', 'max:480'],
            'reason' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'notes' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Statut invalide.',
        ]);

if (isset($data['scheduled_at'])) {
            $start = Carbon::parse($data['scheduled_at']);
            $duration = $data['duration_minutes'] ?? $appointment->duration_minutes;

if ($appointment->status !== 'cancelled') {
                if ($this->hasConflict($facility, $appointment->practitioner_id, $start, $duration, $appointment->id)) {
                    abort(422, 'Conflit d\'agenda : le praticien a déjà un rendez-vous sur ce créneau.');
                }
            }

$appointment->scheduled_at = $start;
        }

if (isset($data['duration_minutes'])) {
            $appointment->duration_minutes = $data['duration_minutes'];
        }

if (array_key_exists('reason', $data)) {
            $appointment->reason = $data['reason'];
        }

if (array_key_exists('notes', $data)) {
            $appointment->notes = $data['notes'];
        }

if (isset($data['status'])) {
            $appointment->status = $data['status'];
        }

$appointment->save();

return response()->json([
            'appointment' => $this->payload($appointment->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

private function hasConflict(
        Facility $facility,
        int $practitionerId,
        $start,
        int $durationMinutes,
        ?int $ignoreId = null
    ): bool {
        $end = (clone $start)->addMinutes($durationMinutes);
        $maxDuration = 480;

$query = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->where('practitioner_id', $practitionerId)
            ->whereIn('status', ['scheduled', 'confirmed'])
            ->where('scheduled_at', '<', $end)
            ->where('scheduled_at', '>=', (clone $start)->subMinutes($maxDuration));

if ($ignoreId !== null) {
            $query->where('id', '!=', $ignoreId);
        }

foreach ($query->get(['scheduled_at', 'duration_minutes']) as $existing) {
            $existingEnd = $existing->scheduled_at->addMinutes($existing->duration_minutes);

if ($existingEnd > $start) {
                return true;
            }
        }

return false;
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        $allowed = $request->user()->can('patients.view');

if (! $allowed) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de consulter les rendez-vous.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        $allowed = $request->user()->can('appointments.manage');

if (! $allowed) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de gérer les rendez-vous.');
        }
    }

private function payload(Appointment $a): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $a->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $a->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $a->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $a->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $a->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'practitioner' => [
                'id' => $a->practitioner->id,
                'name' => $a->practitioner->name,
            ],
            'scheduled_at' => $a->scheduled_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            'duration_minutes' => $a->duration_minutes,
            'status' => $a->status,
            'reason' => $a->reason,
            'notes' => $a->notes,
        ];
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Routes API ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AppointmentController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AuthController;
use App\Http\Controllers\FacilityController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PatientController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json([
    'app' => 'HOSPIFLOW',
    'status' => 'ok',
]));

Route::prefix('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
        Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    });
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('facilities')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [FacilityController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [FacilityController::class, 'store']);
    Route::patch('/{facility}', [FacilityController::class, 'update'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/switch', [FacilityController::class, 'switch'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/users', [FacilityController::class, 'attachUser'])->scopeBindings();
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('patients')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [PatientController::class, 'index']);
    Route::get('/search', [PatientController::class, 'search']);
    Route::post('/', [PatientController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
    Route::patch('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('appointments')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/practitioners', [AppointmentController::class, 'practitioners']);
    Route::get('/', [AppointmentController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [AppointmentController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('appointmentId');
    Route::patch('/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('appointmentId');
});
`
  );

ok('Modèle, contrôleur et routes écrits');
}


===== scripts/m04/e2e.mjs ===== (266 lignes, 9 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — rendez-vous & agenda
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8013;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M04/5', 'Test de bout en bout — rendez-vous & agenda');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }
  }

function toLocalIso(date) {
    return date.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const meResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/me', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const meData = await meResponse.json();
    const adminUserId = meData.user.id;
    ok('Praticien de test : admin id=' + adminUserId);

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (secrétaire → CSM, admin → HREF)');

const patientsResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });
    const patientsData = await patientsResponse.json();
    const demoPatient = patientsData.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-CSM-0001'
    );

if (!demoPatient) fail('Patient P-CSM-0001 introuvable dans CSM.');

// Créneau « demain 09:00 » : recalculé à chaque exécution.
    const slot = new Date();
    slot.setDate(slot.getDate() + 1);
    slot.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
    const slotIso = toLocalIso(slot);

const overlap = new Date(slot.getTime() + 10 * 60 * 1000);
    const overlapIso = toLocalIso(overlap);

// Test 1 : création → 201
    const createResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: demoPatient.id,
        practitioner_id: adminUserId,
        scheduled_at: slotIso,
        duration_minutes: 30,
        reason: 'Consultation générale',
      }),
    });

if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Création de rendez-vous : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Création de rendez-vous par la secrétaire (201)');

const created = (await createResponse.json()).appointment;

if (created.status !== 'scheduled') {
      fail('Statut initial invalide : ' + created.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Statut initial « scheduled »');

// Test 2 : liste
    const listResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (listResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Liste rendez-vous : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + listResponse.status + '.');
    }

const listData = await listResponse.json();
    const found = listData.appointments.find((a) => a.id === created.id);

if (!found) fail('Le rendez-vous créé ne figure pas dans la liste CSM.');
    ok('Rendez-vous visible dans la liste (CSM)');

if (found.patient.local_code !== 'P-CSM-0001') {
      fail('Le patient rattaché au rendez-vous est incorrect.');
    }

if (found.practitioner.id !== adminUserId) {
      fail('Le praticien rattaché au rendez-vous est incorrect.');
    }
    ok('Patient et praticien correctement rattachés');

// Test 3 : conflit → 422
    const conflictResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: demoPatient.id,
        practitioner_id: adminUserId,
        scheduled_at: overlapIso,
        duration_minutes: 30,
        reason: 'Tentative de chevauchement',
      }),
    });

if (conflictResponse.status !== 422) {
      fail('Conflit d\'agenda : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + conflictResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Créneau chevauchant rejeté par détection de conflit (422)');

// Test 4 : annulation
    const cancelResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments/' + created.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'cancelled' }),
    });

if (cancelResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Annulation : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + cancelResponse.status + '.');
    }

const cancelled = (await cancelResponse.json()).appointment;

if (cancelled.status !== 'cancelled') {
      fail('Statut après annulation invalide : ' + cancelled.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Annulation du rendez-vous (statut « cancelled »)');

// Test 5 : le créneau annulé redevient disponible → 201
    const rebookResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: demoPatient.id,
        practitioner_id: adminUserId,
        scheduled_at: slotIso,
        duration_minutes: 30,
        reason: 'Reprise du créneau annulé',
      }),
    });

if (rebookResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Re-réservation du créneau : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + rebookResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Créneau annulé re-réservable (201)');

// Test 6 : isolation — l'admin (HREF) ne voit pas le RDV de CSM
    const hrefShowResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments/' + created.id, {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (hrefShowResponse.status !== 404) {
      fail('Isolation : HTTP 404 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + hrefShowResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Rendez-vous d\'un autre établissement inaccessible (404)');

const hrefListResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const hrefList = await hrefListResponse.json();
    const leaked = hrefList.appointments.find((a) => a.id === created.id);

if (leaked) fail('Isolation rompue : le RDV de CSM apparaît dans la liste HREF.');
    ok('Le RDV de CSM est absent de la liste HREF');

// Test 7 : accès anonyme → 401
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });

if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 (3/4) : webapp Vue — page Rendez-vous
import path from 'node:path';

export function installFrontend() {
  // --- Routeur ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
    String.raw`import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
