  );

// --- Page Rendez-vous ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'AppointmentsView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const appointments = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const practitioners = ref([]);
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const filterDate = ref(today);

const patientId = ref('');
const practitionerId = ref('');
const scheduledAt = ref('');
const durationMinutes = ref(30);
const reason = ref('');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  scheduled: 'Planifié',
  confirmed: 'Confirmé',
  done: 'Réalisé',
  cancelled: 'Annulé',
  no_show: 'Absent',
};

async function loadAppointments() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = filterDate.value
      ? '?date=' + encodeURIComponent(filterDate.value)
      : '';
    const data = await api('/appointments' + params, { token: auth.token });
    appointments.value = data.appointments;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function loadReferences() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
  try {
    const practitionersData = await api('/appointments/practitioners', {
      token: auth.token,
    });
    practitioners.value = practitionersData.practitioners;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createAppointment() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        practitioner_id: Number(practitionerId.value),
        scheduled_at: scheduledAt.value,
        duration_minutes: Number(durationMinutes.value),
        reason: reason.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    practitionerId.value = '';
    scheduledAt.value = '';
    durationMinutes.value = 30;
    reason.value = '';
    await loadAppointments();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(appointmentId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/appointments/' + appointmentId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadAppointments();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadReferences();
  await loadAppointments();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">

    </header>

<section class="content">

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadAppointments">
        <label>

            </td>
            <td>{{ appointment.practitioner.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ appointment.reason || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ appointment.duration_minutes }} min</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="appointment.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[appointment.status] || appointment.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="appointment.status === 'scheduled'"
                @click="setStatus(appointment.id, 'confirmed')"
              >
                Confirmer

              <button
                v-if="appointment.status === 'confirmed'"

                v-if="appointment.status !== 'cancelled'"

            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else-if="!loading">Aucun rendez-vous pour cette date.</p>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createAppointment">
        <h2>Nouveau rendez-vous</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

===== scripts/m04/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}


===== scripts/m05/backend.mjs ===== (427 lignes, 14 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M05 (2/4) : backend — consultations & permissions cliniques
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { API, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Migration consultations ---
  const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
  const migrationExists = fs
    .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .some((f) => f.includes('create_consultations_table'));

if (migrationExists) {
    ok('Migration déjà présente');
  } else {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
      path.join(migrationsDir, timestamp + '_create_consultations_table.php'),
      String.raw`<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('consultations', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('patient_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('appointment_id')->nullable()->constrained()->nullOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('practitioner_id')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('created_by')->nullable()->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete();
            $table->string('chief_complaint', 1000);
            $table->text('examination')->nullable();
            $table->string('diagnosis', 1000)->nullable();
            $table->text('treatment')->nullable();
            $table->date('follow_up_date')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->index(['facility_id', 'patient_id']);
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('consultations');
    }
};
`
    );
    ok('Migration consultations créée');
  }

// --- Modèle Consultation ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'Consultation.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class Consultation extends Model
{
    protected $fillable = [
        'facility_id',
        'patient_id',
        'appointment_id',
        'practitioner_id',
        'created_by',
        'chief_complaint',
        'examination',
        'diagnosis',
        'treatment',
        'follow_up_date',
    ];

protected $casts = [
        'follow_up_date' => 'date',
    ];

public function facility(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Facility::class);
    }

public function patient(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Patient::class);
    }

public function practitioner(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'practitioner_id');
    }

public function appointment(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Appointment::class);
    }

public function scopeInFacility($query, Facility $facility)
    {
        return $query->where('facility_id', $facility->id);
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Contrôleur ConsultationController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'ConsultationController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ConsultationController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$query = Consultation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

$patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

return response()->json([
            'consultations' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($c) => $this->payload($c)),
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'appointment_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:appointments,id'],
            'chief_complaint' => ['required', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'examination' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'diagnosis' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'treatment' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'follow_up_date' => ['nullable', 'date'],
        ], [
            'chief_complaint.required' => 'Le motif de consultation est obligatoire.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'appointment_id.exists' => 'Rendez-vous inconnu.',
        ]);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

$appointment = null;

if (! empty($data['appointment_id'])) {
            $appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)->find($data['appointment_id']);

if (! $appointment) {
                abort(422, 'Ce rendez-vous n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

$consultation = Consultation::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'appointment_id' => $appointment?->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $request->user()->id,
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'chief_complaint' => $data['chief_complaint'],
            'examination' => $data['examination'] ?? null,
            'diagnosis' => $data['diagnosis'] ?? null,
            'treatment' => $data['treatment'] ?? null,
            'follow_up_date' => $data['follow_up_date'] ?? null,
        ]);

if ($appointment && in_array($appointment->status, ['scheduled', 'confirmed'], true)) {
            $appointment->status = 'done';
            $appointment->save();
        }

return response()->json(
            ['consultation' => $this->payload($consultation->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

public function show(Request $request, int $consultationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($consultationId);

return response()->json([
            'consultation' => $this->payload($consultation),
        ]);
    }

public function update(Request $request, int $consultationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($consultationId);

$data = $request->validate([
            'chief_complaint' => ['sometimes', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'examination' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
            'diagnosis' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'treatment' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
            'follow_up_date' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'date'],
        ]);

foreach (['chief_complaint', 'examination', 'diagnosis', 'treatment', 'follow_up_date'] as $field) {
            if (array_key_exists($field, $data)) {
                $consultation->{$field} = $data[$field];
            }
        }

$consultation->save();

return response()->json([
            'consultation' => $this->payload($consultation->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        $allowed = $request->user()->can('consultations.view');

if (! $allowed) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de consulter les dossiers cliniques.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        $allowed = $request->user()->can('consultations.manage');

if (! $allowed) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de rédiger les comptes-rendus.');
        }
    }

private function payload(Consultation $c): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $c->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $c->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $c->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $c->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $c->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'practitioner' => [
                'id' => $c->practitioner->id,
                'name' => $c->practitioner->name,
            ],
            'appointment_id' => $c->appointment_id,
            'chief_complaint' => $c->chief_complaint,
            'examination' => $c->examination,
            'diagnosis' => $c->diagnosis,
            'treatment' => $c->treatment,
            'follow_up_date' => $c->follow_up_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'created_at' => $c->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Seeder additif : permissions cliniques ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'ConsultationsPermissionsSeeder.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class ConsultationsPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('consultations.view');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('consultations.manage');

$map = [
            'admin' => [$view, $manage],
            'medecin' => [$view, $manage],
            'infirmier' => [$view],
        ];

foreach ($map as $roleName => $permissions) {
            $role = Role::findOrCreate($roleName);
            $role->givePermissionTo($permissions);
        }

app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Routes API (auth + facilities + patients + appointments + consultations) ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AppointmentController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AuthController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ConsultationController;
use App\Http\Controllers\FacilityController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PatientController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json([
    'app' => 'HOSPIFLOW',
    'status' => 'ok',
]));

Route::prefix('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
        Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    });
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('facilities')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [FacilityController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [FacilityController::class, 'store']);
    Route::patch('/{facility}', [FacilityController::class, 'update'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/switch', [FacilityController::class, 'switch'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/users', [FacilityController::class, 'attachUser'])->scopeBindings();
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('patients')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [PatientController::class, 'index']);
    Route::get('/search', [PatientController::class, 'search']);
    Route::post('/', [PatientController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
    Route::patch('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('appointments')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/practitioners', [AppointmentController::class, 'practitioners']);
    Route::get('/', [AppointmentController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [AppointmentController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('appointmentId');
    Route::patch('/{appointmentId}', [AppointmentController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('appointmentId');
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('consultations')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [ConsultationController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [ConsultationController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{consultationId}', [ConsultationController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('consultationId');
    Route::patch('/{consultationId}', [ConsultationController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('consultationId');
});
`
  );

ok('Migration, modèle, contrôleur, seeder et routes écrits');
}


===== scripts/m05/e2e.mjs ===== (317 lignes, 11 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M05 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — consultations & suivi clinique
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8015;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M05/5', 'Test de bout en bout — consultations & suivi clinique');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }
  }

function toLocalIso(date) {
    return date.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const meData = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/me', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const adminUserId = meData.user.id;
    ok('Praticien de test : admin id=' + adminUserId);

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (admin → HREF, secrétaire → CSM)');

const hrefPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const hrefPatient = hrefPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-HREF-0001'
    );

if (!hrefPatient) fail('Patient P-HREF-0001 introuvable dans HREF.');

const csmPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csmPatient = csmPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-CSM-0001'
    );

if (!csmPatient) fail('Patient P-CSM-0001 introuvable dans CSM.');

// Test 1 : rendez-vous support (créneau unique par exécution)
    const slot = new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 86400000);
    slot.setMinutes(slot.getMinutes() + (process.pid % 55));

const createAppointment = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        practitioner_id: adminUserId,
        scheduled_at: toLocalIso(slot),
        duration_minutes: 30,
        reason: 'Consultation de suivi',
      }),
    });

if (createAppointment.status !== 201) {
      fail('Rendez-vous support : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createAppointment.status + '.');
    }

const appointment = (await createAppointment.json()).appointment;
    ok('Rendez-vous support créé dans HREF (201)');

// Test 2 : création du compte-rendu lié au RDV
    const followUpIso = new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

const createResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        appointment_id: appointment.id,
        chief_complaint: 'Douleurs abdominales depuis 48 h',
        examination: 'Palpation sensible au quadrant inférieur droit. T 38,2 °C.',
        diagnosis: 'Suspicion d\'appendicite aiguë',
        treatment: 'Biologie sanguine et échographie abdominale, avis chirurgical.',
        follow_up_date: followUpIso,
      }),
    });

if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Création de consultation : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createResponse.status + '.');
    }

const created = (await createResponse.json()).consultation;
    ok('Compte-rendu de consultation créé (201)');

if (created.diagnosis !== 'Suspicion d\'appendicite aiguë') {
      fail('Diagnostic non enregistré.');
    }
    ok('Motif, examen, diagnostic, traitement et date de suivi enregistrés');

// Test 3 : le RDV lié passe automatiquement en « done »
    const appointmentAfter = await (await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/appointments/' + appointment.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    )).json();

if (appointmentAfter.appointment.status !== 'done') {
      fail('Le RDV lié n\'a pas été marqué « done » (statut : ' + appointmentAfter.appointment.status + ').');
    }
    ok('Rendez-vous automatiquement marqué « done »');

// Test 4 : mise à jour du diagnostic
    const updateResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations/' + created.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ diagnosis: 'Appendicite aiguë confirmée' }),
    });

if (updateResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Mise à jour : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + updateResponse.status + '.');
    }

const updated = (await updateResponse.json()).consultation;

if (updated.diagnosis !== 'Appendicite aiguë confirmée') {
      fail('Diagnostic non mis à jour.');
    }
    ok('Mise à jour du diagnostic (200)');

// Test 5 : liste des consultations de l'établissement
    const listResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const listData = await listResponse.json();
    const found = listData.consultations.find((c) => c.id === created.id);

if (!found) fail('La consultation créée ne figure pas dans la liste HREF.');
    ok('Consultation visible dans la liste (HREF)');

// Test 6 : historique filtré par patient
    const filterUrl = baseUrl + '/api/consultations?patient_id=' + hrefPatient.id;
    const filterResponse = await fetch(filterUrl, {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const filterData = await filterResponse.json();
    const wrongPatient = filterData.consultations.find(
      (c) => c.patient.id !== hrefPatient.id
    );

if (wrongPatient) fail('Le filtre patient renvoie d\'autres patients.');
    ok('Historique par patient opérationnel');

// Test 7 : patient d'un autre établissement rejeté → 422
    const crossPatient = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        chief_complaint: 'Tentative cross-établissement',
      }),
    });

if (crossPatient.status !== 422) {
      fail('Patient cross-établissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + crossPatient.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Patient d\'un autre établissement rejeté (422)');

// Test 8 : rendez-vous d'un autre établissement rejeté → 422
    const csmAppointments = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/appointments', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csmAppointment = csmAppointments.appointments[0];

if (csmAppointment) {
      const crossAppointment = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({
          patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
          appointment_id: csmAppointment.id,
          chief_complaint: 'Tentative cross-établissement (RDV)',
        }),
      });

if (crossAppointment.status !== 422) {
        fail('RDV cross-établissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + crossAppointment.status + '.');
      }
      ok('Rendez-vous d\'un autre établissement rejeté (422)');
    } else {
      ok('Aucun RDV CSM disponible : test croisé RDV ignoré');
    }

// Test 9 : secrétaire sans accès clinique → 403 partout
    const secList = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (secList.status !== 403) {
      fail('Liste (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secList.status + '.');
    }

const secShow = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations/' + created.id, {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (secShow.status !== 403) {
      fail('Détail (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secShow.status + '.');
    }

const secCreate = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        chief_complaint: 'Tentative secrétaire',
      }),
    });

if (secCreate.status !== 403) {
      fail('Création (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secCreate.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Secrétaire sans accès clinique : liste, détail et création rejetés (403)');

// Test 10 : accès anonyme rejeté
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });

if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}


===== scripts/m05/frontend.mjs ===== (375 lignes, 11 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M05 (3/4) : webapp Vue — page Consultations
import path from 'node:path';
import { WEB, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installFrontend() {
  // --- Routeur ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
    String.raw`import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/consultations',
      component: () => import('../views/ConsultationsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
  );

// --- Page Consultations ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'ConsultationsView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const consultations = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const appointments = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const filterPatientId = ref('');

const patientId = ref('');
const appointmentId = ref('');
const chiefComplaint = ref('');
const examination = ref('');
const diagnosis = ref('');
const treatment = ref('');
const followUpDate = ref('');

async function loadConsultations() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const suffix = filterPatientId.value
      ? '?patient_id=' + filterPatientId.value
      : '';
    const data = await api('/consultations' + suffix, { token: auth.token });
    consultations.value = data.consultations;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadReferences() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
  try {
    const appointmentsData = await api('/appointments', { token: auth.token });
    appointments.value = appointmentsData.appointments;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createConsultation() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/consultations', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        appointment_id: appointmentId.value ? Number(appointmentId.value) : null,
        chief_complaint: chiefComplaint.value.trim(),
        examination: examination.value.trim() || null,
        diagnosis: diagnosis.value.trim() || null,
        treatment: treatment.value.trim() || null,
        follow_up_date: followUpDate.value || null,
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    appointmentId.value = '';
    chiefComplaint.value = '';
    examination.value = '';
    diagnosis.value = '';
    treatment.value = '';
    followUpDate.value = '';
    await loadConsultations();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadReferences();
  await loadConsultations();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Consultations</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadConsultations">
        <label>
          Historique du patient
          <select v-model="filterPatientId">
            <option value="">Tous les patients</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="consultations.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Praticien</th>
            <th>Motif</th>
            <th>Diagnostic</th>
            <th>Suivi</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="consultation in consultations" :key="consultation.id">
            <td>{{ consultation.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ consultation.patient.last_name }} {{ consultation.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ consultation.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ consultation.practitioner.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ consultation.chief_complaint }}</td>
            <td>{{ consultation.diagnosis || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ consultation.follow_up_date || '—' }}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else>Aucune consultation enregistrée.</p>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createConsultation">
        <h2>Nouvelle consultation</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Rendez-vous lié (optionnel)
          <select v-model="appointmentId">
            <option value="">Aucun</option>
            <option v-for="appointment in appointments" :key="appointment.id" :value="appointment.id">
              {{ appointment.scheduled_at }} — {{ appointment.patient.last_name }}
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Motif de consultation
          <input v-model="chiefComplaint" required placeholder="Douleurs abdominales…" />
        </label>

<label>
          Examen clinique
          <textarea v-model="examination" rows="3"></textarea>
        </label>

<label>
          Diagnostic
          <input v-model="diagnosis" placeholder="Suspicion d'appendicite aiguë" />
        </label>

<label>
          Traitement / conduite à tenir
          <textarea v-model="treatment" rows="3"></textarea>
        </label>

<label>
          Date de suivi
          <input v-model="followUpDate" type="date" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Enregistrer le compte-rendu</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>

// --- Tableau de bord : lien Consultations ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'DashboardView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();
const router = useRouter();
const currentFacilityName = ref('');

async function logout() {
  await auth.logout();
  await router.push('/login');
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const facilities = await auth.loadFacilities();
    const current = facilities.find(
      (facility) => facility.id === auth.currentFacilityId
    );
    currentFacilityName.value = current ? current.name : '';
  } catch {
    currentFacilityName.value = '';
  }
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="dash">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
        <span>{{ auth.user?.name }}</span>
        <button @click="logout">Déconnexion</button>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Bienvenue, {{ auth.user?.name }}</h1>
      <p>
        Rôles :
        <span v-for="role in auth.roles" :key="role" class="badge">{{ role }}</span>
      </p>
      <p v-if="currentFacilityName">
        Établissement actif : <strong>{{ currentFacilityName }}</strong>
      </p>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
.dash { min-height: 100vh; }
header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 1rem 2rem;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgb(0 0 0 / 6%);
}
header strong { color: #0d6efd; letter-spacing: .05em; }
nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1rem; }
nav a { color: #1f2937; text-decoration: none; }
nav a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
nav button { padding: .4rem .9rem; border: 0; border-radius: 8px; background: #ef4444; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; }
.content { padding: 2rem; }
.badge { display: inline-block; margin-right: .4rem; padding: .15rem .6rem; border-radius: 999px; background: #e0e7ff; color: #3730a3; font-size: .85rem; }
</style>
`
  );

ok('Fichiers frontend écrits');
}


===== scripts/m05/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M06 (2/4) : backend — laboratoire, demandes & résultats
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { API, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Migration lab_requests + lab_results ---
  const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
  const migrationExists = fs
    .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .some((f) => f.includes('create_lab_tables'));

if (migrationExists) {
    ok('Migration déjà présente');
  } else {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
      path.join(migrationsDir, timestamp + '_create_lab_tables.php'),
      String.raw`<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('lab_requests', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('patient_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('consultation_id')->nullable()->constrained()->nullOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('requested_by')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('panel', 200);
            $table->string('priority', 20)->default('routine');
            $table->string('status', 20)->default('pending');
            $table->string('notes', 500)->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->index(['facility_id', 'status']);
            $table->index(['facility_id', 'patient_id']);
        });

Schema::create('lab_results', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('lab_request_id')->unique()->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->text('values');
            $table->string('conclusion', 1000)->nullable();
            $table->foreignId('resulted_by')->nullable()->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete();
            $table->timestamp('resulted_at')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('lab_results');
        Schema::dropIfExists('lab_requests');
    }
};
`
    );
    ok('Migration laboratoire créée');
  }

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasOne;

class LabRequest extends Model
{
    public const STATUSES = ['pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'];

protected $fillable = [
        'facility_id',
        'patient_id',
        'consultation_id',
        'requested_by',
        'panel',
        'priority',
        'status',
        'notes',
    ];

public function patient(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Patient::class);
    }

public function requester(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'requested_by');
    }

public function consultation(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Consultation::class);
    }

public function result(): HasOne
    {
        return $this->hasOne(LabResult::class);
    }

public function scopeInFacility($query, Facility $facility)
    {
        return $query->where('facility_id', $facility->id);
    }
}
`

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class LabResult extends Model

protected $casts = [

public function labRequest(): BelongsTo

        return $this->belongsTo(LabRequest::class);

// --- Contrôleur LabRequestController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'LabRequestController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\LabRequest;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class LabRequestController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$query = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)
            ->with([
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name',
            ])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

$patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

$status = $request->query('status');

if ($status) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }

return response()->json([
            'lab_requests' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($r) => $this->payload($r)),
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeRequest($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'consultation_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:consultations,id'],
            'panel' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'priority' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:routine,urgent'],
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'panel.required' => 'Le type d\'analyse est obligatoire.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'consultation_id.exists' => 'Consultation inconnue.',
            'priority.in' => 'Priorité invalide.',
        ]);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

$consultation = null;

if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

$labRequest = LabRequest::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
            'requested_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'panel' => $data['panel'],
            'priority' => $data['priority'] ?? 'routine',
            'status' => 'pending',
            'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
        ]);

return response()->json(
            ['lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

public function show(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)
            ->with([
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name',
            ])
            ->findOrFail($labRequestId);

return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest),
        ]);
    }

public function update(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeRequest($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($labRequestId);

if ($labRequest->status === 'completed') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande est complétée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

$data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['required', 'string', 'in:' . implode(',', ['pending', 'in_progress', 'cancelled'])],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Statut invalide (la complétion passe par la saisie du résultat).',
        ]);

$labRequest->status = $data['status'];
        $labRequest->save();

return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

public function storeResult(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeResults($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($labRequestId);

if ($labRequest->status === 'cancelled') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande d\'analyse a été annulée.');
        }

if ($labRequest->status === 'completed') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande a déjà un résultat enregistré.');
        }

$data = $request->validate([
            'values' => ['required', 'string', 'max:4000'],
            'conclusion' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
        ], [
            'values.required' => 'Les valeurs des analyses sont obligatoires.',
        ]);

$labRequest->result()->updateOrCreate(
            [],
            [
                'values' => $data['values'],
                'conclusion' => $data['conclusion'] ?? null,
                'resulted_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'resulted_at' => now(),
            ]
        );

$labRequest->status = 'completed';
        $labRequest->save();

return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('lab.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas accès au laboratoire.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeRequest(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('lab.requests.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Seuls les médecins peuvent prescrire des analyses.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeResults(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('lab.results.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Seul le laboratoire peut saisir les résultats.');
        }
    }

private function payload(LabRequest $r): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $r->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $r->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $r->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $r->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $r->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'requested_by' => $r->requester?->name,
            'consultation_id' => $r->consultation_id,
            'panel' => $r->panel,
            'priority' => $r->priority,
            'status' => $r->status,
            'notes' => $r->notes,
            'result' => $r->result ? [
                'values' => $r->result->values,
                'conclusion' => $r->result->conclusion,
                'resulted_by' => $r->result->resultedBy?->name,
                'resulted_at' => $r->result->resulted_at?->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            ] : null,
            'created_at' => $r->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Seeder additif : permissions laboratoire ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'LabPermissionsSeeder.php'),

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class LabPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('lab.view');
        $manageRequests = Permission::findOrCreate('lab.requests.manage');
        $manageResults = Permission::findOrCreate('lab.results.manage');

$map = [
            'admin' => [$view, $manageRequests, $manageResults],
            'medecin' => [$view, $manageRequests],
            'infirmier' => [$view],
            'laborantin' => [$view, $manageResults],
        ];

foreach ($map as $roleName => $permissions) {
            $role = Role::findOrCreate($roleName);
            $role->givePermissionTo($permissions);
        }

app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}
`
  );
