===== scripts/m06/e2e.mjs ===== (395 lignes, 13 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M06 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — laboratoire
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8016;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M06/5', 'Test de bout en bout — laboratoire');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (admin → HREF, secrétaire → CSM)');

const hrefPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const hrefPatient = hrefPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-HREF-0001'
    );

if (!hrefPatient) fail('Patient P-HREF-0001 introuvable dans HREF.');

const csmPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csmPatient = csmPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-CSM-0001'
    );

if (!csmPatient) fail('Patient P-CSM-0001 introuvable dans CSM.');

// Consultation support (réutilisée si présente, créée sinon)
    const consultationsResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/consultations?patient_id=' + hrefPatient.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    );
    const consultationsData = await consultationsResponse.json();
    let consultation = consultationsData.consultations[0];

if (!consultation) {
      const createConsultation = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/consultations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({
          patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
          chief_complaint: 'Consultation support laboratoire',
        }),
      });

if (createConsultation.status !== 201) {
        fail('Consultation support : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createConsultation.status + '.');
      }

consultation = (await createConsultation.json()).consultation;
    }
    ok('Consultation support prête (id=' + consultation.id + ')');

// Test 1 : création de la demande d'analyse liée à la consultation
    const createResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        consultation_id: consultation.id,
        panel: 'NFS + CRP',
        priority: 'urgent',
        notes: 'Contrôle avant avis chirurgical',
      }),
    });

if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Demande d\'analyse : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createResponse.status + '.');
    }

const created = (await createResponse.json()).lab_request;

if (created.status !== 'pending') {
      fail('Statut initial invalide : ' + created.status + '.');
    }

if (created.consultation_id !== consultation.id) {
      fail('La demande n\'est pas liée à la consultation.');
    }
    ok('Demande d\'analyse créée, liée à la consultation (201, pending)');

// Test 2 : liste + filtres
    const listResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const listData = await listResponse.json();
    const found = listData.lab_requests.find((r) => r.id === created.id);

if (!found) fail('La demande créée ne figure pas dans la liste HREF.');

if (found.requested_by !== 'Admin') {
      // Le nom du prescripteur doit être présent (comparaison souple)
      if (!found.requested_by) fail('Prescripteur absent du rendu.');
    }
    ok('Demande visible dans la liste, prescripteur et patient corrects');

const filterResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests?patient_id=' + hrefPatient.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    );
    const filterData = await filterResponse.json();
    const wrongPatient = filterData.lab_requests.find(
      (r) => r.patient.id !== hrefPatient.id
    );

if (wrongPatient) fail('Le filtre patient renvoie d\'autres patients.');

const statusResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests?status=pending',
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    );
    const statusData = await statusResponse.json();
    const wrongStatus = statusData.lab_requests.find((r) => r.status !== 'pending');

if (wrongStatus) fail('Le filtre statut renvoie d\'autres statuts.');
    ok('Filtres par patient et par statut opérationnels');

// Test 3 : prise en charge
    const startResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + created.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'in_progress' }),
    });

if (startResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Prise en charge : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + startResponse.status + '.');
    }

const started = (await startResponse.json()).lab_request;

if (started.status !== 'in_progress') {
      fail('Statut après prise en charge invalide : ' + started.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Prise en charge de la demande (in_progress)');

// Test 4 : saisie du résultat
    const resultResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + created.id + '/result',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({
          values: 'Hb : 13,4 g/dL\nGB : 7 800/mm3\nCRP : 4 mg/L',
          conclusion: 'Bilan biologique sans particularité',
        }),
      }
    );

if (resultResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Saisie du résultat : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + resultResponse.status + '.');
    }

const completed = (await resultResponse.json()).lab_request;

if (completed.status !== 'completed') {
      fail('Statut après résultat invalide : ' + completed.status + '.');
    }

if (!completed.result || !completed.result.resulted_at) {
      fail('Résultat non enregistré ou non horodaté.');
    }

if (completed.result.conclusion !== 'Bilan biologique sans particularité') {
      fail('Conclusion non enregistrée.');
    }
    ok('Résultat saisi : statut completed, résultat horodaté et tracé');

// Test 5 : une demande complétée refuse un nouveau résultat
    const redoResult = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + created.id + '/result',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({ values: 'Tentative de doublon' }),
      }
    );

if (redoResult.status !== 422) {
      fail('Doublon de résultat : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + redoResult.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Résultat en double rejeté (422)');

// Test 6 : annulation + résultat sur demande annulée
    const secondCreate = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        panel: 'Glycémie à jeun',
      }),
    });

if (secondCreate.status !== 201) {
      fail('Seconde demande : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secondCreate.status + '.');
    }

const second = (await secondCreate.json()).lab_request;

const cancelResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + second.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'cancelled' }),
    });

if (cancelResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Annulation : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + cancelResponse.status + '.');
    }

const cancelled = (await cancelResponse.json()).lab_request;

if (cancelled.status !== 'cancelled') {
      fail('Statut après annulation invalide : ' + cancelled.status + '.');
    }

const resultOnCancelled = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + second.id + '/result',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({ values: 'Tentative sur demande annulée' }),
      }
    );

if (resultOnCancelled.status !== 422) {
      fail('Résultat sur demande annulée : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + resultOnCancelled.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Demande annulée : résultat refusé (422)');

// Test 7 : isolation — patient d'un autre établissement
    const crossPatient = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        panel: 'Tentative cross-établissement',
      }),
    });

if (crossPatient.status !== 422) {
      fail('Patient cross-établissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + crossPatient.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Patient d\'un autre établissement rejeté (422)');

// Test 8 : la secrétaire n'a aucun accès laboratoire
    const secList = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (secList.status !== 403) {
      fail('Liste (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secList.status + '.');
    }

const secCreate = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        panel: 'Tentative secrétaire',
      }),
    });

if (secCreate.status !== 403) {
      fail('Prescription (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secCreate.status + '.');
    }

const secResult = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/' + created.id + '/result',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({ values: 'Tentative secrétaire' }),
      }
    );

if (secResult.status !== 403) {
      fail('Résultat (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secResult.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Secrétaire sans accès laboratoire : liste, prescription et résultat rejetés (403)');

// Test 9 : isolation de la liste — CSM ne voit pas les demandes HREF
    // (la secrétaire est bloquée en 403, on vérifie via un GET admin sur un id inexistant)
    const wrongId = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests/999999', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (wrongId.status !== 404) {
      fail('Demande inexistante : HTTP 404 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + wrongId.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Demande hors périmètre introuvable (404)');

// Test 10 : accès anonyme
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/lab-requests', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });

if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}


===== scripts/m06/frontend.mjs ===== (487 lignes, 15 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M06 (3/4) : webapp Vue — page Laboratoire
import path from 'node:path';
import { WEB, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installFrontend() {
  // --- Routeur ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
    String.raw`import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/consultations',
      component: () => import('../views/ConsultationsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/laboratoire',
      component: () => import('../views/LabView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
  );

// --- Page Laboratoire ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'LabView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const labRequests = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const filterPatientId = ref('');
const filterStatus = ref('');

const patientId = ref('');
const panel = ref('');
const priority = ref('routine');
const notes = ref('');

const resultTargetId = ref(null);
const resultValues = ref('');
const resultConclusion = ref('');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  pending: 'En attente',
  in_progress: 'En cours',
  completed: 'Complétée',
  cancelled: 'Annulée',
};

async function loadLabRequests() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

if (filterPatientId.value) params.set('patient_id', filterPatientId.value);
    if (filterStatus.value) params.set('status', filterStatus.value);

const suffix = params.toString() ? '?' + params.toString() : '';
    const data = await api('/lab-requests' + suffix, { token: auth.token });
    labRequests.value = data.lab_requests;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createRequest() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        panel: panel.value.trim(),
        priority: priority.value,
        notes: notes.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    panel.value = '';
    priority.value = 'routine';
    notes.value = '';
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(requestId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests/' + requestId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

function openResultForm(requestId) {
  resultTargetId.value = requestId;
  resultValues.value = '';
  resultConclusion.value = '';
}

async function submitResult() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests/' + resultTargetId.value + '/result', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        values: resultValues.value.trim(),
        conclusion: resultConclusion.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    resultTargetId.value = null;
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadLabRequests();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Laboratoire</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadLabRequests">
        <label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="filterPatientId">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Statut
          <select v-model="filterStatus">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option value="pending">En attente</option>
            <option value="in_progress">En cours</option>
            <option value="completed">Complétée</option>
            <option value="cancelled">Annulée</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="labRequests.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Analyse</th>
            <th>Prescrit par</th>
            <th>Priorité</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th>Résultat</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="labRequest in labRequests" :key="labRequest.id">
            <td>{{ labRequest.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ labRequest.patient.last_name }} {{ labRequest.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ labRequest.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ labRequest.panel }}</td>
            <td>{{ labRequest.requested_by }}</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="labRequest.priority">
                {{ labRequest.priority === 'urgent' ? 'Urgent' : 'Routine' }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="labRequest.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[labRequest.status] || labRequest.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td>
              <template v-if="labRequest.result">
                {{ labRequest.result.conclusion || 'Saisi' }}
                <br />
                <small>{{ labRequest.result.resulted_at }} — {{ labRequest.result.resulted_by }}</small>
              </template>
              <template v-else>—</template>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending'"
                @click="setStatus(labRequest.id, 'in_progress')"
              >
                Prendre en charge
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending' || labRequest.status === 'in_progress'"
                @click="openResultForm(labRequest.id)"
              >
                Saisir résultat
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending' || labRequest.status === 'in_progress'"
                @click="setStatus(labRequest.id, 'cancelled')"
              >
                Annuler
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else>Aucune demande d'analyse.</p>

<form
        v-if="resultTargetId !== null"
        class="create"
        @submit.prevent="submitResult"
      >
        <h2>Saisie du résultat (demande #{{ resultTargetId }})</h2>

<label>
          Valeurs
          <textarea
            v-model="resultValues"
            rows="4"
            required
            placeholder="Hb : 13,4 g/dL&#10;GB : 7 800/mm³&#10;CRP : 4 mg/L"
          ></textarea>
        </label>

<label>
          Conclusion
          <input v-model="resultConclusion" placeholder="Bilan normal" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Valider le résultat</button>
        <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="resultTargetId = null">
          Annuler
        </button>
      </form>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createRequest">
        <h2>Nouvelle demande d'analyse</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Type d'analyse
          <input v-model="panel" required placeholder="NFS, glycémie, CRP…" />
        </label>

<label>
          Priorité
          <select v-model="priority">
            <option value="routine">Routine</option>
            <option value="urgent">Urgent</option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Notes
          <input v-model="notes" placeholder="Contexte clinique éventuel" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Prescrire</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
.page { min-height: 100vh; }
header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 1rem 2rem;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgb(0 0 0 / 6%);
}
header strong { color: #0d6efd; letter-spacing: .05em; }
nav { display: flex; gap: 1.2rem; }
nav a { color: #1f2937; text-decoration: none; }
nav a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
.content { padding: 2rem; max-width: 1100px; }
.error { color: #dc2626; }
.filters { display: flex; gap: .8rem; align-items: end; margin-bottom: 1.2rem; }
.filters label { display: grid; gap: .25rem; font-size: .9rem; }
.filters button { padding: .5rem 1rem; border: 0; border-radius: 8px; background: #0d6efd; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; }
table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; background: #fff; border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; }
th, td { padding: .55rem .8rem; border-bottom: 1px solid #e5e7eb; text-align: left; font-size: .9rem; }
th { background: #f3f4f6; }
.badge { padding: .15rem .6rem; border-radius: 999px; font-size: .8rem; background: #e5e7eb; color: #374151; }
.badge.urgent { background: #fee2e2; color: #991b1b; }
.badge.pending { background: #e0e7ff; color: #3730a3; }
.badge.in_progress { background: #fef3c7; color: #92400e; }
.badge.completed { background: #dcfce7; color: #166534; }
.badge.cancelled { background: #f3f4f6; color: #6b7280; }
.actions { display: flex; gap: .35rem; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.actions button { padding: .25rem .55rem; border: 0; border-radius: 6px; background: #0d6efd; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; font-size: .78rem; }
.create { margin-top: 2rem; display: grid; gap: .6rem; max-width: 520px; background: #fff; padding: 1.5rem; border-radius: 8px; }
.create h2 { margin: 0 0 .3rem; font-size: 1.1rem; }
label { display: grid; gap: .25rem; font-size: .9rem; }
input, select, textarea { padding: .5rem .7rem; border: 1px solid #d1d5db; border-radius: 8px; font-size: .95rem; font-family: inherit; }
.create button { padding: .6rem; border: 0; border-radius: 8px; background: #0d6efd; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; }
.create button.secondary { background: #6b7280; }
</style>
`
  );

// --- Tableau de bord : lien Laboratoire ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'DashboardView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();
const router = useRouter();
const currentFacilityName = ref('');

async function logout() {
  await auth.logout();
  await router.push('/login');
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const facilities = await auth.loadFacilities();
    const current = facilities.find(
      (facility) => facility.id === auth.currentFacilityId
    );
    currentFacilityName.value = current ? current.name : '';
  } catch {
    currentFacilityName.value = '';
  }
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="dash">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/laboratoire">Laboratoire</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
        <span>{{ auth.user?.name }}</span>
        <button @click="logout">Déconnexion</button>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Bienvenue, {{ auth.user?.name }}</h1>
      <p>
        Rôles :
        <span v-for="role in auth.roles" :key="role" class="badge">{{ role }}</span>
      </p>
      <p v-if="currentFacilityName">
        Établissement actif : <strong>{{ currentFacilityName }}</strong>
      </p>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
.dash { min-height: 100vh; }
header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 1rem 2rem;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgb(0 0 0 / 6%);
}
header strong { color: #0d6efd; letter-spacing: .05em; }
nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1rem; }
nav a { color: #1f2937; text-decoration: none; }
nav a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
nav button { padding: .4rem .9rem; border: 0; border-radius: 8px; background: #ef4444; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; }
.content { padding: 2rem; }
.badge { display: inline-block; margin-right: .4rem; padding: .15rem .6rem; border-radius: 999px; background: #e0e7ff; color: #3730a3; font-size: .85rem; }
</style>
`
  );

ok('Fichiers frontend écrits');
}


===== scripts/m06/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M07 (2/4) : backend — pharmacie, stock & dispensations
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { API, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Migration pharmacy ---
  const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
  const migrationExists = fs
    .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .some((f) => f.includes('create_pharmacy_tables'));

if (migrationExists) {
    ok('Migration déjà présente');
  } else {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
      path.join(migrationsDir, timestamp + '_create_pharmacy_tables.php'),
      String.raw`<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('drugs', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->string('name', 200)->unique();
            $table->string('form', 100);
            $table->string('dosage', 100)->nullable();
            $table->string('unit', 50);
            $table->boolean('is_active')->default(true);
            $table->timestamps();
        });

Schema::create('drug_lots', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('drug_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('lot_number', 100)->nullable();
            $table->unsignedInteger('quantity')->default(0);
            $table->date('expires_at')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->index(['facility_id', 'drug_id']);
        });

Schema::create('dispensations', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('patient_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('consultation_id')->nullable()->constrained()->nullOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('drug_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('dispensed_by')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->unsignedInteger('quantity');
            $table->string('notes', 500)->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->index(['facility_id', 'patient_id']);
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('dispensations');
        Schema::dropIfExists('drug_lots');
        Schema::dropIfExists('drugs');
    }
};
`
    );
    ok('Migration pharmacie créée');
  }

// --- Contrôleur PharmacyController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'PharmacyController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Dispensation;
use App\Models\Drug;
use App\Models\DrugLot;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class PharmacyController extends Controller
{
    // ---------- Catalogue ----------

public function drugs(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$this->currentFacility($request);

$drugs = Drug::orderBy('name')->get();

return response()->json([
            'drugs' => $drugs->map(fn ($d) => [
                'id' => $d->id,
                'name' => $d->name,
                'form' => $d->form,
                'dosage' => $d->dosage,
                'unit' => $d->unit,
                'is_active' => $d->is_active,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

public function storeDrug(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200', 'unique:drugs,name'],
            'form' => ['required', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'dosage' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'unit' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
        ], [
            'name.required' => 'Le nom du médicament est obligatoire.',
            'name.unique' => 'Ce médicament existe déjà au catalogue.',
            'unit.required' => 'L\'unité de dispensation est obligatoire.',
        ]);

$drug = Drug::create([
            'name' => $data['name'],
            'form' => $data['form'],
            'dosage' => $data['dosage'] ?? null,
            'unit' => $data['unit'],
            'is_active' => true,
        ]);

return response()->json(['drug' => [
            'id' => $drug->id,
            'name' => $drug->name,
            'form' => $drug->form,
            'dosage' => $drug->dosage,
            'unit' => $drug->unit,
            'is_active' => $drug->is_active,
        ]], 201);
    }

// ---------- Stock ----------

public function stock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$expiringDays = $request->query('expiring_days');

$query = DrugLot::inFacility($facility)
            ->with('drug:id,name,form,dosage,unit')
            ->orderBy('expires_at');

if ($expiringDays !== null) {
            $limit = now()->addDays((int) $expiringDays)->endOfDay();

$query->whereNotNull('expires_at')
                ->where('expires_at', '<=', $limit)
                ->where('expires_at', '>', now())
                ->where('quantity', '>', 0);
        }

return response()->json([
            'lots' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($lot) => $this->lotPayload($lot)),
        ]);
    }

public function storeStock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'drug_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:drugs,id'],
            'lot_number' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'quantity' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:1000000'],
            'expires_at' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after:today'],
        ], [
            'quantity.required' => 'La quantité du lot est obligatoire.',
            'expires_at.after' => 'La date de péremption doit être future.',
            'drug_id.exists' => 'Médicament inconnu.',
        ]);

$drug = Drug::where('is_active', true)->find($data['drug_id']);

if (! $drug) {
            abort(422, 'Ce médicament est inconnu ou inactif.');
        }

$lot = DrugLot::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'drug_id' => $drug->id,
            'lot_number' => $data['lot_number'] ?? null,
            'quantity' => $data['quantity'],
            'expires_at' => $data['expires_at'] ?? null,
        ]);

return response()->json(
            ['lot' => $this->lotPayload($lot->load('drug:id,name,form,dosage,unit'))],
            201
        );
    }

// ---------- Dispensations ----------

public function dispensations(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$query = Dispensation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'drug:id,name,unit', 'dispenser:id,name'])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

$patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

return response()->json([
            'dispensations' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($d) => $this->dispensationPayload($d)),
        ]);
    }

public function dispense(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'consultation_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:consultations,id'],
            'drug_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:drugs,id'],
            'quantity' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:10000'],
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'quantity.required' => 'La quantité dispensée est obligatoire.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'drug_id.exists' => 'Médicament inconnu.',
        ]);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

$consultation = null;

if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

$drug = Drug::where('is_active', true)->find($data['drug_id']);

if (! $drug) {
            abort(422, 'Ce médicament est inconnu ou inactif.');
        }

$quantity = $data['quantity'];

[$dispensation, $remaining] = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $patient, $consultation, $drug, $quantity, $request) {
            $lots = DrugLot::inFacility($facility)
                ->where('drug_id', $drug->id)
                ->where('quantity', '>', 0)
                ->where(function ($q) {
                    $q->whereNull('expires_at')->orWhere('expires_at', '>', now());
                })
                ->orderBy('expires_at')
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->get();

$available = $lots->sum('quantity');

if ($available < $quantity) {
                abort(422, 'Stock insuffisant : ' . $available . ' ' . $drug->unit . '(s) disponible(s).');
            }

$left = $quantity;

foreach ($lots as $lot) {
                if ($left <= 0) {
                    break;
                }

$take = min($lot->quantity, $left);
                $lot->quantity -= $take;
                $lot->save();
                $left -= $take;
            }

$record = Dispensation::create([
                'facility_id' => $facility->id,
                'patient_id' => $patient->id,
                'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
                'drug_id' => $drug->id,
                'dispensed_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'quantity' => $quantity,
                'notes' => request('notes'),
            ]);

return [$record, $available - $quantity];
        });

return response()->json([
            'dispensation' => $this->dispensationPayload($dispensation->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'drug:id,name,unit',
                'dispenser:id,name'
            )),
            'remaining_stock' => $remaining,
        ], 201);
    }

public function showDispensation(Request $request, int $dispensationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$dispensation = Dispensation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'drug:id,name,unit', 'dispenser:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($dispensationId);

return response()->json([
            'dispensation' => $this->dispensationPayload($dispensation),
        ]);
    }

// ---------- Helpers ----------

private function lotPayload(DrugLot $lot): array
    {
        $expiringSoon = $lot->expires_at !== null
            && $lot->expires_at->isFuture()
            && $lot->expires_at->lessThanOrEqualTo(now()->addDays(30))
            && $lot->quantity > 0;

return [
            'id' => $lot->id,
            'drug' => [
                'id' => $lot->drug->id,
                'name' => $lot->drug->name,
                'form' => $lot->drug->form,
                'dosage' => $lot->drug->dosage,
                'unit' => $lot->drug->unit,
            ],
            'lot_number' => $lot->lot_number,
            'quantity' => $lot->quantity,
            'expires_at' => $lot->expires_at?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'expired' => $lot->expires_at !== null && $lot->expires_at->isPast(),
            'expiring_soon' => $expiringSoon,
        ];
    }

private function dispensationPayload(Dispensation $d): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $d->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $d->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $d->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $d->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $d->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'drug' => [
                'id' => $d->drug->id,
                'name' => $d->drug->name,
                'unit' => $d->drug->unit,
            ],
            'quantity' => $d->quantity,
            'dispensed_by' => $d->dispenser?->name,
            'consultation_id' => $d->consultation_id,
            'notes' => $d->notes,
            'created_at' => $d->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('pharmacy.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas accès à la pharmacie.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('pharmacy.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de gérer la pharmacie.');
        }
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Seeder additif : permissions pharmacie ---

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class PharmacyPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('pharmacy.view');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('pharmacy.manage');

$map = [
            'admin' => [$view, $manage],
            'medecin' => [$view],
            'infirmier' => [$view],
            'pharmacien' => [$view, $manage],
        ];

foreach ($map as $roleName => $permissions) {
            $role = Role::findOrCreate($roleName);
            $role->givePermissionTo($permissions);
        }

app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}
`
  );

  writeFile(

===== scripts/m07/e2e.mjs ===== (364 lignes, 12 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M07 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — pharmacie & dispensation
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const DRUG_NAME = 'Paracétamol';
const PORT = 8017;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M07/5', 'Test de bout en bout — pharmacie & dispensation');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + ').');
    }
  }

function isoDate(offsetDays) {
    return new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (admin → HREF, secrétaire → CSM)');

const hrefPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const hrefPatient = hrefPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-HREF-0001'
    );

if (!hrefPatient) fail('Patient P-HREF-0001 introuvable dans HREF.');

const csmPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csmPatient = csmPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-CSM-0001'
    );

if (!csmPatient) fail('Patient P-CSM-0001 introuvable dans CSM.');

// Test 1 : médicament au catalogue (créé si absent — reruns idempotents)
    const drugsResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/drugs', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const drugsData = await drugsResponse.json();
    let drug = drugsData.drugs.find((d) => d.name === DRUG_NAME);

if (!drug) {
      const createDrug = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/drugs', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: DRUG_NAME,
          form: 'comprimé',
          dosage: '500 mg',
          unit: 'comprimé',
        }),
      });

if (createDrug.status !== 201) {
        fail('Catalogue : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createDrug.status + '.');
      }

drug = (await createDrug.json()).drug;
    }
    ok('Médicament au catalogue : ' + drug.name + ' (id=' + drug.id + ')');

// Test 2 : entrée de stock (lot de 100, péremption lointaine)
    const beforeStock = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const beforeTotal = beforeStock.lots
      .filter((lot) => lot.drug.id === drug.id && !lot.expired)
      .reduce((sum, lot) => sum + lot.quantity, 0);

const addLot = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        drug_id: drug.id,
        lot_number: 'LOT-' + Date.now(),
        quantity: 100,
        expires_at: isoDate(300),
      }),
    });

if (addLot.status !== 201) {
      fail('Entrée de stock : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + addLot.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Lot de 100 ' + drug.unit + 's ajouté au stock HREF (201)');

// Test 3 : dispensation partielle → décrément vérifié
    const dispensePartial = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        drug_id: drug.id,
        quantity: 20,
        notes: '1 comprimé 3 fois par jour',
      }),
    });

if (dispensePartial.status !== 201) {
      fail('Dispensation : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + dispensePartial.status + '.');
    }

const partial = await dispensePartial.json();

if (partial.dispensation.patient.local_code !== 'P-HREF-0001') {
      fail('Patient incorrect sur la dispensation.');
    }

if (partial.dispensation.dispensed_by !== 'Admin') {
      if (!partial.dispensation.dispensed_by) fail('Dispensateur absent du rendu.');
    }

const afterStock = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const afterTotal = afterStock.lots
      .filter((lot) => lot.drug.id === drug.id && !lot.expired)
      .reduce((sum, lot) => sum + lot.quantity, 0);

if (afterTotal !== beforeTotal + 100 - 20) {
      fail('Décrément de stock incorrect : attendu ' + (beforeTotal + 80) + ', trouvé ' + afterTotal + '.');
    }

if (partial.remaining_stock !== afterTotal) {
      fail('Stock restant incohérent entre la réponse et la liste.');
    }
    ok('Dispensation de 20 enregistrée, stock décrémenté (' + afterTotal + ' restants)');

// Test 4 : dispensation du reste → stock épuisé
    const dispenseRest = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        drug_id: drug.id,
        quantity: afterTotal,
      }),
    });

if (dispenseRest.status !== 201) {
      fail('Dispensation complète : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + dispenseRest.status + '.');
    }

const rest = await dispenseRest.json();

if (rest.remaining_stock !== 0) {
      fail('Stock restant attendu : 0, trouvé ' + rest.remaining_stock + '.');
    }
    ok('Stock épuisé proprement (remaining 0)');

// Test 5 : sur-dispensation → 422
    const overDispense = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        drug_id: drug.id,
        quantity: 1,
      }),
    });

if (overDispense.status !== 422) {
      fail('Sur-dispensation : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + overDispense.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Dispensation au-delà du stock rejetée (422)');

// Test 6 : lot proche de péremption → signalé
    const expiringLot = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        drug_id: drug.id,
        lot_number: 'EXP-' + Date.now(),
        quantity: 10,
        expires_at: isoDate(10),
      }),
    });

if (expiringLot.status !== 201) {
      fail('Lot proche péremption : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + expiringLot.status + '.');
    }

const expiringList = await (await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock?expiring_days=30',
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    )).json();
    const flagged = expiringList.lots.find((lot) => lot.expiring_soon);

if (!flagged) fail('Aucun lot signalé en péremption proche.');
    ok('Lot proche de péremption signalé (expiring_soon)');

// Test 7 : patient d'un autre établissement → 422
    const crossPatient = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        drug_id: drug.id,
        quantity: 1,
      }),
    });

if (crossPatient.status !== 422) {
      fail('Patient cross-établissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + crossPatient.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Patient d\'un autre établissement rejeté (422)');

// Test 8 : historique filtré par patient + détail
    const historyResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations?patient_id=' + hrefPatient.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    );
    const historyData = await historyResponse.json();
    const wrongPatient = historyData.dispensations.find(
      (d) => d.patient.id !== hrefPatient.id
    );

if (wrongPatient) fail('Le filtre patient renvoie d\'autres patients.');
    ok('Historique des dispensations filtré par patient opérationnel');

const detailResponse = await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations/' + partial.dispensation.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    );

if (detailResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Détail dispensation : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + detailResponse.status + '.');
    }

const wrongId = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations/999999', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (wrongId.status !== 404) {
      fail('Dispensation inexistante : HTTP 404 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + wrongId.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Détail dispensation accessible, id hors périmètre introuvable (200/404)');

// Test 9 : secrétaire sans accès pharmacie
    const secStock = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/stock', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (secStock.status !== 403) {
      fail('Stock (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secStock.status + '.');
    }

const secDispense = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        drug_id: drug.id,
        quantity: 1,
      }),
    });

if (secDispense.status !== 403) {
      fail('Dispensation (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secDispense.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Secrétaire sans accès pharmacie : stock et dispensation rejetés (403)');

// Test 10 : accès anonyme
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/pharmacy/drugs', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });

if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}


===== scripts/m07/frontend.mjs ===== (523 lignes, 16 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M07 (3/4) : webapp Vue — page Pharmacie
import path from 'node:path';
import { WEB, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installFrontend() {
  // --- Routeur ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
    String.raw`import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/consultations',
      component: () => import('../views/ConsultationsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/laboratoire',
      component: () => import('../views/LabView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/pharmacie',
      component: () => import('../views/PharmacyView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
  );

// --- Page Pharmacie ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'PharmacyView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const drugs = ref([]);
const lots = ref([]);
const dispensations = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const showOnlyExpiring = ref(false);

const drugId = ref('');
const drugName = ref('');
const drugForm = ref('comprimé');
const drugDosage = ref('');
const drugUnit = ref('');

const lotDrugId = ref('');
const lotNumber = ref('');
const lotQuantity = ref('');
const lotExpiresAt = ref('');

const dispensePatientId = ref('');
const dispenseDrugId = ref('');
const dispenseQuantity = ref('');
const dispenseNotes = ref('');

const lotsSorted = computed(() => {
  const sorted = [...lots.value].sort((a, b) => {
    const dateA = a.expires_at || '9999-12-31';
    const dateB = b.expires_at || '9999-12-31';
    return dateA.localeCompare(dateB);
  });
  return showOnlyExpiring.value
    ? sorted.filter((lot) => lot.expiring_soon)
    : sorted;
});

async function loadAll() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const drugsData = await api('/pharmacy/drugs', { token: auth.token });
    drugs.value = drugsData.drugs;

const stockData = await api('/pharmacy/stock', { token: auth.token });
    lots.value = stockData.lots;

const dispensationsData = await api('/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      token: auth.token,
    });
    dispensations.value = dispensationsData.dispensations;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createDrug() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/drugs', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        name: drugName.value.trim(),
        form: drugForm.value,
        dosage: drugDosage.value.trim() || null,
        unit: drugUnit.value.trim(),
      },
    });
    drugName.value = '';
    drugDosage.value = '';
    drugUnit.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function addLot() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/stock', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        drug_id: Number(lotDrugId.value),
        lot_number: lotNumber.value.trim() || null,
        quantity: Number(lotQuantity.value),
        expires_at: lotExpiresAt.value || null,
      },
    });
    lotDrugId.value = '';
    lotNumber.value = '';
    lotQuantity.value = '';
    lotExpiresAt.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function dispense() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(dispensePatientId.value),
        drug_id: Number(dispenseDrugId.value),
        quantity: Number(dispenseQuantity.value),
        notes: dispenseNotes.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    dispensePatientId.value = '';
    dispenseDrugId.value = '';
    dispenseQuantity.value = '';
    dispenseNotes.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadAll();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/laboratoire">Laboratoire</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Pharmacie</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<h2>Stock</h2>
      <label class="toggle">
        <input v-model="showOnlyExpiring" type="checkbox" />
        Afficher uniquement les lots proches de péremption
      </label>

<table v-if="lotsSorted.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Médicament</th>
            <th>Lot</th>
            <th>Quantité</th>
            <th>Péremption</th>
            <th>Alerte</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="lot in lotsSorted" :key="lot.id">
            <td>
              {{ lot.drug.name }}
              <small v-if="lot.drug.dosage"> — {{ lot.drug.dosage }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ lot.lot_number || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ lot.quantity }} {{ lot.drug.unit }}(s)</td>
            <td>{{ lot.expires_at || '—' }}</td>
            <td>
              <span v-if="lot.expired" class="badge expired">Périmé</span>
              <span v-else-if="lot.expiring_soon" class="badge expiring">Péremption proche</span>
              <span v-else-if="lot.quantity === 0" class="badge empty">Épuisé</span>
              <template v-else>—</template>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <p v-else>Aucun lot en stock pour ce filtre.</p>

<div class="grid">
        <form class="card" @submit.prevent="addLot">
          <h2>Entrée de stock (nouveau lot)</h2>

<label>
            Médicament
            <select v-model="lotDrugId" required>
              <option value="" disabled>— Choisir —</option>
              <option v-for="drug in drugs.filter((d) => d.is_active)" :key="drug.id" :value="drug.id">
                {{ drug.name }}
              </option>
            </select>
          </label>

<label>
            N° de lot
            <input v-model="lotNumber" placeholder="L-2026-04" />
          </label>

<label>
            Quantité
            <input v-model="lotQuantity" type="number" min="1" required />
          </label>

<label>
            Péremption
            <input v-model="lotExpiresAt" type="date" />
          </label>

<button type="submit">Ajouter le lot</button>
        </form>

<form class="card" @submit.prevent="dispense">
          <h2>Dispenser au patient</h2>

<label>
            Patient
            <select v-model="dispensePatientId" required>
              <option value="" disabled>— Choisir —</option>
              <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
                {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
              </option>
            </select>
          </label>

<label>
            Médicament
            <select v-model="dispenseDrugId" required>
              <option value="" disabled>— Choisir —</option>
              <option v-for="drug in drugs.filter((d) => d.is_active)" :key="drug.id" :value="drug.id">
                {{ drug.name }}
              </option>
            </select>
          </label>

<label>
            Quantité
            <input v-model="dispenseQuantity" type="number" min="1" required />
          </label>

<label>
            Notes
            <input v-model="dispenseNotes" placeholder="Posologie, instructions…" />
          </label>

<button type="submit">Dispenser</button>
        </form>

<form class="card" @submit.prevent="createDrug">
          <h2>Ajouter au catalogue</h2>

<label>
            Nom
            <input v-model="drugName" required placeholder="Paracétamol" />
          </label>

<label>
            Forme
            <select v-model="drugForm">
              <option value="comprimé">Comprimé</option>
              <option value="capsule">Capsule</option>
              <option value="sirop">Sirop</option>
              <option value="injection">Injection</option>
              <option value="pommade">Pommade</option>
              <option value="autre">Autre</option>
            </select>
          </label>

<label>
            Dosage
            <input v-model="drugDosage" placeholder="500 mg" />
          </label>

<label>
            Unité
            <input v-model="drugUnit" required placeholder="comprimé, ml…" />
          </label>

<button type="submit">Enregistrer</button>
        </form>
      </div>

<h2>Historique des dispensations</h2>
      <table v-if="dispensations.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Médicament</th>
            <th>Quantité</th>
            <th>Par</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="dispensation in dispensations" :key="dispensation.id">
            <td>{{ dispensation.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ dispensation.patient.last_name }} {{ dispensation.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ dispensation.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ dispensation.drug.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ dispensation.quantity }} {{ dispensation.drug.unit }}(s)</td>
            <td>{{ dispensation.dispensed_by }}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <p v-else>Aucune dispensation enregistrée.</p>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

// --- Tableau de bord : lien Pharmacie ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'DashboardView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();
const router = useRouter();
const currentFacilityName = ref('');

async function logout() {
  await auth.logout();
  await router.push('/login');
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const facilities = await auth.loadFacilities();
    const current = facilities.find(
      (facility) => facility.id === auth.currentFacilityId
    );
    currentFacilityName.value = current ? current.name : '';
  } catch {
    currentFacilityName.value = '';
  }
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="dash">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>

        <router-link to="/pharmacie">Pharmacie</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
        <span>{{ auth.user?.name }}</span>
        <button @click="logout">Déconnexion</button>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">

===== scripts/m07/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Migration billing ---

    .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .some((f) => f.includes('create_billing_tables'));

if (migrationExists) {
    ok('Migration déjà présente');
  } else {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
      path.join(migrationsDir, timestamp + '_create_billing_tables.php'),
      String.raw`<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('invoices', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('patient_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('consultation_id')->nullable()->constrained()->nullOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('created_by')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('number', 40);
            $table->string('status', 20)->default('draft');
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('total_amount')->default(0);
            $table->date('due_date')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();

$table->unique(['facility_id', 'number']);
            $table->index(['facility_id', 'status']);
        });

Schema::create('invoice_items', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('invoice_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('label', 300);
            $table->unsignedInteger('quantity')->default(1);
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('unit_price')->default(0);
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('amount')->default(0);
            $table->timestamps();
        });

Schema::create('payments', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('invoice_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('amount');
            $table->string('method', 30);
            $table->foreignId('received_by')->constrained('users')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->timestamp('paid_at');
            $table->timestamps();
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('payments');
        Schema::dropIfExists('invoice_items');
        Schema::dropIfExists('invoices');
    }
};
`
    );
    ok('Migration facturation créée');
  }

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

class Invoice extends Model
{
    public const STATUSES = ['draft', 'issued', 'paid', 'cancelled'];

public const PAYMENT_METHODS = ['cash', 'mobile_money', 'transfer'];

protected $fillable = [
        'facility_id',
        'patient_id',
        'consultation_id',
        'created_by',
        'number',
        'status',
        'total_amount',
        'due_date',
    ];

protected $casts = [
        'due_date' => 'date',
    ];

public function patient(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Patient::class);
    }

public function items(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(InvoiceItem::class);
    }

public function payments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Payment::class);
    }

public function scopeInFacility($query, Facility $facility)
    {
        return $query->where('facility_id', $facility->id);
    }
}
