// --- Contrôleur InvoiceController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'InvoiceController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Invoice;
use App\Models\InvoiceItem;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class InvoiceController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$query = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name'])
            ->withSum('payments as paid_amount', 'amount')
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

$patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

$status = $request->query('status');

if ($status) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }

return response()->json([
            'invoices' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($i) => $this->payload($i)),
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'consultation_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:consultations,id'],
            'due_date' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after:today'],
            'items' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'items.*.label' => ['required', 'string', 'max:300'],
            'items.*.quantity' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:10000'],
            'items.*.unit_price' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:0', 'max:100000000'],
        ], [
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'consultation_id.exists' => 'Consultation inconnue.',
            'items.required' => 'La facture doit contenir au moins une ligne.',
            'due_date.after' => 'L\'échéance doit être future.',
        ]);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

$consultation = null;

if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

$invoice = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $patient, $consultation, $data, $request) {
            $total = 0;

foreach ($data['items'] as $item) {
                $total += $item['quantity'] * $item['unit_price'];
            }

$record = Invoice::create([
                'facility_id' => $facility->id,
                'patient_id' => $patient->id,
                'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
                'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'number' => 'TMP-' . uniqid(),
                'status' => 'draft',
                'total_amount' => $total,
                'due_date' => $data['due_date'] ?? null,
            ]);

$record->number = 'FAC-' . str_pad((string) $record->id, 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
            $record->save();

foreach ($data['items'] as $item) {
                InvoiceItem::create([
                    'invoice_id' => $record->id,
                    'label' => $item['label'],
                    'quantity' => $item['quantity'],
                    'unit_price' => $item['unit_price'],
                    'amount' => $item['quantity'] * $item['unit_price'],
                ]);
            }

return $record;
        });

return response()->json(
            ['invoice' => $this->payload($invoice->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

public function show(Request $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$invoice = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'items', 'payments'])
            ->withSum('payments as paid_amount', 'amount')
            ->findOrFail($invoiceId);

return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice),
        ]);
    }

public function update(Request $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$invoice = Invoice::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($invoiceId);

$data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['required', 'string', 'in:issued,cancelled'],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Transition invalide (émettre ou annuler uniquement).',
        ]);

if ($invoice->status === 'paid') {
            abort(422, 'Cette facture est payée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

if ($invoice->status === 'cancelled') {
            abort(422, 'Cette facture est annulée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

if ($data['status'] === 'cancelled') {
            $paid = (int) $invoice->payments()->sum('amount');

if ($paid > 0) {
                abort(422, 'Impossible d\'annuler une facture ayant encaissé ' . $paid . ' XOF.');
            }
        }

if ($data['status'] === 'issued') {
            if ($invoice->status !== 'draft') {
                abort(422, 'Seule une facture brouillon peut être émise.');
            }
        }

$invoice->status = $data['status'];
        $invoice->save();

return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            )),
        ]);
    }

public function pay(Request $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'amount' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:100000000'],
            'method' => ['required', 'string', 'in:' . implode(',', Invoice::PAYMENT_METHODS)],
        ], [
            'amount.required' => 'Le montant encaissé est obligatoire.',
            'method.in' => 'Mode de paiement invalide.',
        ]);

$invoice = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $invoiceId, $data, $request) {
            $record = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
                ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'items', 'payments'])
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->findOrFail($invoiceId);

if ($record->status !== 'issued') {
                abort(422, 'Seule une facture émise peut être encaissée (statut actuel : ' . $record->status . ').');
            }

$paid = (int) $record->payments()->sum('amount');
            $remaining = $record->total_amount - $paid;

if ($data['amount'] > $remaining) {
                abort(422, 'Montant supérieur au reste à payer (' . $remaining . ' XOF).');
            }

$record->payments()->create([
                'amount' => $data['amount'],
                'method' => $data['method'],
                'received_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'paid_at' => now(),
            ]);

$paid += $data['amount'];

if ($paid >= $record->total_amount) {
                $record->status = 'paid';
                $record->save();
            }

return $record->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            );
        });

return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice),
        ]);
    }

private function payload(Invoice $i): array
    {
        $paidAmount = (int) ($i->paid_amount ?? $i->payments->sum('amount'));

return [
            'id' => $i->id,
            'number' => $i->number,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $i->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $i->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $i->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $i->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'consultation_id' => $i->consultation_id,
            'status' => $i->status,
            'total_amount' => (int) $i->total_amount,
            'paid_amount' => $paidAmount,
            'remaining_amount' => (int) $i->total_amount - $paidAmount,
            'due_date' => $i->due_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'items' => $i->items->map(fn ($item) => [
                'label' => $item->label,
                'quantity' => $item->quantity,
                'unit_price' => (int) $item->unit_price,
                'amount' => (int) $item->amount,
            ]),
            'payments' => $i->payments->map(fn ($p) => [
                'amount' => (int) $p->amount,
                'method' => $p->method,
                'paid_at' => $p->paid_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            ]),
            'created_at' => $i->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('billing.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas accès à la facturation.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('billing.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas le droit de gérer la facturation.');
        }
    }
}
`
  );

// --- Seeder additif : permissions facturation ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'BillingPermissionsSeeder.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class BillingPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $view = Permission::findOrCreate('billing.view');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('billing.manage');

$map = [
            'admin' => [$view, $manage],
            'comptable' => [$view, $manage],
            'medecin' => [$view],
        ];

foreach ($map as $roleName => $permissions) {
            $role = Role::findOrCreate($roleName);
            $role->givePermissionTo($permissions);
        }

app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}
`
  );

===== scripts/m08/e2e.mjs ===== (404 lignes, 14 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M08 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — facturation & encaissements
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8018;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function detail(response) {
  try {
    const body = await response.text();
    return body ? ' — réponse API : ' + body.slice(0, 400) : '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M08/5', 'Test de bout en bout — facturation');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + await detail(response) + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + await detail(response) + ').');
    }
  }

function isoDate(offsetDays) {
    return new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (admin → HREF, secrétaire → CSM)');

const hrefPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const hrefPatient = hrefPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-HREF-0001'
    );

if (!hrefPatient) fail('Patient P-HREF-0001 introuvable dans HREF.');

const csmPatients = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csmPatient = csmPatients.patients.find(
      (p) => p.local_code === 'P-CSM-0001'
    );

if (!csmPatient) fail('Patient P-CSM-0001 introuvable dans CSM.');

// Test 1 : création de facture — total et lignes vérifiés
    const createResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        due_date: isoDate(30),
        items: [
          { label: 'Consultation générale', quantity: 1, unit_price: 10000 },
          { label: 'Paracétamol 500 mg', quantity: 2, unit_price: 500 },
        ],
      }),
    });

if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Création de facture : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createResponse.status + await detail(createResponse) + '.');
    }

const created = (await createResponse.json()).invoice;

if (created.status !== 'draft') {
      fail('Statut initial invalide : ' + created.status + '.');
    }

if (!created.number.startsWith('FAC-')) {
      fail('Numérotation invalide : ' + created.number + '.');
    }

if (created.total_amount !== 11000) {
      fail('Total incorrect : attendu 11000, reçu ' + created.total_amount + '.');
    }

if (created.items.length !== 2) {
      fail('Lignes manquantes : ' + created.items.length + ' reçue(s).');
    }

if (created.remaining_amount !== 11000) {
      fail('Reste à payer initial incorrect : ' + created.remaining_amount + '.');
    }
    ok('Facture ' + created.number + ' créée (draft, 2 lignes, total 11000 XOF)');

// Test 2 : encaissement refusé sur brouillon → 422
    const payDraft = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 1000, method: 'cash' }),
    });

if (payDraft.status !== 422) {
      fail('Paiement sur brouillon : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + payDraft.status + await detail(payDraft) + '.');
    }
    ok('Encaissement sur brouillon rejeté (422)');

// Test 3 : émission puis paiement partiel
    const issueResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'issued' }),
    });

if (issueResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Émission : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + issueResponse.status + await detail(issueResponse) + '.');
    }

const issued = (await issueResponse.json()).invoice;

if (issued.status !== 'issued') {
      fail('Statut après émission invalide : ' + issued.status + '.');
    }
    ok('Facture émise (issued)');

const partialResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 6000, method: 'mobile_money' }),
    });

if (partialResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Paiement partiel : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + partialResponse.status + await detail(partialResponse) + '.');
    }

const partial = (await partialResponse.json()).invoice;

if (partial.status !== 'issued') {
      fail('Statut après paiement partiel invalide : ' + partial.status + '.');
    }

if (partial.paid_amount !== 6000) {
      fail('Montant payé incorrect : attendu 6000, reçu ' + partial.paid_amount + '.');
    }

if (partial.remaining_amount !== 5000) {
      fail('Reste à payer incorrect : attendu 5000, reçu ' + partial.remaining_amount + '.');
    }
    ok('Paiement partiel de 6000 XOF (reste 5000, toujours issued)');

// Test 4 : sur-encaissement → 422
    const overPay = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 6000, method: 'cash' }),
    });

if (overPay.status !== 422) {
      fail('Sur-encaissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + overPay.status + await detail(overPay) + '.');
    }
    ok('Encaissement supérieur au reste rejeté (422)');

// Test 5 : solde → paid
    const finalPay = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 5000, method: 'cash' }),
    });

if (finalPay.status !== 200) {
      fail('Solde : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + finalPay.status + await detail(finalPay) + '.');
    }

const paid = (await finalPay.json()).invoice;

if (paid.status !== 'paid') {
      fail('Statut invalide après solde : ' + paid.status + '.');
    }

if (paid.remaining_amount !== 0) {
      fail('Reste à payer non nul : ' + paid.remaining_amount + '.');
    }
    ok('Facture soldée : statut paid, 2 paiements enregistrés');

// Test 6 : facture payée verrouillée → 422 sur toute transition
    const touchPaid = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + created.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'cancelled' }),
    });

if (touchPaid.status !== 422) {
      fail('Facture payée : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + touchPaid.status + await detail(touchPaid) + '.');
    }
    ok('Facture payée non modifiable (422)');

// Test 7 : annulation propre + paiement sur annulée refusé
    const secondCreate = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        items: [{ label: 'Glycémie à jeun', quantity: 1, unit_price: 3000 }],
      }),
    });

if (secondCreate.status !== 201) {
      fail('Seconde facture : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secondCreate.status + await detail(secondCreate) + '.');
    }

const second = (await secondCreate.json()).invoice;

const cancelResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + second.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'cancelled' }),
    });

if (cancelResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Annulation : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + cancelResponse.status + await detail(cancelResponse) + '.');
    }

const cancelled = (await cancelResponse.json()).invoice;

if (cancelled.status !== 'cancelled') {
      fail('Statut après annulation invalide : ' + cancelled.status + '.');
    }

const payCancelled = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + second.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 1000, method: 'cash' }),
    });

if (payCancelled.status !== 422) {
      fail('Paiement sur facture annulée : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + payCancelled.status + await detail(payCancelled) + '.');
    }
    ok('Annulation propre (facture vierge) et paiement sur annulée refusé (422)');

// Test 8 : patient cross-établissement → 422 + filtres + 404
    const crossPatient = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        items: [{ label: 'Tentative cross-établissement', quantity: 1, unit_price: 100 }],
      }),
    });

if (crossPatient.status !== 422) {
      fail('Patient cross-établissement : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + crossPatient.status + await detail(crossPatient) + '.');
    }
    ok('Patient d\'un autre établissement rejeté (422)');

const listResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });
    const listData = await listResponse.json();
    const found = listData.invoices.find((i) => i.id === created.id);

if (!found) fail('La facture créée ne figure pas dans la liste HREF.');

const statusFilter = await (await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/invoices?status=paid',
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    )).json();
    const wrongStatus = statusFilter.invoices.find((i) => i.status !== 'paid');

if (wrongStatus) fail('Le filtre statut renvoie d\'autres statuts.');

const patientFilter = await (await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/invoices?patient_id=' + hrefPatient.id,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    )).json();
    const wrongPatient = patientFilter.invoices.find(
      (i) => i.patient.id !== hrefPatient.id
    );

if (wrongPatient) fail('Le filtre patient renvoie d\'autres patients.');
    ok('Liste + filtres par statut et par patient opérationnels');

const wrongId = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/999999', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (wrongId.status !== 404) {
      fail('Facture inexistante : HTTP 404 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + wrongId.status + await detail(wrongId) + '.');
    }
    ok('Facture hors périmètre introuvable (404)');

// Test 9 : secrétaire sans accès facturation → 403
    const secList = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });

if (secList.status !== 403) {
      fail('Liste (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secList.status + await detail(secList) + '.');
    }

const secCreate = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: csmPatient.id,
        items: [{ label: 'Tentative secrétaire', quantity: 1, unit_price: 100 }],
      }),
    });

if (secCreate.status !== 403) {
      fail('Création (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secCreate.status + await detail(secCreate) + '.');
    }
    ok('Secrétaire sans accès facturation : liste et création rejetées (403)');

// Test 10 : accès anonyme
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });

if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + await detail(anonResponse) + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}


===== scripts/m08/frontend.mjs ===== (520 lignes, 16 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M08 (3/4) : webapp Vue — page Facturation
import path from 'node:path';
import { WEB, ok, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

export function installFrontend() {
  // --- Routeur ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
    String.raw`import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/consultations',
      component: () => import('../views/ConsultationsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/laboratoire',
      component: () => import('../views/LabView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/pharmacie',
      component: () => import('../views/PharmacyView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facturation',
      component: () => import('../views/BillingView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
  );

// --- Page Facturation ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'BillingView.vue'),
    String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const invoices = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const filterPatientId = ref('');
const filterStatus = ref('');

const patientId = ref('');
const dueDate = ref('');
const lines = ref([{ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 }]);

const payTarget = ref(null);
const payAmount = ref('');
const payMethod = ref('cash');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  draft: 'Brouillon',
  issued: 'Émise',
  paid: 'Payée',
  cancelled: 'Annulée',
};

const METHOD_LABELS = {
  cash: 'Espèces',
  mobile_money: 'Mobile Money',
  transfer: 'Virement',
};

function addLine() {
  lines.value.push({ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 });
}

function removeLine(index) {
  if (lines.value.length > 1) {
    lines.value.splice(index, 1);
  }
}

function formatXof(amount) {
  return Number(amount).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' XOF';
}

async function loadInvoices() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

if (filterPatientId.value) params.set('patient_id', filterPatientId.value);
    if (filterStatus.value) params.set('status', filterStatus.value);

const suffix = params.toString() ? '?' + params.toString() : '';
    const data = await api('/invoices' + suffix, { token: auth.token });
    invoices.value = data.invoices;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createInvoice() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        due_date: dueDate.value || null,
        items: lines.value
          .filter((line) => line.label.trim() && line.quantity > 0)
          .map((line) => ({
            label: line.label.trim(),
            quantity: Number(line.quantity),
            unit_price: Number(line.unit_price),
          })),
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    dueDate.value = '';
    lines.value = [{ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 }];
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(invoiceId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices/' + invoiceId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

function openPayForm(invoice) {
  payTarget.value = invoice;
  payAmount.value = invoice.remaining_amount;
  payMethod.value = 'cash';
}

async function submitPayment() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices/' + payTarget.value.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        amount: Number(payAmount.value),
        method: payMethod.value,
      },
    });
    payTarget.value = null;
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadInvoices();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/laboratoire">Laboratoire</router-link>
        <router-link to="/pharmacie">Pharmacie</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Facturation</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadInvoices">
        <label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="filterPatientId">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Statut
          <select v-model="filterStatus">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option value="draft">Brouillon</option>
            <option value="issued">Émise</option>
            <option value="paid">Payée</option>
            <option value="cancelled">Annulée</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="invoices.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>N°</th>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Total</th>
            <th>Payé</th>
            <th>Reste</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="invoice in invoices" :key="invoice.id">
            <td>{{ invoice.number }}</td>
            <td>{{ invoice.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ invoice.patient.last_name }} {{ invoice.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ invoice.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.total_amount) }}</td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.paid_amount) }}</td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.remaining_amount) }}</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="invoice.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[invoice.status] || invoice.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'draft'"
                @click="setStatus(invoice.id, 'issued')"
              >
                Émettre
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'issued'"
                @click="openPayForm(invoice)"
              >
                Encaisser
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'draft' || invoice.status === 'issued'"
                @click="setStatus(invoice.id, 'cancelled')"
              >
                Annuler
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else>Aucune facture pour ce filtre.</p>

<form v-if="payTarget" class="card" @submit.prevent="submitPayment">
        <h2>Encaissement — {{ payTarget.number }} (reste {{ formatXof(payTarget.remaining_amount) }})</h2>

<label>
          Montant (XOF)
          <input v-model="payAmount" type="number" min="1" required />
        </label>

<label>
          Mode
          <select v-model="payMethod">
            <option value="cash">Espèces</option>
            <option value="mobile_money">Mobile Money</option>
            <option value="transfer">Virement</option>
          </select>
        </label>

<button type="submit">Enregistrer le paiement</button>
        <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="payTarget = null">
          Annuler
        </button>
      </form>

<form class="card" @submit.prevent="createInvoice">
        <h2>Nouvelle facture</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Échéance
          <input v-model="dueDate" type="date" />
        </label>

<fieldset class="lines">
          <legend>Lignes de facture</legend>

<div v-for="(line, index) in lines" :key="index" class="line">
            <label class="grow">
              Libellé
              <input v-model="line.label" placeholder="Consultation générale" />
            </label>
            <label>
              Qté
              <input v-model="line.quantity" type="number" min="1" />
            </label>
            <label>
              Prix unitaire
              <input v-model="line.unit_price" type="number" min="0" />
            </label>
            <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="removeLine(index)">×</button>
          </div>

<button type="button" class="secondary" @click="addLine">Ajouter une ligne</button>
        </fieldset>

<button type="submit">Créer la facture</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>

===== scripts/m08/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}


===== scripts/m09/backend.mjs ===== (450 lignes, 17 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M09 (2/4) : backend — rapports & statistiques
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { API, ok, fail, writeFile } from './helpers.mjs';

function createdTables(content) {
  const tables = [];
  const marker = "Schema::create('";
  let cursor = 0;

while (cursor < content.length) {
    const start = content.indexOf(marker, cursor);
    if (start === -1) {
      break;
    }
    const nameStart = start + marker.length;
    const nameEnd = content.indexOf("'", nameStart);
    if (nameEnd === -1) {
      break;
    }
    tables.push(content.slice(nameStart, nameEnd));
    cursor = nameEnd + 1;
  }

return tables;
}

function tableBody(content, tableName) {
  const marker = "Schema::create('" + tableName + "'";
  const start = content.indexOf(marker);
  if (start === -1) {
    return '';
  }
  const rest = content.slice(start);
  const next = rest.indexOf('Schema::create', marker.length);
  if (next === -1) {
    return rest;
  }
  return rest.slice(0, next);
}

export function installBackend() {
  // --- Résolution dynamique des tables M04/M06/M07 ---
  const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
  const migrationFiles = fs.readdirSync(migrationsDir);

function readMigration(needle) {
    const name = migrationFiles.find((f) => f.includes(needle));
    if (!name) {
      return '';
    }
    return fs.readFileSync(path.join(migrationsDir, name), 'utf8');
  }

const pharmacySql = readMigration('pharmacy');
  const pharmacyTables = createdTables(pharmacySql);

const drugTable = pharmacyTables.find((t) => t === 'drugs')
    || pharmacyTables.find((t) => t.includes('drug'));
  const stockTable = pharmacyTables.find((t) => t.includes('stock'))
    || pharmacyTables.find((t) => t.includes('lot'));
  const dispensationTable = pharmacyTables.find((t) => t.includes('dispens'));

if (!drugTable) {
    fail('Table médicaments introuvable — le module M07 doit être installé.');
  }
  if (!stockTable) {
    fail('Table de lots de stock introuvable — le module M07 doit être installé.');
  }
  if (!dispensationTable) {
    fail('Table dispensations introuvable — le module M07 doit être installé.');
  }

const labTable = createdTables(readMigration('lab'))
    .find((t) => t.includes('lab'));

if (!labTable) {
    fail('Table laboratoire introuvable — le module M06 doit être installé.');
  }

const appointmentBody = tableBody(readMigration('appointment'), 'appointments');
  const appointmentDateCol = ['scheduled_at', 'starts_at', 'date_time', 'appointment_date']
    .find((c) => appointmentBody.includes("'" + c + "'")) || 'scheduled_at';

const stockBody = tableBody(pharmacySql, stockTable);
  const stockQtyCol = ['quantity', 'remaining_quantity', 'current_quantity', 'units']
    .find((c) => stockBody.includes("'" + c + "'")) || 'quantity';
  const expiryCol = stockBody.includes('expires_at') ? 'expires_at' : 'expiry_date';

const drugsBody = tableBody(pharmacySql, drugTable);
  const drugNameCol = drugsBody.includes("'name'") ? 'name' : 'label';

ok('Tables résolues — labo: ' + labTable + ', stock: ' + stockTable
    + ', dispensations: ' + dispensationTable + ', date RDV: ' + appointmentDateCol);

// --- Contrôleur ReportController ---
  writeFile(
    path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'ReportController.php'),
    String.raw`<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Facility;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class ReportController extends Controller
{
    private const LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD = 20;

private const EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS = 60;

public function overview(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeReport($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);
        [$from, $to] = $this->period($request);

$fromAt = $from . ' 00:00:00';
        $toAt = $to . ' 23:59:59';

$patientsTotal = DB::table('patients')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->count();

$newPatients = DB::table('patients')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

$appointmentsByStatus = DB::table('appointments')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('${appointmentDateCol}', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('status')
            ->selectRaw('status, count(*) as total')
            ->get()
            ->mapWithKeys(fn ($row) => [$row->status => (int) $row->total]);

$consultationsTotal = DB::table('consultations')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

$labRequestsByStatus = DB::table('${labTable}')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('status')
            ->selectRaw('status, count(*) as total')
            ->get()
            ->mapWithKeys(fn ($row) => [$row->status => (int) $row->total]);

$dispensationsTotal = DB::table('${dispensationTable}')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

$invoices = DB::table('invoices')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->where('status', '!=', 'cancelled')
            ->get(['id', 'total_amount']);

$billedTotal = (int) $invoices->sum('total_amount');

$paidInPeriod = (int) DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->sum('payments.amount');

$issuedInvoices = DB::table('invoices')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->where('status', 'issued')
            ->get(['id', 'total_amount']);

$issuedBilled = (int) $issuedInvoices->sum('total_amount');

$issuedPaid = (int) DB::table('payments')
            ->whereIn('invoice_id', $issuedInvoices->pluck('id'))
            ->sum('amount');

$outstandingTotal = $issuedBilled - $issuedPaid;

$recoveryRate = null;

if ($billedTotal > 0) {
            $recoveryRate = round($paidInPeriod * 100 / $billedTotal, 1);
        }

return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'facility' => ['id' => $facility->id, 'code' => $facility->code],
                'period' => ['from' => $from, 'to' => $to],
                'patients' => [
                    'total' => $patientsTotal,
                    'new_in_period' => $newPatients,
                ],
                'appointments_by_status' => $appointmentsByStatus,
                'consultations_in_period' => $consultationsTotal,
                'lab_requests_by_status' => $labRequestsByStatus,
                'dispensations_in_period' => $dispensationsTotal,
                'billing' => [
                    'invoices_in_period' => $invoices->count(),
                    'billed_in_period' => $billedTotal,
                    'paid_in_period' => $paidInPeriod,
                    'outstanding_total' => $outstandingTotal,
                    'recovery_rate_percent' => $recoveryRate,
                ],
            ],
        ]);
    }

public function revenue(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeReport($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);
        [$from, $to] = $this->period($request);

$fromAt = $from . ' 00:00:00';
        $toAt = $to . ' 23:59:59';

$daily = DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupByRaw("to_char(payments.paid_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD')")
            ->orderByRaw("to_char(payments.paid_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD')")
            ->selectRaw("to_char(payments.paid_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') as day, sum(payments.amount) as total")
            ->get();

$byMethod = DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('payments.method')
            ->selectRaw('payments.method as method, sum(payments.amount) as total')
            ->get();

return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'period' => ['from' => $from, 'to' => $to],
                'paid_total' => (int) $daily->sum('total'),
                'daily' => $daily->map(fn ($row) => [
                    'day' => $row->day,
                    'total' => (int) $row->total,
                ]),
                'by_method' => $byMethod->map(fn ($row) => [
                    'method' => $row->method,
                    'total' => (int) $row->total,
                ]),
            ],
        ]);
    }

public function stock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeReport($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$lowStock = DB::table('${drugTable} as d')
            ->join('${stockTable} as s', 's.drug_id', '=', 'd.id')
            ->where('s.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->groupBy('d.id', 'd.${drugNameCol}')
            ->havingRaw('sum(s.${stockQtyCol}) <= ?', [self::LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD])
            ->selectRaw('d.${drugNameCol} as drug, sum(s.${stockQtyCol}) as total_quantity')
            ->get();

$expiringLots = DB::table('${stockTable} as s')
            ->join('${drugTable} as d', 'd.id', '=', 's.drug_id')
            ->where('s.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->where('s.${stockQtyCol}', '>', 0)
            ->where('s.${expiryCol}', '<=', now()->addDays(self::EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS))
            ->orderBy('s.${expiryCol}')
            ->selectRaw("d.${drugNameCol} as drug, s.${stockQtyCol} as quantity, s.${expiryCol} as expiry_date, extract(day from (s.${expiryCol} - now())) as days_left")
            ->get();

return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'low_stock_threshold' => self::LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD,
                'low_stock' => $lowStock->map(fn ($item) => [
                    'drug' => $item->drug,
                    'total_quantity' => (int) $item->total_quantity,
                ]),
                'expiring_window_days' => self::EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS,
                'expiring_lots' => $expiringLots->map(fn ($lot) => [
                    'drug' => $lot->drug,
                    'quantity' => (int) $lot->quantity,
                    'expiry_date' => $lot->expiry_date,
                    'days_left' => (int) $lot->days_left,
                ]),
            ],
        ]);
    }

private function period(Request $request): array
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'from' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'to' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:from'],
        ], [
            'to.after_or_equal' => 'La date de fin doit suivre la date de début.',
        ]);

$from = $data['from'] ?? now()->subDays(29)->toDateString();
        $to = $data['to'] ?? now()->toDateString();

return [$from, $to];
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeReport(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('reports.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas accès aux rapports.');
        }
    }
}
`
  );

===== scripts/m09/e2e.mjs ===== (361 lignes, 13 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M09 (4/4) : test de bout en bout — rapports & statistiques
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { API, log, ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8019;

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function detail(response) {
  try {
    const body = await response.text();
    return body ? ' — réponse API : ' + body.slice(0, 400) : '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

export async function runE2e() {
  log('M09/5', 'Test de bout en bout — rapports');

const baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;

const server = spawn(
    'php',
    ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', '--port=' + PORT],
    { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
  );

function authHeaders(token) {
    return {
      Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }

async function login(credentials) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    });

if (!response.ok) {
      fail('Connexion impossible pour ' + credentials.email + ' (HTTP ' + response.status + await detail(response) + ').');
    }

return (await response.json()).token;
  }

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
    const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(token),
    });

if (response.status !== 200) {
      fail('Bascule d\'établissement refusée (HTTP ' + response.status + await detail(response) + ').');
    }
  }

try {
    let ready = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
      if (server.exitCode !== null) {
        fail('Le serveur Laravel s\'est arrêté (code ' + server.exitCode + ').');
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/health', {
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        });
        ready = response.ok;
      } catch {
        await sleep(1000);
      }
    }

if (!ready) fail('L\'API n\'a pas répondu sur le port ' + PORT + '.');
    ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
    const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
    ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');

if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

const secFacilities = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/facilities', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    })).json();
    const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
    await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
    ok('Établissements actifs définis (admin → HREF, secrétaire → CSM)');

// Test 1 : vue d'ensemble par défaut
    const overviewResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (overviewResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Vue d\'ensemble : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + overviewResponse.status + await detail(overviewResponse) + '.');
    }

const overview = (await overviewResponse.json()).report;

if (!overview.patients) {
      fail('Champ patients manquant dans le rapport.');
    }
    if (overview.patients.total <= 0) {
      fail('Aucun patient comptabilisé dans HREF (seed M03 requis).');
    }
    if (!overview.period) {
      fail('Champ period manquant dans le rapport.');
    }
    if (!overview.appointments_by_status) {
      fail('Champ appointments_by_status manquant dans le rapport.');
    }
    if (!overview.lab_requests_by_status) {
      fail('Champ lab_requests_by_status manquant dans le rapport.');
    }
    if (!overview.billing) {
      fail('Champ billing manquant dans le rapport.');
    }
    ok('Vue d\'ensemble par défaut (structure complète, patients HREF comptés)');

// Test 2 : période invalide
    const badPeriod = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview?from=2026-01-10&to=2026-01-01', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (badPeriod.status !== 422) {
      fail('Période invalide : HTTP 422 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + badPeriod.status + await detail(badPeriod) + '.');
    }
    ok('Période invalide (fin avant début) rejetée (422)');

// Test 3 : période vide cohérente
    const oldResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview?from=2020-01-01&to=2020-01-31', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (oldResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Période 2020 : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + oldResponse.status + await detail(oldResponse) + '.');
    }

const oldReport = (await oldResponse.json()).report;

if (oldReport.patients.new_in_period !== 0) {
      fail('Nouveaux patients fantômes en 2020 : ' + oldReport.patients.new_in_period + '.');
    }
    if (oldReport.consultations_in_period !== 0) {
      fail('Consultations fantômes en 2020 : ' + oldReport.consultations_in_period + '.');
    }
    if (oldReport.billing.paid_in_period !== 0) {
      fail('Encaissements fantômes en 2020 : ' + oldReport.billing.paid_in_period + '.');
    }
    ok('Période vide (janvier 2020) : tous les compteurs de période à zéro');

// Test 4 : revenus — cohérence des séries
    const revenueResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/revenue', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (revenueResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Revenus : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + revenueResponse.status + await detail(revenueResponse) + '.');
    }

const revenue = (await revenueResponse.json()).report;

if (revenue.paid_total <= 0) {
      fail('Aucun encaissement comptabilisé — lancez d\'abord npm run m08.');
    }

let dailySum = 0;
    for (const day of revenue.daily) {
      dailySum += day.total;
    }
    if (dailySum !== revenue.paid_total) {
      fail('Séries quotidiennes incohérentes : somme ' + dailySum + ' ≠ total ' + revenue.paid_total + '.');
    }
    ok('Revenus : encaissements comptabilisés et série quotidienne cohérente');

// Test 5 : un encaissement du jour apparaît dans le rapport du jour
    const patientsData = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/patients', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    })).json();
    const hrefPatient = patientsData.patients.find((p) => p.local_code === 'P-HREF-0001');

if (!hrefPatient) fail('Patient P-HREF-0001 introuvable dans HREF.');

const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

const createResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({
        patient_id: hrefPatient.id,
        items: [{ label: 'Frais de rapport M09', quantity: 1, unit_price: 2500 }],
      }),
    });

if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
      fail('Facture de test : HTTP 201 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + createResponse.status + await detail(createResponse) + '.');
    }

const invoice = (await createResponse.json()).invoice;

const issueResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + invoice.id, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'issued' }),
    });

if (issueResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Émission facture de test : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + issueResponse.status + await detail(issueResponse) + '.');
    }

const payResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/invoices/' + invoice.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      body: JSON.stringify({ amount: 2500, method: 'cash' }),
    });

if (payResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Encaissement de test : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + payResponse.status + await detail(payResponse) + '.');
    }

const todayRevenue = await (await fetch(
      baseUrl + '/api/reports/revenue?from=' + today + '&to=' + today,
      { headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
    )).json();

if (todayRevenue.report.paid_total < 2500) {
      fail('L\'encaissement du jour n\'apparaît pas dans le rapport (' + todayRevenue.report.paid_total + ' < 2500).');
    }
    ok('Encaissement du jour reflété immédiatement dans le rapport quotidien');

// Test 6 : ventilation par mode de paiement
    const allowedMethods = ['cash', 'mobile_money', 'transfer'];

for (const entry of revenue.by_method) {
      if (!allowedMethods.includes(entry.method)) {
        fail('Mode de paiement inattendu : ' + entry.method + '.');
      }
    }

let methodSum = 0;
    for (const entry of revenue.by_method) {
      methodSum += entry.total;
    }
    if (methodSum !== revenue.paid_total) {
      fail('Ventilation par mode incohérente : somme ' + methodSum + ' ≠ total ' + revenue.paid_total + '.');
    }
    ok('Ventilation par mode de paiement valide et cohérente');

// Test 7 : stock — seuils bas et péremption
    const stockResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/stock', {
      headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    });

if (stockResponse.status !== 200) {
      fail('Stock : HTTP 200 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + stockResponse.status + await detail(stockResponse) + '.');
    }

const stock = (await stockResponse.json()).report;

if (!Array.isArray(stock.low_stock)) {
      fail('Champ low_stock manquant dans le rapport stock.');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(stock.expiring_lots)) {
      fail('Champ expiring_lots manquant dans le rapport stock.');
    }

for (const item of stock.low_stock) {
      if (item.total_quantity > stock.low_stock_threshold) {
        fail('Médicament au-dessus du seuil listé comme bas : ' + item.drug + '.');
      }
    }

for (const lot of stock.expiring_lots) {
      if (lot.quantity <= 0) {
        fail('Lot en alerte avec quantité nulle : ' + lot.drug + '.');
      }
      if (lot.days_left > stock.expiring_window_days) {
        fail('Lot hors fenêtre d\'alerte listé : ' + lot.drug + ' (' + lot.days_left + ' jours).');
      }
    }
    ok('Stock : ' + stock.low_stock.length + ' médicament(s) bas, ' + stock.expiring_lots.length + ' lot(s) en alerte péremption');

// Test 8 : isolation inter-établissements
    const adminCsm = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');

if (!adminCsm) {
      ok('Isolation : admin mono-établissement, rapport limité à HREF');
    } else {
      const hrefPatientsTotal = overview.patients.total;

await switchFacility(adminToken, adminCsm.id);

const csmOverview = await (await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview', {
        headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
      })).json();

if (csmOverview.report.patients.total === hrefPatientsTotal) {
        fail('Isolation non vérifiée : CSM et HREF montrent le même total (' + hrefPatientsTotal + ').');
      }

await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
      ok('Isolation HREF ↔ CSM : les rapports suivent l\'établissement actif');
    }

// Test 9 : secrétaire sans reports.view → 403 partout
    const secOverview = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });
    if (secOverview.status !== 403) {
      fail('Rapports (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secOverview.status + await detail(secOverview) + '.');
    }

const secRevenue = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/revenue', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });
    if (secRevenue.status !== 403) {
      fail('Revenus (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secRevenue.status + await detail(secRevenue) + '.');
    }

const secStock = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/stock', {
      headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    });
    if (secStock.status !== 403) {
      fail('Stock (secrétaire) : HTTP 403 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + secStock.status + await detail(secStock) + '.');
    }
    ok('Secrétaire sans reports.view : 3 rapports rejetés (403)');

// Test 10 : accès anonyme
    const anonResponse = await fetch(baseUrl + '/api/reports/overview', {
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    });
    if (anonResponse.status !== 401) {
      fail('Accès anonyme : HTTP 401 attendu, reçu HTTP ' + anonResponse.status + await detail(anonResponse) + '.');
    }
    ok('Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
  } finally {
    if (server.exitCode === null) {
      server.kill();
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }
}


===== scripts/m09/frontend.mjs ===== (533 lignes, 15 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M09 (3/4) : frontend — page Rapports (auto-découverte du routeur)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { ok, fail } from './helpers.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..');
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');
const VIEWS = path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views');

function firstNonSpace(text) {
  const spaces = [' ', '\n', '\r', '\t'];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {
    if (!spaces.includes(text[i])) {
      return i;
    }
  }
  return text.length;
}

function walkSourceFiles(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      out.push(...walkSourceFiles(full));
    } else if (entry.name.endsWith('.vue') || entry.name.endsWith('.js')) {
      out.push(full);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function detectGetCall(source) {
  const lines = source.split('\n');

for (const line of lines) {
    if (!line.includes('/invoices')) {
      continue;
    }

const direct = line.match(/([\w.$]+)\(\s*['"`]\S*\/invoices/);

if (direct) {
      return direct[1];
    }

const loose = line.match(/([\w.$]+)\(\s*[^)]*\/invoices/);

if (loose) {
      return loose[1];
    }
  }

return null;
}

function billingToReports(text) {
  return text
    .split('BillingView.vue').join('Reports.vue')
    .split('BillingView').join('ReportsView')
    .split('/facturation').join('/rapports')
    .split('Billing').join('Reports')
    .split('billing').join('reports')
    .split('Facturation').join('Rapports')
    .split('facturation').join('rapports');
}

// Clone toutes les déclarations (import, route, ligne de template)
// référençant la facturation, dans un fichier donné.
function cloneRegistrations(file) {
  let src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

if (src.includes('ReportsView')) {
    ok('Rapports déjà référencé dans ' + path.relative(ROOT, file));
    return 'already';
  }
  if (src.includes('Rapports')) {
    ok('Rapports déjà référencé dans ' + path.relative(ROOT, file));
    return 'already';
  }

let anchor = null;

if (src.includes('BillingView')) {
    anchor = 'BillingView';
  } else if (src.includes("'billing'")) {
    anchor = "'billing'";
  } else if (src.includes('"billing"')) {
    anchor = '"billing"';
  } else if (src.includes('/billing')) {
    anchor = '/billing';
  } else {
    return null;
  }

let patched = 0;
  let pos = 0;

while (patched < 6) {
    const idx = src.indexOf(anchor, pos);

if (idx === -1) {
      break;
    }

const lineStart = src.lastIndexOf('\n', idx) + 1;
    let lineEnd = src.indexOf('\n', idx);

if (lineEnd === -1) {
      lineEnd = src.length;
    }

const lineText = src.slice(lineStart, lineEnd);
    const trimmed = lineText.trim();

// Cas 1 : import statique du composant → clone de l'import
    if (trimmed.startsWith('import ')) {
      src = src.slice(0, lineEnd) + '\n' + billingToReports(lineText) + src.slice(lineEnd);
      patched += 1;
      pos = lineEnd + 1;
      continue;
    }

// Cas 2 : ligne de template (<BillingView .../>, bouton de menu) → clone de ligne
    if (trimmed.startsWith('<')) {
      src = src.slice(0, lineEnd) + '\n' + billingToReports(lineText) + src.slice(lineEnd);
      patched += 1;
      pos = lineEnd + 1;
      continue;
    }

// Cas 3 : objet englobant { ... } (route de routeur, entrée de menu script)
    const open = src.lastIndexOf('{', idx);

if (open === -1) {
      pos = idx + anchor.length;
      continue;
    }

let depth = 0;
    let close = -1;

for (let i = open; i < src.length; i += 1) {
      const ch = src[i];

if (ch === '{') {
        depth += 1;
      }
      if (ch === '}') {
        depth -= 1;
      }
      if (depth === 0) {
        close = i;
        break;
      }
    }

if (close === -1) {
      pos = idx + anchor.length;
      continue;
    }

if (close - open > 1200) {
      pos = idx + anchor.length;
      continue;
    }

const block = src.slice(open, close + 1);
    const clone = billingToReports(block);

const blockLineStart = src.lastIndexOf('\n', open) + 1;
    let indent = '';

for (const ch of src.slice(blockLineStart, open)) {
      if (![' ', '\t'].includes(ch)) {
        break;
      }
      indent += ch;
    }

const tail = src.slice(close + 1);
    const offset = firstNonSpace(tail);

if (tail[offset] === ',') {
      const at = close + 1 + offset + 1;
      src = src.slice(0, at) + '\n' + indent + clone + src.slice(at);
      pos = at + 1;
    } else {
      const at = close + 1;
      src = src.slice(0, at) + ',\n' + indent + clone + src.slice(at);
      pos = at + 2;
    }

patched += 1;
  }

if (patched === 0) {
    return null;
  }

fs.writeFileSync(file, src, 'utf8');
  ok('Rapports : ' + patched + ' référence(s) ajoutée(s) dans ' + path.relative(ROOT, file));
  return 'patched';
}

const REPORTS_TEMPLATE = String.raw`<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
__IMPORT__

function unwrap(res) {
  if (res && res.data) {
    return res.data;
  }
  return res;
}

function isoDate(offsetDays) {
  return new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

const from = ref(isoDate(-29));
const to = ref(isoDate(0));
const loading = ref(false);
const error = ref('');
const overview = ref(null);
const revenue = ref(null);
const stock = ref(null);

const appointmentLabels = {
  scheduled: 'Planifiés',
  confirmed: 'Confirmés',
  done: 'Honorés',
  cancelled: 'Annulés',
  no_show: 'Absents',
};

const methodLabels = {
  cash: 'Espèces',
  mobile_money: 'Mobile Money',
  transfer: 'Virement',
};

function label(map, key) {
  return map[key] || key;
}

function xof(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR').format(Number(value)) + ' XOF';
}

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';

try {
    const query = '?from=' + from.value + '&to=' + to.value;
    const o = unwrap(await __GET__('/reports/overview' + query, { token: auth.token }));
    const r = unwrap(await __GET__('/reports/revenue' + query, { token: auth.token }));
    const s = unwrap(await __GET__('/reports/stock', { token: auth.token }));
    overview.value = o.report;
    revenue.value = r.report;
    stock.value = s.report;
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = 'Erreur de chargement : ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : String(e));
  }

loading.value = false;
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <section class="reports-page">
    <h1>Rapports &amp; statistiques</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="load">
      <label>Du <input v-model="from" type="date" /></label>
      <label>au <input v-model="to" type="date" /></label>
      <button type="submit" :disabled="loading">
        {{ loading ? 'Chargement…' : 'Actualiser' }}
      </button>
    </form>

<div v-if="overview" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Patients</h2>
        <p class="big">{{ overview.patients.total }}</p>
        <p>{{ overview.patients.new_in_period }} nouveau(x) sur la période</p>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Rendez-vous (période)</h2>
        <ul>
          <li v-for="(total, status) in overview.appointments_by_status" :key="status">
            {{ label(appointmentLabels, status) }} : {{ total }}
          </li>
        </ul>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Activité clinique</h2>
        <p>Consultations : {{ overview.consultations_in_period }}</p>
        <p>Dispensations pharmacie : {{ overview.dispensations_in_period }}</p>
        <ul>
          <li v-for="(total, status) in overview.lab_requests_by_status" :key="status">
            Labo « {{ status }} » : {{ total }}
          </li>
        </ul>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Facturation (période)</h2>
        <p>Factures émises : {{ overview.billing.invoices_in_period }}</p>
        <p>Facturé : {{ xof(overview.billing.billed_in_period) }}</p>
        <p>Encaissé : {{ xof(overview.billing.paid_in_period) }}</p>
        <p>Reste à recouvrer (factures émises) : {{ xof(overview.billing.outstanding_total) }}</p>
        <p>
          Recouvrement :
          {{ overview.billing.recovery_rate_percent === null ? '—' : overview.billing.recovery_rate_percent + ' %' }}
        </p>
      </article>
    </div>

<div v-if="revenue" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Encaissements par jour</h2>
        <p class="big">{{ xof(revenue.paid_total) }}</p>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Jour</th><th>Montant</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="day in revenue.daily" :key="day.day">
              <td>{{ day.day }}</td>
              <td>{{ xof(day.total) }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Par mode de paiement</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Mode</th><th>Montant</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="entry in revenue.by_method" :key="entry.method">
              <td>{{ label(methodLabels, entry.method) }}</td>
              <td>{{ xof(entry.total) }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>
    </div>

<div v-if="stock" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Stocks bas (≤ {{ stock.low_stock_threshold }})</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Médicament</th><th>Quantité</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="item in stock.low_stock" :key="item.drug">
              <td>{{ item.drug }}</td>
              <td>{{ item.total_quantity }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Péremption ≤ {{ stock.expiring_window_days }} jours</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Médicament</th><th>Qté</th><th>Échéance</th><th>Jours</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="lot in stock.expiring_lots" :key="lot.drug + '-' + lot.expiry_date">
              <td>{{ lot.drug }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.quantity }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.expiry_date }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.days_left }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
.reports-page { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; }
.filters { display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.filters label { font-weight: 600; }
.grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }
.card { border: 1px solid #d1d5db; border-radius: 8px; padding: 16px; background: #fff; }
.card h2 { margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 1rem; }
.big { font-size: 1.6rem; font-weight: 700; margin: 4px 0; }
.error { color: #b91c1c; font-weight: 600; }
table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 8px; }
th, td { text-align: left; padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e5e7eb; }
ul { padding-left: 18px; margin: 8px 0 0; }
</style>
`;

export function installFrontend() {
  // 1. Vue de référence (M08)
  const viewFiles = fs.readdirSync(VIEWS).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.vue'));
  let billingName = viewFiles.find((f) => f.toLowerCase().includes('billing'));

if (!billingName) {
    billingName = viewFiles.find((f) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(VIEWS, f), 'utf8').includes('/invoices'));
  }

if (!billingName) {
    fail('Vue Facturation introuvable dans web/src/views — installez M08 d\'abord.');
  }

const billingPath = path.join(VIEWS, billingName);
  const billingSource = fs.readFileSync(billingPath, 'utf8');
  const billingLines = billingSource.split('\n');

const importLine = billingLines.find((l) => l.trim().startsWith('import ') && l.includes('client'))
    || billingLines.find((l) => l.trim().startsWith('import ') && l.includes('api'));

if (!importLine) {
    fail('Import du client API introuvable dans ' + billingName + ' — collez-moi ce fichier.');
  }

const clientCall = detectGetCall(billingSource);

if (!clientCall) {
    fail('Appel GET introuvable dans ' + billingName + ' — collez-moi ce fichier.');
  }

const getCall = clientCall.includes('.') ? clientCall + '(p)' : clientCall;

// 2. Écriture de la page Rapports
  const reportsVue = REPORTS_TEMPLATE
    .split('__IMPORT__').join(importLine.trim())
    .split('__GET__').join(getCall);

fs.writeFileSync(path.join(VIEWS, 'Reports.vue'), reportsVue, 'utf8');
  ok('Page Rapports écrite (client détecté : ' + clientCall + ')');

// 3. Auto-découverte : tout fichier de web/src référençant la facturation
  const allFiles = walkSourceFiles(path.join(WEB, 'src')).filter((file) => {
    if (file === billingPath) {
      return false;
    }
    if (file === path.join(VIEWS, 'Reports.vue')) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const withBilling = allFiles.filter((file) => {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

return /BillingView|\/facturation|Facturation|billing/i.test(src);
  });

withBilling.sort((a, b) => {
    const aRank = fs.readFileSync(a, 'utf8').includes('BillingView') ? 0 : 1;
    const bRank = fs.readFileSync(b, 'utf8').includes('BillingView') ? 0 : 1;
    return aRank - bRank;
  });

let references = 0;

for (const file of withBilling) {
    const result = cloneRegistrations(file);

if (result === 'patched') {
      references += 1;
    }
    if (result === 'already') {
      references += 1;
    }
  }

if (references === 0) {
    fail('Aucun routeur/menu référençant la facturation n\'a été trouvé dans web/src. '
      + 'Lancez : Get-ChildItem -Recurse web\\src -Include *.js,*.vue | Select-String -Pattern billing -List '
      + 'et collez-moi les fichiers listés.');
  }

// 4. Filet de sécurité : menus « Facturation » non détectés ci-dessus
    const navCandidates = allFiles.filter((file) => {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

if (!/Facturation|\/facturation/i.test(src)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (src.includes('Rapports')) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

for (const navFile of navCandidates) {
    const lines = fs.readFileSync(navFile, 'utf8').split('\n');
    const rebuilt = [];
    let navUpdated = false;

for (const line of lines) {
      rebuilt.push(line);

if (navUpdated) {
        continue;
      }
      if (!line.includes('Facturation')) {
        continue;
      }
      if (!line.includes('billing')) {
        continue;
      }

rebuilt.push(billingToReports(line));
      navUpdated = true;
    }

if (navUpdated) {
      fs.writeFileSync(navFile, rebuilt.join('\n'), 'utf8');
      ok('Lien « Rapports » ajouté dans ' + path.relative(ROOT, navFile));
    }
  }

if (navCandidates.length === 0) {
    ok('Menu : aucun lien « Facturation » additionnel à cloner');
  }
}


===== scripts/m09/helpers.mjs ===== (50 lignes, 1 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M04 : utilitaires partagés
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(
  path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  '..',
  '..'
);
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export function log(step, message) {
  console.log('\n[' + step + '] ' + message);
}

export function ok(message) {
  console.log('  ✓ ' + message);
}

export function fail(message) {
  console.error('  ✗ ' + message);
  process.exit(1);
}

export function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

export function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail('Impossible d\'exécuter "' + command + '" : ' + result.error.message);
  }

if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail('Échec de la commande : ' + command + ' (code ' + result.status + ')');
  }
}
