===== scripts/diag-m09-auth.mjs ===== (84 lignes, 3 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — diagnostic « Unauthenticated. » page Rapports
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, "..");
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, "web", "src");

function read(f) {
  try {
    return fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8");
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

function walk(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(full));
    else if (/\.(vue|js)$/.test(e.name)) out.push(full);
  }
  return out;
}

const files = walk(WEB);
let out = "HOSPIFLOW — DIAGNOSTIC M09 AUTH — " + new Date().toISOString() + "\n\n";

// 1. Reports.vue : imports + fonction load()
const reportsSrc = read(path.join(WEB, "views", "Reports.vue"));
out += "=== Reports.vue — 20 premières lignes ===\n" + reportsSrc.split("\n").slice(0, 20).join("\n") + "\n\n";
const loadIdx = reportsSrc.indexOf("async function load");
if (loadIdx !== -1) {
  out += "=== Reports.vue — fonction load() ===\n" + reportsSrc.slice(loadIdx, loadIdx + 800) + "\n\n";
}

// 2. Vue Facturation : imports + appels /invoices (la référence qui fonctionne)
const billing = files.find(
  (f) => /billing/i.test(path.basename(f)) && path.basename(f) !== "Reports.vue"
);
if (billing) {
  const bSrc = read(billing);
  const bLines = bSrc.split("\n");
  out += "=== " + path.relative(ROOT, billing) + " — imports ===\n";
  out += bLines.filter((l) => l.trim().startsWith("import ")).join("\n") + "\n\n";
  out += "=== " + path.relative(ROOT, billing) + " — lignes /invoices ===\n";
  out += bLines.filter((l) => l.includes("/invoices")).join("\n") + "\n\n";
}

// 3. Contenu intégral de web/src/api (petit, c'est la pièce maîtresse)
const apiDir = path.join(WEB, "api");
if (fs.existsSync(apiDir)) {
  for (const f of walk(apiDir)) {
    out += "=== " + path.relative(ROOT, f) + " (intégral) ===\n" + read(f) + "\n\n";
  }
}

// 4. Comment le jeton est attaché (Bearer / Authorization / localStorage / store)
out += "=== Gestion du jeton dans web/src (fichier:ligne) ===\n";
for (const f of files) {
  const src = read(f);
  if (!src) continue;
  src.split("\n").forEach((line, i) => {
    if (/Authorization|Bearer|sanctum|localStorage|getToken|access_token/i.test(line)) {
      out += path.relative(ROOT, f) + ":" + (i + 1) + ": " + line.trim().slice(0, 180) + "\n";
    }
  });
}

// 5. Où la session est enregistrée côté client (store auth)
const authFiles = files.filter((f) => /auth|user|session|store/i.test(path.basename(f)));
out += "\n=== Fichiers candidats auth/store ===\n";
for (const f of authFiles) {
  out += "- " + path.relative(ROOT, f) + "\n";
}

const dest = path.join(ROOT, "M09-diag.txt");
fs.writeFileSync(dest, out, "utf8");
console.log("Diagnostic écrit : M09-diag.txt (" + Math.round(fs.statSync(dest).size / 1024) + " Ko)");
console.log("Uploads-le dans le chat (ou colle son contenu) pour analyse.");


===== scripts/fix-m09-auth.mjs ===== (169 lignes, 6 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — correctif 401 « Unauthenticated. » page Rapports
// Cause : client api(path, { token }) — Reports.vue n'envoie pas le jeton Bearer.
// Ajoute useAuthStore + { token: auth.token } sur les 3 appels /reports/*.
// Corrige aussi le gabarit de frontend.mjs pour les régénérations.
// Idempotent : relançable sans risque.
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, "..");
const REPORTS = path.join(ROOT, "web", "src", "views", "Reports.vue");
const FRONTEND = path.join(HERE, "m09", "frontend.mjs");

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Reports.vue — import du store + jeton sur les 3 appels
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if (!fs.existsSync(REPORTS)) {
  console.error("✗ Reports.vue introuvable : " + REPORTS);
  process.exit(1);
}

let vue = fs.readFileSync(REPORTS, "utf8");
let changed = false;

if (!vue.includes("useAuthStore")) {
  fs.copyFileSync(REPORTS, REPORTS + ".bak");

const importLine = "import { api } from '../api/client';";
  const authImport = "import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';";

if (vue.includes(importLine)) {
    vue = vue.replace(importLine, importLine + "\n" + authImport);
    changed = true;
  } else {
    console.log("⚠ Ligne d'import du client non trouvée telle quelle — vérification manuelle");
  }

// Instanciation du store avant la première utilisation
  const anchor = "function unwrap(res) {";
  const storeLine = "const auth = useAuthStore();";

if (vue.includes(anchor) && !vue.includes(storeLine)) {
    vue = vue.replace(anchor, storeLine + "\n\n" + anchor);
    changed = true;
  }

// Jeton sur les 3 appels
  const callFixes = [
    ["api('/reports/overview' + query)", "api('/reports/overview' + query, { token: auth.token })"],
    ["api('/reports/revenue' + query)", "api('/reports/revenue' + query, { token: auth.token })"],
    ["api('/reports/stock')", "api('/reports/stock', { token: auth.token })"],
  ];

for (const [before, after] of callFixes) {
    if (vue.includes(before)) {
      vue = vue.split(before).join(after);
      changed = true;
      console.log("✓ Reports.vue : jeton ajouté sur " + before.slice(0, 26) + "…");
    }
  }

if (changed) {
    fs.writeFileSync(REPORTS, vue, "utf8");
  }
} else {
  console.log("= Reports.vue : useAuthStore déjà présent");
}

const vueNow = fs.readFileSync(REPORTS, "utf8");
let failures = 0;

for (const marker of [
  "useAuthStore",
  "const auth = useAuthStore();",
  "{ token: auth.token }",
]) {
  if (vueNow.includes(marker)) {
    console.log("✓ Reports.vue contient : " + marker);
  } else {
    console.error("✗ Reports.vue manque : " + marker);
    failures += 1;
  }
}

if ((vueNow.match(/\{ token: auth\.token \}/g) || []).length < 3) {
  console.error("✗ Reports.vue : moins de 3 appels avec jeton");
  failures += 1;
} else {
  console.log("✓ Reports.vue : les 3 appels /reports/* portent le jeton");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. frontend.mjs — gabarit : mêmes correctifs pour les régénérations futures
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
let fe = fs.readFileSync(FRONTEND, "utf8");
let feChanged = false;

const templateFixes = [
  // Import du store dans le gabarit
  [
    "__IMPORT__\n\nfunction unwrap(res) {",
    "__IMPORT__\nimport { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';\n\nconst auth = useAuthStore();\n\nfunction unwrap(res) {",
  ],
  // Jeton dans les 3 appels du gabarit
  [
    "__GET__('/reports/overview' + query)",
    "__GET__('/reports/overview' + query, { token: auth.token })",
  ],
  [
    "__GET__('/reports/revenue' + query)",
    "__GET__('/reports/revenue' + query, { token: auth.token })",
  ],
  [
    "__GET__('/reports/stock')",
    "__GET__('/reports/stock', { token: auth.token })",
  ],
];

for (const [before, after] of templateFixes) {
  if (fe.includes(after)) {
    console.log("= frontend.mjs : correctif gabarit déjà en place");
  } else if (fe.includes(before)) {
    if (!feChanged) {
      fs.copyFileSync(FRONTEND, FRONTEND + ".bak");
    }
    fe = fe.replace(before, after);
    feChanged = true;
    console.log("✓ frontend.mjs : gabarit corrigé — " + before.slice(0, 40).replace("\n", " ") + "…");
  }
}

if (feChanged) {
  fs.writeFileSync(FRONTEND, fe, "utf8");
}

const feCheck = spawnSync(process.execPath, ["--check", FRONTEND], { encoding: "utf8" });

if (feCheck.status !== 0) {
  if (fs.existsSync(FRONTEND + ".bak")) {
    fs.copyFileSync(FRONTEND + ".bak", FRONTEND);
  }
  console.error("✗ node --check frontend.mjs a échoué — original restauré");
  console.error((feCheck.stderr || "").slice(0, 500));
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log("✓ frontend.mjs : syntaxe valide (node --check)");

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Anti-corruption
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
for (const file of [REPORTS, FRONTEND]) {
  if (fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").includes("<mark")) {
    console.error("✗ Corruption <mark dans " + path.relative(ROOT, file));
    failures += 1;
  }
}

console.log("");

if (failures === 0) {
  console.log("Correctif terminé — recharge la page Rapports (hot-reload Vite).");
} else {
  console.log(failures + " contrôle(s) en échec");
  process.exit(1);
}


===== scripts/fix-m09-frontend.mjs ===== (192 lignes, 7 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — patch automatique de scripts/m09/frontend.mjs
// Applique les 4 correctifs de détection/câblage de la page Rapports.
// Idempotent : relançable ; sauvegarde .bak ; node --check de contrôle.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const FILE = path.join(HERE, 'm09', 'frontend.mjs');

if (!fs.existsSync(FILE)) {
  console.error('✗ Introuvable : ' + FILE);
  process.exit(1);
}

let src = fs.readFileSync(FILE, 'utf8');
fs.copyFileSync(FILE, FILE + '.bak');

const report = [];

// Remplace le bloc compris entre startMarker (inclus) et endMarker (inclus).
function replaceBlock(label, startMarker, endMarker, newBlock, alreadyMarker) {
  if (alreadyMarker && src.includes(alreadyMarker)) {
    report.push('= ' + label + ' : déjà correct');
    return;
  }

const start = src.indexOf(startMarker);

if (start === -1) {
    report.push('⚠ ' + label + ' : bloc introuvable (variante déjà différente ?)');
    return;
  }

const end = src.indexOf(endMarker, start + startMarker.length);

if (end === -1) {
    report.push('✗ ' + label + ' : fin de bloc introuvable — patch annulé, voir .bak');
    return;
  }

src = src.slice(0, start) + newBlock + src.slice(end + endMarker.length);
  report.push('✓ ' + label + ' : corrigé');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Correctif 1 — billingToReports : gérer le chemin /facturation → /rapports
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const NEW_BILLING = String.raw`function billingToReports(text) {
  return text
    .split('BillingView.vue').join('Reports.vue')
    .split('BillingView').join('ReportsView')
    .split('/facturation').join('/rapports')
    .split('Billing').join('Reports')
    .split('billing').join('reports')
    .split('Facturation').join('Rapports')
    .split('facturation').join('rapports');
}`;

replaceBlock(
  'billingToReports',
  'function billingToReports(text) {',
  '\n}',
  NEW_BILLING,
  "split('/facturation').join('/rapports')"
);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Correctif 2 — ancres de cloneRegistrations : /facturation et Facturation
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const NEW_ANCHORS = String.raw`  let anchor = null;

if (src.includes('BillingView')) {
    anchor = 'BillingView';
  } else if (src.includes('/facturation')) {
    anchor = '/facturation';
  } else if (src.includes('Facturation')) {
    anchor = 'Facturation';
  } else if (src.includes("'billing'")) {
    anchor = "'billing'";
  } else if (src.includes('"billing"')) {
    anchor = '"billing"';
  } else if (src.includes('/billing')) {
    anchor = '/billing';
  } else {
    return null;
  }`;

replaceBlock(
  'Ancres de détection',
  '  let anchor = null;',
  '    return null;\n  }',
  NEW_ANCHORS,
  "src.includes('/facturation')"
);

// Variante : version à fonction findAnchor(candidates) — mise à jour de la liste
if (src.includes('function findAnchor(')) {
  const oldCandidates = "const candidates = ['BillingView', 'Facturation', 'facturation', '/invoices', 'billing', 'Billing'];";

if (src.includes(oldCandidates)) {
    src = src.split(oldCandidates).join("const candidates = ['BillingView', '/facturation', 'Facturation', 'facturation', '/invoices', 'billing', 'Billing'];");
    report.push('✓ findAnchor : /facturation priorisé');
  } else if (src.includes("'/facturation', 'Facturation'")) {
    report.push('= findAnchor : déjà correct');
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Correctif 3 — sélection des fichiers : détection insensible à la casse
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const NEW_FILTER = String.raw`  const withBilling = allFiles.filter((file) => {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

return /BillingView|\/facturation|Facturation|billing/i.test(src);
  });`;

replaceBlock(
  'Sélection des fichiers',
  'const withBilling = allFiles.filter(',
  '  );',
  NEW_FILTER,
  '/BillingView|\\/facturation|Facturation|billing/i.test(src)'
);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Correctif 4 — filet de sécurité menu : ne plus exiger "billing" à côté
//               de "Facturation", et matcher le texte de ligne aussi
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const NEW_NAV = String.raw`  const navCandidates = allFiles.filter((file) => {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

if (!/Facturation|\/facturation/i.test(src)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (src.includes('Rapports')) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });`;

replaceBlock(
  'Filtre navCandidates',
  'const navCandidates = allFiles.filter((file) => {',
  '  });',
  NEW_NAV,
  '/Facturation|\\/facturation/i.test(src)'
);

const oldLineChecks = "      if (!line.includes('Facturation')) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (!line.includes('billing')) {\n        continue;\n      }";
const newLineCheck = String.raw`      if (!/Facturation|\/facturation/i.test(line)) {
        continue;
      }`;

if (src.includes(oldLineChecks)) {
  src = src.split(oldLineChecks).join(newLineCheck);
  report.push('✓ Match de ligne (menu) : corrigé');
} else if (src.includes(newLineCheck)) {
  report.push('= Match de ligne (menu) : déjà correct');
} else {
  report.push('⚠ Match de ligne (menu) : motif introuvable (variante)');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Écriture + contrôle de syntaxe
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fs.writeFileSync(FILE, src, 'utf8');

const check = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--check', FILE], { encoding: 'utf8' });

if (check.status !== 0) {
  fs.copyFileSync(FILE + '.bak', FILE);
  console.error('✗ node --check a échoué après le patch — original restauré depuis .bak');
  console.error(check.stderr);
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log('Patch de scripts/m09/frontend.mjs :');
for (const line of report) {
  console.log('  ' + line);
}

if (report.some((l) => l.startsWith('✗'))) {
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log('');
console.log('Syntaxe OK (node --check). Sauvegarde de l\'original : frontend.mjs.bak');
console.log('Prochaine étape : npm run m09');


===== scripts/fix-m09-reports-call.mjs ===== (112 lignes, 4 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — correctif « p is not defined » (Reports.vue + frontend.mjs M09)
// Cause : client API de type api(chemin) enveloppé à tort en api(p)(chemin).
// Idempotent : relançable sans risque.
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, "..");
const REPORTS = path.join(ROOT, "web", "src", "views", "Reports.vue");
const FRONTEND = path.join(HERE, "m09", "frontend.mjs");

let failures = 0;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Reports.vue : motif  nom(p)(  →  nom(
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if (!fs.existsSync(REPORTS)) {
  console.error("✗ Reports.vue introuvable : " + REPORTS);
  process.exit(1);
}

let vue = fs.readFileSync(REPORTS, "utf8");
const wrapper = /\b([A-Za-z_$][\w.$]*)\(p\)\(/g;
const matches = [...vue.matchAll(wrapper)];

if (matches.length === 0) {
  console.log("= Reports.vue : aucun appel à enveloppe à corriger");
} else {
  fs.copyFileSync(REPORTS, REPORTS + ".bak");
  vue = vue.replace(wrapper, "$1(");
  fs.writeFileSync(REPORTS, vue, "utf8");
  const names = [...new Set(matches.map((m) => m[1]))].join(", ");
  console.log("✓ Reports.vue : " + matches.length + " appel(s) corrigé(s) — " + names);
}

const vueNow = fs.readFileSync(REPORTS, "utf8");

if (vueNow.includes("(p)(")) {
  console.error("✗ Reports.vue : des appels à enveloppe subsistent");
  failures += 1;
} else {
  console.log("✓ Reports.vue : plus aucun appel à enveloppe");
}

for (const endpoint of ["/reports/overview", "/reports/revenue", "/reports/stock"]) {
  if (vueNow.includes(endpoint)) {
    console.log("✓ Reports.vue appelle " + endpoint);
  } else {
    console.error("✗ Reports.vue n'appelle pas " + endpoint);
    failures += 1;
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. frontend.mjs : getCall adaptatif (méthode .get vs fonction directe)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if (!fs.existsSync(FRONTEND)) {
  console.error("✗ frontend.mjs introuvable : " + FRONTEND);
  process.exit(1);
}

let fe = fs.readFileSync(FRONTEND, "utf8");
const oldLine = "const getCall = clientCall + '(p)';";
const newLine = "const getCall = clientCall.includes('.') ? clientCall + '(p)' : clientCall;";

if (fe.includes(newLine)) {
  console.log("= frontend.mjs : construction getCall déjà corrigée");
} else if (fe.includes(oldLine)) {
  fs.copyFileSync(FRONTEND, FRONTEND + ".bak");
  fe = fe.replace(oldLine, newLine);
  fs.writeFileSync(FRONTEND, fe, "utf8");
  console.log("✓ frontend.mjs : getCall adaptatif installé");
} else {
  console.log("⚠ frontend.mjs : ligne getCall introuvable — vérification manuelle nécessaire");
}

const feCheck = spawnSync(process.execPath, ["--check", FRONTEND], { encoding: "utf8" });

if (feCheck.status !== 0) {
  if (fs.existsSync(FRONTEND + ".bak")) {
    fs.copyFileSync(FRONTEND + ".bak", FRONTEND);
  }
  console.error("✗ node --check frontend.mjs a échoué — original restauré");
  console.error((feCheck.stderr || "").slice(0, 500));
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log("✓ frontend.mjs : syntaxe valide (node --check)");

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Contrôles anti-corruption
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
for (const file of [REPORTS, FRONTEND]) {
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  if (src.includes("<mark")) {
    console.error("✗ Corruption <mark détectée dans " + path.relative(ROOT, file));
    failures += 1;
  }
}

console.log("");

if (failures === 0) {
  console.log("Correctif terminé — recharge la page Rapports dans la webapp.");
  console.log("Si le serveur Vite tourne, le hot-reload suffit ; sinon : cd web puis npm run dev");
} else {
  console.log(failures + " contrôle(s) en échec — voir ci-dessus");
  process.exit(1);
}


===== scripts/lib.mjs ===== (69 lignes, 3 Ko)
// HOSPIFLOW — librairie commune des scripts d'automatisation des modules.
// Chaque module (m00, m01, …) importe ces helpers. Idempotent par conception.
import { execSync, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

// Racine du monorepo : hospiflow/
//   hospiflow/package.json   <- orchestrateur
//   hospiflow/scripts/*.mjs  <- un script par module
//   hospiflow/api/           <- API Laravel (créée par m00)
//   hospiflow/web/           <- Webapp Vue 3 (créée par m00)
export const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
export const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
export const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

export const log = (step, msg) => console.log(`\n[${step}] ${msg}`);
export const ok = (msg) => console.log(`  ✓ ${msg}`);
export const warn = (msg) => console.log(`  ! ${msg}`);
export const fail = (msg) => {
  console.error(`\n  ✗ ${msg}`);
  process.exit(1);
};

// Version du premier token de la sortie de `bin --version` (ex: "v20.11.0", "PHP 8.3.1").
export function version(bin) {
  try {
    const out = execSync(`${bin} --version`, { encoding: 'utf8' });
    const m = out.match(/(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)/);
    return m ? { major: +m[1], minor: +m[2], patch: +m[3] } : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function has(bin) {
  return version(bin) !== null;
}

export function requireVersion(bin, minMajor, minMinor, installHint) {
  const v = version(bin);
  if (!v) fail(`${bin} est introuvable. ${installHint}`);
  if (v.major < minMajor || (v.major === minMajor && v.minor < minMinor))
    fail(`${bin} ${v.major}.${v.minor}.${v.patch} trouvé, mais >= ${minMajor}.${minMinor} requis. ${installHint}`);
  ok(`${bin} ${v.major}.${v.minor}.${v.patch} — conforme`);
}

// Exécute une commande shell avec sortie live. Échec bloquant sauf allowFail=true.
export function run(cmd, { cwd = ROOT, allowFail = false } = {}) {
  const res = spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, stdio: 'inherit', shell: true });
  if (res.status !== 0 && !allowFail) fail(`Échec de la commande : ${cmd} (dans ${cwd})`);
  return res.status === 0;
}

// Comme run() mais silencieux : renvoie true/false (utilisé pour les sondes).
export function tryRun(cmd, cwd = ROOT) {
  const res = spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, stdio: 'pipe', shell: true });
  return res.status === 0;
}

// Écrit un fichier en créant les dossiers parents si besoin.
export function write(file, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(file, content);
  ok(`créé : ${path.relative(ROOT, file)}`);
}

export const sleep = (ms) => spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', `new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,${ms}))`]);


===== scripts/m00-bootstrap.mjs ===== (108 lignes, 4 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — Module 00 : Bootstrap
// Crée le monorepo : API Laravel + Webapp Vue 3 + PostgreSQL (Docker),
// configure .env, applique les migrations et lance les tests.
// Idempotent : relançable sans risque (chaque étape se saute si déjà faite).
// Prérequis : Node >= 20, PHP >= 8.2, Composer >= 2, Docker (facultatif si PostgreSQL dispo).
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { ROOT, API, WEB, log, ok, warn, fail, requireVersion, has, run, tryRun, write, sleep } from './lib.mjs';

const DB = { host: '127.0.0.1', port: '5432', db: 'hospiflow', user: 'hospiflow', pass: 'hospiflow_dev' };

// ---------- 1. Prérequis ----------
log('M00/1', 'Vérification des prérequis');
requireVersion('node', 20, 0, 'Installe Node.js LTS >= 20 depuis https://nodejs.org');
requireVersion('php', 8, 2, 'Installe PHP >= 8.2 (avec extensions pgsql, mbstring, openssl, pdo).');
requireVersion('composer', 2, 0, 'Installe Composer >= 2 depuis https://getcomposer.org');
if (!has('docker')) warn('Docker absent : utilise un PostgreSQL local accessible et vérifie api/.env.');

// ---------- 2. Infrastructure locale (PostgreSQL) ----------
log('M00/2', 'Infrastructure locale');
write(
  path.join(ROOT, 'docker-compose.yml'),
  `services:
  db:
    image: postgres:16-alpine
    container_name: hospiflow-db
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${DB.db}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${DB.user}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB.pass}
    ports:
      - "${DB.port}:5432"
    volumes:
      - hospiflow-db:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${DB.user} -d ${DB.db}"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20

volumes:
  hospiflow-db:
`
);
if (has('docker')) run('docker compose up -d db', { cwd: ROOT });

// ---------- 3. API Laravel ----------
log('M00/3', 'API Laravel');
if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(API, 'composer.json'))) {
  run('composer create-project laravel/laravel api --no-interaction', { cwd: ROOT });
} else ok('api/ existe déjà — étape ignorée');

// Sanctum (API tokens) + Spatie Permission (rôles/permissions) — cf. context.md §11.2
run('composer require laravel/sanctum spatie/laravel-permission --no-interaction', { cwd: API });
run('php artisan vendor:publish --provider="Spatie\\Permission\\PermissionServiceProvider" --no-interaction', { cwd: API });

// .env : PostgreSQL + français (langue MVP, cf. context.md §5)
const envPath = path.join(API, '.env');
let env = fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8');
const set = (k, v) => {
  const re = new RegExp(`^${k}=.*$`, 'm');
  env = re.test(env) ? env.replace(re, `${k}=${v}`) : `${env}\n${k}=${v}`;
};
set('APP_NAME', '"HOSPIFLOW"');
set('APP_LOCALE', 'fr');
set('APP_FAKER_LOCALE', 'fr_FR');
set('DB_CONNECTION', 'pgsql');
set('DB_HOST', DB.host);
set('DB_PORT', DB.port);
set('DB_DATABASE', DB.db);
set('DB_USERNAME', DB.user);
set('DB_PASSWORD', DB.pass);
fs.writeFileSync(envPath, env);
ok('.env configuré (PostgreSQL, locale fr)');
run('php artisan key:generate --force', { cwd: API });

// ---------- 4. Migrations (attend que PostgreSQL soit prêt) ----------
log('M00/4', 'Migrations');
let migrated = false;
for (let i = 1; i <= 30 && !migrated; i++) {
  migrated = tryRun('php artisan migrate --force', API);
  if (!migrated) {
    warn(`PostgreSQL pas prêt (tentative ${i}/30)…`);
    sleep(2000);
  }
}
if (!migrated) fail('PostgreSQL injoignable après 30 tentatives. Vérifie : docker compose logs db');
ok('Migrations appliquées');

// ---------- 5. Webapp Vue 3 ----------
log('M00/5', 'Webapp Vue 3');
if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(WEB, 'package.json'))) {
  run('npm create vite@latest web -- --template vue', { cwd: ROOT });
} else ok('web/ existe déjà — étape ignorée');
run('npm install', { cwd: WEB });
run('npm install vue-router@4 pinia @vueuse/core', { cwd: WEB });
// PWA/offline (M10) : préinstallation du plugin, la config viendra au module M10.
run('npm install -D vite-plugin-pwa', { cwd: WEB });

// ---------- 6. Vérifications finales ----------
log('M00/6', 'Vérifications');
run('php artisan test', { cwd: API });
run('npm run build', { cwd: WEB });

ok('Module 00 terminé : monorepo opérationnel.');
console.log('\nProchaine étape : "npm run m01" — Auth, rôles et permissions.');


===== scripts/m01-auth.mjs ===== (839 lignes, 22 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M01 : Authentification, rôles et permissions
// Prérequis : M00 terminé, PostgreSQL configuré dans api/.env.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

const ADMIN = {
  email: 'admin@hospiflow.local',
  password: 'hospiflow_admin',
};
const PORT = 8010;

function log(step, message) {
  console.log(`\n[${step}] ${message}`);
}

function ok(message) {
  console.log(`  ✓ ${message}`);
}

function fail(message) {
  console.error(`  ✗ ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

/**
 * Exécute une commande depuis le dossier indiqué.
 * Les commandes du script sont des chaînes fixes, sans données utilisateur.
 */
function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });

if (result.error) {
    fail(`Impossible d'exécuter "${command}" : ${result.error.message}`);
  }
  if (result.status !== 0) {
    fail(`Échec de la commande : ${command} (code ${result.status})`);
  }
}

/**
 * Exécute la commande Artisan install:api, qui peut déjà avoir été exécutée.
 * En cas d'échec, la suite vérifie que les fichiers requis existent déjà.
 */
function tryRun(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, {
    cwd,
    shell: true,
    stdio: 'inherit',
    env: process.env,
  });
  return result.status === 0;
}

// M01/1 — Prérequis
log('M01/1', 'Vérification du module M00');

for (const required of [
  path.join(API, 'composer.json'),
  path.join(API, '.env'),
  path.join(WEB, 'package.json'),
]) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(required)) {
    fail(`M00 incomplet : fichier manquant ${required}.`);
  }
}
ok('api/ et web/ présents');

// M01/2 — API Laravel, Sanctum et routes
log('M01/2', 'Backend Laravel — authentification, rôles et permissions');

const installApiSucceeded = tryRun(
  'php artisan install:api --no-interaction',
  API
);

const routesApiPath = path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php');
const bootstrapPath = path.join(API, 'bootstrap', 'app.php');

if (!fs.existsSync(routesApiPath)) {
  if (!installApiSucceeded) {
    fail(
      'La commande install:api a échoué et routes/api.php est absent. ' +
        'Vérifie api/ puis relance le module.'
    );
  }
  // Laravel install:api crée normalement ce fichier.
  writeFile(routesApiPath, '<?php\n\nuse Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Route;\n');
}

if (!fs.existsSync(bootstrapPath)) {
  fail('Fichier Laravel introuvable : api/bootstrap/app.php.');
}

let bootstrap = fs.readFileSync(bootstrapPath, 'utf8');

if (!bootstrap.includes("api: __DIR__.'/../routes/api.php'")) {
  const webRoutePattern = /web:\s*__DIR__\s*\.\s*['"]\/\.\.\/routes\/web\.php['"]\s*,?/;

if (!webRoutePattern.test(bootstrap)) {
    fail(
      'Impossible d’enregistrer routes/api.php automatiquement dans ' +
        'bootstrap/app.php. Vérifie ce fichier avant de continuer.'
    );
  }

bootstrap = bootstrap.replace(
    webRoutePattern,
    (match) =>
      `${match.replace(/,\s*$/, '')},\n        api: __DIR__.'/../routes/api.php',`
  );

fs.writeFileSync(bootstrapPath, bootstrap, 'utf8');
}
ok('Routing API enregistré');

// Migration utilisateurs : téléphone et compte actif
const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
fs.mkdirSync(migrationsDir, { recursive: true });

const userMigrationExists = fs
  .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
  .some((filename) => filename.includes('add_phone_is_active_to_users_table'));

if (!userMigrationExists) {
  const timestamp = new Date()
    .toISOString()
    .replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '')
    .slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
    path.join(
      migrationsDir,
      `${timestamp}_add_phone_is_active_to_users_table.php`
    ),
    `<?php

use Illuminate\\Database\\Migrations\\Migration;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Schema\\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->string('phone', 30)->nullable();
            $table->boolean('is_active')->default(true);
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropColumn(['phone', 'is_active']);
        });
    }
};
`
  );
  ok('Migration phone/is_active créée');
} else {
  ok('Migration phone/is_active déjà présente');
}

// Modèle utilisateur
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'User.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Models;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Factories\\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\\Foundation\\Auth\\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\\Notifications\\Notifiable;
use Laravel\\Sanctum\\HasApiTokens;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Traits\\HasRoles;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasApiTokens, HasFactory, HasRoles, Notifiable;

protected $guard_name = 'web';

protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
        'phone',
        'is_active',
    ];

protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }
}
`
);

// Contrôleur d'authentification
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'AuthController.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Http\\Controllers;

use App\\Models\\User;
use Illuminate\\Http\\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\\Http\\Request;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Hash;
use Illuminate\\Validation\\ValidationException;

class AuthController extends Controller
{
    public function login(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ], [
            'email.required' => "L'adresse e-mail est obligatoire.",
            'email.email' => "L'adresse e-mail est invalide.",
            'password.required' => 'Le mot de passe est obligatoire.',
        ]);

$user = User::where('email', $data['email'])->first();

if (! $user || ! Hash::check($data['password'], $user->password)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => 'Identifiants incorrects.',
            ]);
        }

if (! $user->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Compte désactivé. Contactez un administrateur.');
        }

$user->tokens()->where('name', 'spa')->delete();

return response()->json([
            'token' => $user->createToken('spa')->plainTextToken,
            'user' => $this->payload($user),
        ]);
    }

public function me(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        return response()->json([
            'user' => $this->payload($request->user()),
        ]);
    }

public function logout(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $token = $request->user()->currentAccessToken();

if ($token && method_exists($token, 'delete')) {
            $token->delete();
        }

return response()->json(['message' => 'Déconnecté.']);
    }

private function payload(User $user): array
    {
        $user->loadMissing('roles.permissions');

return [
            'id' => $user->id,
            'name' => $user->name,
            'email' => $user->email,
            'phone' => $user->phone,
            'roles' => $user->getRoleNames(),
            'permissions' => $user->getAllPermissions()->pluck('name'),
        ];
    }
}
`
);

// Routes API
writeFile(
  routesApiPath,
  `<?php

use App\\Http\\Controllers\\AuthController;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Route;

Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json([
    'app' => 'HOSPIFLOW',
    'status' => 'ok',
]));

Route::prefix('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
        Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    });
});
`
);

// Rôles, permissions et compte administrateur local
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php'),
  `<?php

namespace Database\\Seeders;

use App\\Models\\User;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Seeder;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Models\\Permission;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Models\\Role;
use Spatie\\Permission\\PermissionRegistrar;

class RolesAndPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

$matrix = [
            'users.manage' => ['admin'],
            'patients.view' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'infirmier', 'secretaire'],
            'patients.manage' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'secretaire'],
            'appointments.manage' => ['admin', 'secretaire', 'infirmier'],
            'lab.results.manage' => ['admin', 'laborantin'],
            'pharmacy.manage' => ['admin', 'pharmacien'],
            'billing.manage' => ['admin', 'comptable'],
            'reports.view' => ['admin', 'medecin', 'comptable'],
        ];

foreach (array_keys($matrix) as $permissionName) {
            Permission::findOrCreate($permissionName, 'web');
        }

foreach ($matrix as $permissionName => $roleNames) {
            foreach ($roleNames as $roleName) {
                Role::findOrCreate($roleName, 'web')
                    ->givePermissionTo($permissionName);
            }
        }

Role::findByName('admin', 'web')
            ->syncPermissions(array_keys($matrix));

$user = User::firstOrCreate(
            ['email' => '${ADMIN.email}'],
            [
                'name' => 'Administrateur HOSPIFLOW',
                'password' => '${ADMIN.password}',
                'phone' => null,
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

$user->assignRole('admin');
    }
}
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'DatabaseSeeder.php'),
  `<?php

namespace Database\\Seeders;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Seeder;

class DatabaseSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        $this->call([
            RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class,
        ]);
    }
}
`
);

ok('Modèle, contrôleur, routes et seeder écrits');

// M01/3 — Migrations et seed
log('M01/3', 'Migrations et création du compte initial');
run('php artisan migrate --force', API);
run('php artisan db:seed --force', API);
ok('Migrations et seed terminés');

// M01/4 — Frontend Vue
log('M01/4', 'Webapp Vue — router, store et écrans');
run('npm install vue-router@4 pinia @vueuse/core', WEB);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, '.env'),
  'VITE_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000/api\n'
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'api', 'client.js'),
  `const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api';

export async function api(path, { method = 'GET', body, token } = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(\`\${BASE}\${path}\`, {
    method,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: \`Bearer \${token}\` } : {}),
    },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });

const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.message ||
        (data.errors && Object.values(data.errors).flat().join(' ')) ||
        \`Erreur HTTP \${response.status}\`
    );
  }

return data;
}
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'stores', 'auth.js'),
  `import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
import { api } from '../api/client';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'hospiflow_token';
const USER_KEY = 'hospiflow_user';

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', {
  state: () => ({
    token: localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY),
    user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY) || 'null'),
  }),

getters: {
    isAuthenticated: (state) => Boolean(state.token),
    roles: (state) => state.user?.roles || [],
  },

actions: {
    async login(email, password) {
      const data = await api('/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { email, password },
      });

this.token = data.token;
      this.user = data.user;

localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, this.token);
      localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.user));
    },

async logout() {
      try {
        await api('/auth/logout', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: this.token,
        });
      } catch {
        // Le token peut déjà avoir expiré ou être révoqué.
      }

this.token = null;
      this.user = null;
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    },
  },
});
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
  `import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
);

writeFile(

import { ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const email = ref('');
const password = ref('');
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);
const router = useRouter();
const auth = useAuthStore();

async function submit() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';

try {
    await auth.login(email.value, password.value);
    await router.push('/');
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message || 'Connexion impossible.';
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}
</script>

          type="password"
          required
          autocomplete="current-password"
        />
      </label>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<button type="submit" :disabled="loading">
        {{ loading ? 'Connexion…' : 'Se connecter' }}

}

`
);

// M01/5 — Tests de bout en bout
log('M01/5', 'Test de bout en bout de l’API');

const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
const server = spawn(
  'php',
  ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', `--port=${PORT}`],
  {
    cwd: API,
    stdio: 'ignore',
    windowsHide: true,
  }
);

const sleep = (milliseconds) =>
  new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds));

async function stopServer() {
  if (server.exitCode === null) {
    server.kill();
    await sleep(300);
  }
}

try {
  let serverReady = false;

for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !serverReady; attempt += 1) {
    if (server.exitCode !== null) {
      fail(`Le serveur Laravel s’est arrêté (code ${server.exitCode}).`);
    }

try {
      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/health`, {
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
      });
      serverReady = response.ok;
    } catch {
      await sleep(1000);
    }
  }

if (!serverReady) {
    fail(`L’API n’a pas répondu sur le port ${PORT}.`);
  }
  ok('API en ligne');

const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    Accept: 'application/json',
  };

const loginResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers,
    body: JSON.stringify(ADMIN),
  });

if (!loginResponse.ok) {
    fail(`Échec de connexion admin (HTTP ${loginResponse.status}).`);
  }

const { token, user } = await loginResponse.json();

if (!token || !user) {
    fail('La réponse de connexion ne contient pas de token ou de profil.');
  }
  ok(`Connexion OK — ${user.email} | rôles : ${user.roles.join(', ')}`);

const meResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/me`, {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      Accept: 'application/json',
    },
  });

if (meResponse.status !== 200) {
    fail(`Échec de /api/auth/me (HTTP ${meResponse.status}).`);
  }
  ok('/api/auth/me protégé par Sanctum');

const badLoginResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers,
    body: JSON.stringify({
      email: ADMIN.email,
      password: 'mot-de-passe-incorrect',
    }),
  });

if (badLoginResponse.status !== 422) {
    fail(
      `Mauvais mot de passe : attendu HTTP 422, reçu HTTP ${badLoginResponse.status}.`
    );
  }
  ok('Rejet des identifiants incorrects (HTTP 422)');

const logoutResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/logout`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      Accept: 'application/json',
    },
  });

if (logoutResponse.status !== 200) {
    fail(`Échec de déconnexion (HTTP ${logoutResponse.status}).`);
  }
  ok('Déconnexion et révocation du token OK');
} finally {
  await stopServer();
}

log('M01', 'Terminé');
console.log('');
console.log('Compte administrateur local de démarrage :');
console.log(`  E-mail       : ${ADMIN.email}`);
console.log(`  Mot de passe : ${ADMIN.password}`);
console.log('À changer avant toute utilisation réelle.');
console.log('');
console.log('Pour démarrer les applications dans deux terminaux :');
console.log('  API : cd api; php artisan serve');
console.log('  Web : cd web; npm run dev');


===== scripts/m02-facilities.mjs ===== (1024 lignes, 30 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M02 : Établissements (multi-tenant) & isolation stricte
// Prérequis : M01 terminé (auth Sanctum + Spatie opérationnels).
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8011;

function log(step, message) {
  console.log(`\n[${step}] ${message}`);
}

function ok(message) {
  console.log(`  ✓ ${message}`);
}

function fail(message) {
  console.error(`  ✗ ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, { cwd, shell: true, stdio: 'inherit', env: process.env });
  if (result.error) fail(`Impossible d'exécuter "${command}" : ${result.error.message}`);
  if (result.status !== 0) fail(`Échec de la commande : ${command} (code ${result.status})`);
}

// ---------- M02/1 : Prérequis ----------
log('M02/1', 'Vérification du module M01');

for (const required of [
  path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php'),
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'AuthController.php'),
  path.join(API, '.env'),
]) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(required)) {
    fail(`M01 incomplet : fichier manquant ${required}. Relance "npm run m01".`);
  }
}
ok('API M01 opérationnelle');

// ---------- M02/2 : Backend ----------
log('M02/2', 'Backend — établissements, adhésions, isolation');

// 2a. Migration
const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
const migrationExists = fs
  .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
  .some((f) => f.includes('create_facilities_tables'));

if (!migrationExists) {
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
    path.join(migrationsDir, `${timestamp}_create_facilities_tables.php`),
    `<?php

use Illuminate\\Database\\Migrations\\Migration;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Schema\\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('facilities', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->string('code', 20)->unique();
            $table->string('name');
            $table->string('city', 100)->nullable();
            $table->string('address', 255)->nullable();
            $table->string('phone', 30)->nullable();
            $table->string('email')->nullable();
            $table->boolean('is_active')->default(true);
            $table->timestamps();
            $table->softDeletes();
        });

Schema::create('facility_user', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->foreignId('user_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->timestamps();
            $table->unique(['facility_id', 'user_id']);
        });

Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table
                ->foreignId('current_facility_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->constrained('facilities')
                ->nullOnDelete();
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropConstrainedForeignId('current_facility_id');
        });
        Schema::dropIfExists('facility_user');
        Schema::dropIfExists('facilities');
    }
};
`
  );
  ok('Migration facilities/facility_user/current_facility_id créée');
} else {
  ok('Migration déjà présente');
}

// 2b. Modèle Facility
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'Facility.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Models;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Model;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Relations\\BelongsToMany;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\SoftDeletes;

class Facility extends Model
{
    use SoftDeletes;

protected $fillable = [
        'code',
        'name',
        'city',
        'address',
        'phone',
        'email',
        'is_active',
    ];

protected $casts = [
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
    ];

public function users(): BelongsToMany
    {
        return $this->belongsToMany(User::class);
    }

public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query->where('is_active', true);
    }
}
`
);

// 2c. Modèle User (M01 + relations établissements)
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'User.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Models;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Factories\\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Relations\\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Relations\\BelongsToMany;
use Illuminate\\Foundation\\Auth\\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\\Notifications\\Notifiable;
use Laravel\\Sanctum\\HasApiTokens;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Traits\\HasRoles;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasApiTokens, HasFactory, HasRoles, Notifiable;

protected $guard_name = 'web';

protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
        'phone',
        'is_active',
    ];

protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }

public function facilities(): BelongsToMany
    {
        return $this->belongsToMany(Facility::class);
    }

public function currentFacility(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Facility::class, 'current_facility_id');
    }

public function isMemberOf(Facility $facility): bool
    {
        return $this->facilities()
            ->where('facilities.id', $facility->id)
            ->exists();
    }
}
`
);

// 2d. Contrôleur FacilityController
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'FacilityController.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Http\\Controllers;

use App\\Models\\Facility;
use App\\Models\\User;
use Illuminate\\Http\\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\\Http\\Request;

class FacilityController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

$facilities = $user->can('facilities.manage')
            ? Facility::query()->orderBy('name')->get()
            : $user->facilities()->orderBy('name')->get();

return response()->json([
            'facilities' => $facilities,
            'current_facility_id' => $user->current_facility_id,
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'code' => ['required', 'string', 'max:20', 'unique:facilities,code'],
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'city' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'email' => ['nullable', 'email'],
        ], [
            'code.required' => 'Le code établissement est obligatoire.',
            'code.unique' => 'Ce code établissement existe déjà.',
            'name.required' => 'Le nom de l\\'établissement est obligatoire.',
        ]);

$facility = Facility::create($data);
        $request->user()->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$facility->id]);

return response()->json(['facility' => $facility], 201);
    }

public function update(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['sometimes', 'required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'city' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'email' => ['nullable', 'email'],
            'is_active' => ['sometimes', 'boolean'],
        ]);

$facility->update($data);

return response()->json(['facility' => $facility->fresh()]);
    }

public function switch(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

if (! $user->isMemberOf($facility)) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\\'êtes pas membre de cet établissement.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Cet établissement est désactivé.');
        }

$user->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $facility->id])->save();

return response()->json([
            'message' => 'Établissement actif mis à jour.',
            'current_facility_id' => $facility->id,
        ]);
    }

public function attachUser(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email', 'exists:users,email'],
        ], [
            'email.exists' => 'Aucun utilisateur avec cette adresse e-mail.',
        ]);

$target = User::where('email', $data['email'])->firstOrFail();
        $facility->users()->syncWithoutDetaching([$target->id]);

return response()->json([
            'message' => 'Utilisateur rattaché à l\\'établissement.',
            'user_id' => $target->id,
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
        ]);
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('facilities.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Action réservée aux administrateurs.');
        }
    }
}
`
);

// 2e. Routes API (complètes : auth + facilities)
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php'),
  `<?php

use App\\Http\\Controllers\\AuthController;
use App\\Http\\Controllers\\FacilityController;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Route;

Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json([
    'app' => 'HOSPIFLOW',
    'status' => 'ok',
]));

Route::prefix('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
        Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    });
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('facilities')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [FacilityController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [FacilityController::class, 'store']);
    Route::patch('/{facility}', [FacilityController::class, 'update'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/switch', [FacilityController::class, 'switch'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/users', [FacilityController::class, 'attachUser'])->scopeBindings();
});
`
);

// 2f. Seeder : permissions + établissements de démarrage
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'database', 'seeders', 'FacilitiesSeeder.php'),
  `<?php

namespace Database\\Seeders;

use App\\Models\\Facility;
use App\\Models\\User;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Seeder;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Models\\Permission;
use Spatie\\Permission\\Models\\Role;
use Spatie\\Permission\\PermissionRegistrar;

class FacilitiesSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

$view = Permission::findOrCreate('facilities.view', 'web');
        $manage = Permission::findOrCreate('facilities.manage', 'web');

Role::findByName('admin', 'web')->givePermissionTo([$view, $manage]);

foreach (['medecin', 'infirmier', 'secretaire', 'laborantin', 'pharmacien', 'comptable'] as $roleName) {
            Role::findOrCreate($roleName, 'web')->givePermissionTo($view);
        }

$csm = Facility::firstOrCreate(
            ['code' => 'CSM'],
            [
                'name' => 'Clinique Saint-Michel',
                'city' => 'Cotonou',
                'address' => 'Zone résidentielle, Cotonou',
                'phone' => '+229 21 00 00 00',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

$href = Facility::firstOrCreate(
            ['code' => 'HREF'],
            [
                'name' => 'Hôpital de Référence',
                'city' => 'Porto-Novo',
                'address' => 'Avenue de la République, Porto-Novo',
                'phone' => '+229 21 00 00 01',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );

$admin = User::where('email', 'admin@hospiflow.local')->firstOrFail();
        $admin->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$csm->id, $href->id]);

if (! $admin->current_facility_id) {
            $admin->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $csm->id])->save();
        }

$secretary = User::firstOrCreate(
            ['email' => 'secretaire@hospiflow.local'],
            [
                'name' => 'Secrétaire Démarrage',
                'password' => 'hospiflow_sec',
                'is_active' => true,
            ]
        );
        $secretary->assignRole('secretaire');
        $secretary->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$csm->id]);

if (! $secretary->current_facility_id) {
            $secretary->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $csm->id])->save();
        }
    }
}
`
);

writeFile(

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'api', 'client.js'),
  `const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api';

export async function api(path, { method = 'GET', body, token, facilityId } = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(\`\${BASE}\${path}\`, {
    method,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: \`Bearer \${token}\` } : {}),
      ...(facilityId ? { 'X-Facility-Id': String(facilityId) } : {}),
    },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });

const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.message ||
        (data.errors && Object.values(data.errors).flat().join(' ')) ||
        \`Erreur HTTP \${response.status}\`
    );
  }

return data;
}
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'stores', 'auth.js'),
  `import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
import { api } from '../api/client';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'hospiflow_token';
const USER_KEY = 'hospiflow_user';
const FACILITY_KEY = 'hospiflow_facility';

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', {
  state: () => ({
    token: localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY),
    user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY) || 'null'),
    currentFacilityId: localStorage.getItem(FACILITY_KEY),
  }),

getters: {
    isAuthenticated: (state) => Boolean(state.token),
    roles: (state) => state.user?.roles || [],
    canManageFacilities: (state) =>
      (state.user?.permissions || []).includes('facilities.manage'),
  },

actions: {
    async login(email, password) {
      const data = await api('/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { email, password },
      });

this.token = data.token;
      this.user = data.user;

localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, this.token);
      localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.user));
    },

async loadFacilities() {
      const data = await api('/facilities', { token: this.token });
      this.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
      localStorage.setItem(FACILITY_KEY, this.currentFacilityId || '');
      return data.facilities;
    },

async switchFacility(facilityId) {
      const data = await api('/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
        method: 'POST',
        token: this.token,
      });
      this.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
      localStorage.setItem(FACILITY_KEY, this.currentFacilityId || '');
    },

async logout() {
      try {
        await api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', token: this.token });
      } catch {
        // Token peut être déjà révoqué ou expiré.
      }

this.token = null;
      this.user = null;
      this.currentFacilityId = null;
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(FACILITY_KEY);
    },
  },
});
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js'),
  `import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`
);

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'FacilitiesView.vue'),
  `<script setup>
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const facilities = ref([]);
const currentFacilityId = ref(null);
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const code = ref('');
const name = ref('');
const city = ref('');

const canManage = computed(() => auth.canManageFacilities);

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const data = await api('/facilities', { token: auth.token });
    facilities.value = data.facilities;
    currentFacilityId.value = data.current_facility_id;
    auth.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function createFacility() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/facilities', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        code: code.value.trim(),
        name: name.value.trim(),
        city: city.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    code.value = '';
    name.value = '';
    city.value = '';
    await load();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function switchFacility(facilityId) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await auth.switchFacility(facilityId);
    currentFacilityId.value = auth.currentFacilityId;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Établissements</h1>
      <p class="muted">
        Votre établissement actif détermine les données visibles dans les
        prochains modules.
      </p>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<table v-if="facilities.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Code</th>
            <th>Nom</th>
            <th>Ville</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th></th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="facility in facilities" :key="facility.id">
            <td>{{ facility.code }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.city }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.is_active ? 'Actif' : 'Désactivé' }}</td>
            <td>
              <button
                v-if="facility.id !== currentFacilityId"
                :disabled="!facility.is_active"
                @click="switchFacility(facility.id)"
              >
                Définir actif
              </button>
              <span v-else class="badge">Actif</span>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else-if="!loading">Aucun établissement rattaché à votre compte.</p>

<form v-if="canManage" class="create" @submit.prevent="createFacility">
        <h2>Créer un établissement</h2>
        <label>
          Code
          <input v-model="code" required maxlength="20" placeholder="CLIN01" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Nom
          <input v-model="name" required placeholder="Clinique Nouvelle Espérance" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Ville
          <input v-model="city" placeholder="Cotonou" />
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Créer</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>
