// ---------- M02/5 : Test de bout en bout ----------
log('M02/5', 'Test de bout en bout — isolation multi-établissements');

const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
const server = spawn(
  'php',
  ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', `--port=${PORT}`],
  { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
);

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function stopServer() {
  if (server.exitCode === null) {
    server.kill();
    await sleep(300);
  }
}

async function login(credentials) {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    fail(`Connexion impossible pour ${credentials.email} (HTTP ${response.status}).`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).token;
}

try {
  let ready = false;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
    if (server.exitCode !== null) {
      fail(`Le serveur Laravel s'est arrêté (code ${server.exitCode}).`);
    }
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/health`, {
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
      });
      ready = response.ok;
    } catch {
      await sleep(1000);
    }
  }
  if (!ready) fail(`L'API n'a pas répondu sur le port ${PORT}.`);
  ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
  const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
  ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

const authHeaders = (token) => ({
    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  });

// 1. Admin crée un établissement
  const createResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    body: JSON.stringify({
      code: 'E2E01',
      name: 'Clinique E2E',
      city: 'Abomey-Calavi',
    }),
  });
  if (createResponse.status !== 201) {
    fail(`Création d'établissement par admin attendue en 201, reçu ${createResponse.status}.`);
  }
  ok('Création d\'établissement par admin (201)');

// 2. Admin voit ses établissements
  const adminList = await (await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
  })).json();
  const adminCodes = adminList.facilities.map((f) => f.code).sort();
  for (const expected of ['CSM', 'HREF', 'E2E01']) {
    if (!adminCodes.includes(expected)) {
      fail(`L'admin devrait voir l'établissement ${expected}. Reçu : ${adminCodes.join(', ')}`);
    }
  }
  ok(`Admin voit bien ses 3 établissements (${adminCodes.join(', ')})`);

// 3. Admin bascule vers l'établissement créé
  const e2eFacility = adminList.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'E2E01');
  const switchResponse = await fetch(
    `${baseUrl}/api/facilities/${e2eFacility.id}/switch`,
    { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders(adminToken) }
  );
  if (switchResponse.status !== 200) {
    fail(`Bascule admin attendue en 200, reçu ${switchResponse.status}.`);
  }
  ok('Bascule d\'établissement par admin (200)');

// 4. La secrétaire ne voit QUE son établissement (isolation)
  const secretaryList = await (await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
  })).json();
  const secretaryCodes = secretaryList.facilities.map((f) => f.code);
    if (secretaryCodes.length !== 1) {
    fail(
      `Isolation rompue : la secrétaire devrait ne voir qu'un seul établissement, reçu ${secretaryCodes.join(', ')}.`
    );
  }
  if (secretaryCodes[0] !== 'CSM') {
    fail(
      `Isolation rompue : la secrétaire devrait ne voir que CSM, reçu ${secretaryCodes.join(', ')}.`
    );
  }

  ok('Isolation : la secrétaire ne voit que CSM');

// 5. La secrétaire ne peut PAS basculer vers HREF
  const hrefFacility = adminList.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');
  const forbiddenSwitch = await fetch(
    `${baseUrl}/api/facilities/${hrefFacility.id}/switch`,
    { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken) }
  );
  if (forbiddenSwitch.status !== 403) {
    fail(`Bascule interdite attendue en 403, reçu ${forbiddenSwitch.status}.`);
  }
  ok('Bascule vers un établissement non membre rejetée (403)');

// 6. La secrétaire ne peut PAS créer d'établissement
  const forbiddenCreate = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    body: JSON.stringify({ code: 'HACK', name: 'Clinique Pirate' }),
  });
  if (forbiddenCreate.status !== 403) {
    fail(`Création interdite attendue en 403, reçu ${forbiddenCreate.status}.`);
  }
  ok('Création d\'établissement par secrétaire rejetée (403)');
} finally {
  await stopServer();
}

log('M02', 'Terminé');
console.log('');
console.log('Comptes de démarrage :');
console.log(`  Admin      : ${ADMIN.email} / ${ADMIN.password}`);
console.log(`  Secrétaire : ${SECRETARY.email} / ${SECRETARY.password}`);
console.log('Mot de passe à changer avant toute utilisation réelle.');


===== scripts/m03-patients.mjs ===== (1115 lignes, 35 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — M03 : Patients & MPI (identifiant réseau fédéré)
// Prérequis : M02 terminé (établissements + isolation opérationnels).
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_admin' };
const SECRETARY = { email: 'secretaire@hospiflow.local', password: 'hospiflow_sec' };
const PORT = 8012;

function log(step, message) {
  console.log(`\n[${step}] ${message}`);
}

function ok(message) {
  console.log(`  ✓ ${message}`);
}

function fail(message) {
  console.error(`  ✗ ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

function writeFile(filePath, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, 'utf8');
}

function run(command, cwd) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, { cwd, shell: true, stdio: 'inherit', env: process.env });
  if (result.error) fail(`Impossible d'exécuter "${command}" : ${result.error.message}`);
  if (result.status !== 0) fail(`Échec de la commande : ${command} (code ${result.status})`);
}

// ---------- M03/1 : Prérequis ----------
log('M03/1', 'Vérification du module M02');

for (const required of [
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'Facility.php'),
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'FacilityController.php'),
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'FacilitiesView.vue'),
]) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(required)) {
    fail(`M02 incomplet : fichier manquant ${required}. Relance "npm run m02".`);
  }
}
ok('Module M02 opérationnel');

// ---------- M03/2 : Backend ----------
log('M03/2', 'Backend — patients, MPI, isolation par établissement');

// 2a. Migration
const migrationsDir = path.join(API, 'database', 'migrations');
const migrationExists = fs
  .readdirSync(migrationsDir)
  .some((f) => f.includes('create_patients_tables'));

if (!migrationExists) {
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:TZ.]/g, '').slice(0, 14);

writeFile(
    path.join(migrationsDir, `${timestamp}_create_patients_tables.php`),
    `<?php

use Illuminate\\Database\\Migrations\\Migration;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Schema\\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('master_patient_index', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->uuid('id')->primary();
            $table->string('hospiflow_code', 20)->unique();
            $table->string('first_name');
            $table->string('last_name');
            $table->date('date_of_birth')->nullable();
            $table->string('gender', 20)->nullable();
            $table->string('phone', 30)->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();
        });

Schema::create('patients', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->foreignId('facility_id')->constrained()->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->uuid('mpi_id');
            $table->foreign('mpi_id')->references('id')
                ->on('master_patient_index')->cascadeOnDelete();
            $table->string('local_code', 30);
            $table->string('first_name');
            $table->string('last_name');
            $table->date('date_of_birth')->nullable();
            $table->string('gender', 20)->nullable();
            $table->string('phone', 30)->nullable();
            $table->string('address', 255)->nullable();
            $table->string('blood_group', 10)->nullable();
            $table->text('allergies')->nullable();
            $table->timestamps();
            $table->softDeletes();
            $table->unique(['facility_id', 'mpi_id']);
            $table->unique(['facility_id', 'local_code']);
        });
    }

public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('patients');
        Schema::dropIfExists('master_patient_index');
    }
};
`
  );
  ok('Migration patients/master_patient_index créée');
} else {
  ok('Migration déjà présente');
}

// 2b. Modèle MasterPatientIndex
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'MasterPatientIndex.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Models;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Model;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Str;

class MasterPatientIndex extends Model
{
    public $incrementing = false;

protected $keyType = 'string';

protected $table = 'master_patient_index';

protected $fillable = [
        'id',
        'hospiflow_code',
        'first_name',
        'last_name',
        'date_of_birth',
        'gender',
        'phone',
    ];

protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::creating(function (self $mpi) {
            if (empty($mpi->id)) {
                $mpi->id = Str::uuid()->toString();
            }

if (empty($mpi->hospiflow_code)) {
                $mpi->hospiflow_code = 'HF-' . strtoupper(bin2hex(random_bytes(6)));
            }
        });
    }

public function patients()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Patient::class, 'mpi_id');
    }
}
`
);

// 2c. Modèle Patient
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Models', 'Patient.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Models;

use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Model;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\Relations\\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\\Database\\Eloquent\\SoftDeletes;

class Patient extends Model
{
    use SoftDeletes;

protected $fillable = [
        'facility_id',
        'mpi_id',
        'local_code',
        'first_name',
        'last_name',
        'date_of_birth',
        'gender',
        'phone',
        'address',
        'blood_group',
        'allergies',
    ];

protected $casts = [
        'date_of_birth' => 'date:Y-m-d',
    ];

public function facility(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Facility::class);
    }

public function mpi(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(MasterPatientIndex::class, 'mpi_id');
    }

public function scopeInFacility($query, Facility $facility)
    {
        return $query->where('facility_id', $facility->id);
    }
}
`
);

// 2d. Contrôleur PatientController
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'PatientController.php'),
  `<?php

namespace App\\Http\\Controllers;

use App\\Models\\Facility;
use App\\Models\\MasterPatientIndex;
use App\\Models\\Patient;
use Illuminate\\Http\\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\\Http\\Request;

class PatientController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$patients = $facility->patients()
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->orderBy('last_name')
            ->orderBy('first_name')
            ->get();

return response()->json([
            'facility' => ['id' => $facility->id, 'code' => $facility->code],
            'patients' => $patients->map(fn ($p) => $this->payload($p)),
        ]);
    }

public function search(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);
        $q = trim((string) $request->query('q', ''));

if ($q === '') {
            return response()->json(['results' => []]);
        }

$patients = $facility->patients()
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->where(function ($w) use ($q) {
                $w->where('first_name', 'ilike', '%'.$q.'%')
                    ->orWhere('last_name', 'ilike', '%'.$q.'%')
                    ->orWhere('local_code', 'ilike', '%'.$q.'%')
                    ->orWhere('phone', 'ilike', '%'.$q.'%')
                    ->orWhereHas('mpi', function ($m) use ($q) {
                        $m->where('hospiflow_code', 'ilike', '%'.$q.'%');
                    });
            })
            ->limit(50)
            ->get();

return response()->json([
            'results' => $patients->map(fn ($p) => $this->payload($p)),
        ]);
    }

public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$data = $request->validate([
            'first_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'last_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'date_of_birth' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'gender' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:20', 'in:male,female,other'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'blood_group' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:10'],
            'allergies' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'mpi_code' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:20'],
        ], [
            'first_name.required' => 'Le prénom est obligatoire.',
            'last_name.required' => 'Le nom est obligatoire.',
        ]);

$mpi = null;

if (! empty($data['mpi_code'])) {
            $mpi = MasterPatientIndex::where('hospiflow_code', $data['mpi_code'])->first();

if (! $mpi) {
                abort(422, 'Identifiant réseau HOSPIFLOW inconnu.');
            }
        }

if (! $mpi) {
            $mpi = MasterPatientIndex::create([
                'first_name' => $data['first_name'],
                'last_name' => $data['last_name'],
                'date_of_birth' => $data['date_of_birth'] ?? null,
                'gender' => $data['gender'] ?? null,
                'phone' => $data['phone'] ?? null,
            ]);
        }

$existing = $facility->patients()->where('mpi_id', $mpi->id)->first();

if ($existing) {
            return response()->json([
                'patient' => $this->payload($existing->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')),
                'message' => 'Ce patient réseau existe déjà dans cet établissement.',
            ]);
        }

$patient = $facility->patients()->create([
            'mpi_id' => $mpi->id,
            'local_code' => $this->nextLocalCode($facility),
            'first_name' => $data['first_name'],
            'last_name' => $data['last_name'],
            'date_of_birth' => $data['date_of_birth'] ?? null,
            'gender' => $data['gender'] ?? null,
            'phone' => $data['phone'] ?? null,
            'address' => $data['address'] ?? null,
            'blood_group' => $data['blood_group'] ?? null,
            'allergies' => $data['allergies'] ?? null,
        ]);

return response()->json(
            ['patient' => $this->payload($patient->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code'))],
            201
        );
    }

public function show(Request $request, int $patientId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeView($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->findOrFail($patientId);

return response()->json(['patient' => $this->payload($patient)]);
    }

public function update(Request $request, int $patientId): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

$facility = $this->currentFacility($request);

$patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($patientId);

$data = $request->validate([
            'first_name' => ['sometimes', 'required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'last_name' => ['sometimes', 'required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'date_of_birth' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'gender' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:20', 'in:male,female,other'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'blood_group' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:10'],
            'allergies' => ['nullable', 'string'],
        ]);

$patient->update(array_filter($data, fn ($v) => $v !== null));

return response()->json([
            'patient' => $this->payload($patient->fresh()->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')),
        ]);
    }

private function currentFacility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->user()->currentFacility;

if (! $facility) {
            abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
        }

if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
        }

return $facility;
    }

private function authorizeView(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('patients.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\\'avez pas le droit de consulter les patients.');
        }
    }

private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('patients.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\\'avez pas le droit de gérer les patients.');
        }
    }

private function nextLocalCode(Facility $facility): string
    {
        $count = $facility->patients()->withTrashed()->count() + 1;

return 'P-' . strtoupper($facility->code) . '-'
            . str_pad((string) $count, 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
    }

private function payload(Patient $patient): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $patient->id,
            'local_code' => $patient->local_code,
            'hospiflow_code' => $patient->mpi->hospiflow_code ?? null,
            'first_name' => $patient->first_name,
            'last_name' => $patient->last_name,
            'date_of_birth' => $patient->date_of_birth?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'gender' => $patient->gender,
            'phone' => $patient->phone,
            'address' => $patient->address,
            'blood_group' => $patient->blood_group,
            'allergies' => $patient->allergies,
            'facility_id' => $patient->facility_id,
        ];
    }
}
`
);

// 2e. Routes API (auth + facilities + patients)
writeFile(
  path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php'),
  `<?php

use App\\Http\\Controllers\\AuthController;
use App\\Http\\Controllers\\FacilityController;
use App\\Http\\Controllers\\PatientController;
use Illuminate\\Support\\Facades\\Route;

Route::get('/health', fn () => response()->json([
    'app' => 'HOSPIFLOW',
    'status' => 'ok',
]));

Route::prefix('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/me', [AuthController::class, 'me']);
        Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    });
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('facilities')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [FacilityController::class, 'index']);
    Route::post('/', [FacilityController::class, 'store']);
    Route::patch('/{facility}', [FacilityController::class, 'update'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/switch', [FacilityController::class, 'switch'])->scopeBindings();
    Route::post('/{facility}/users', [FacilityController::class, 'attachUser'])->scopeBindings();
});

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->prefix('patients')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/', [PatientController::class, 'index']);
    Route::get('/search', [PatientController::class, 'search']);
    Route::post('/', [PatientController::class, 'store']);
    Route::get('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'show'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
    Route::patch('/{patientId}', [PatientController::class, 'update'])
        ->whereNumber('patientId');
});
`
);

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),

    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },

      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;
`

writeFile(
  path.join(WEB, 'src', 'views', 'PatientsView.vue'),
  `<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const patients = ref([]);
const query = ref('');
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const firstName = ref('');
const lastName = ref('');
const dateOfBirth = ref('');
const gender = ref('');
const phone = ref('');
const address = ref('');
const bloodGroup = ref('');
const mpiCode = ref('');

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const data = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = data.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function search() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    if (query.value.trim() === '') {
      await load();
      return;
    }
    const data = await api(
      '/patients/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query.value.trim()),
      { token: auth.token }
    );
    patients.value = data.results;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createPatient() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/patients', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        first_name: firstName.value.trim(),
        last_name: lastName.value.trim(),
        date_of_birth: dateOfBirth.value || null,
        gender: gender.value || null,
        phone: phone.value.trim() || null,
        address: address.value.trim() || null,
        blood_group: bloodGroup.value.trim() || null,
        mpi_code: mpiCode.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    firstName.value = '';
    lastName.value = '';
    dateOfBirth.value = '';
    gender.value = '';
    phone.value = '';
    address.value = '';
    bloodGroup.value = '';
    mpiCode.value = '';
    await load();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Patients</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="search" @submit.prevent="search">
        <input
          v-model="query"
          placeholder="Nom, téléphone, code local ou code réseau HF-…"
        />
        <button type="submit">Rechercher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="patients.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Code local</th>
            <th>Code réseau</th>
            <th>Nom</th>
            <th>Naissance</th>
            <th>Téléphone</th>
            <th>Groupe</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id">
            <td>{{ patient.local_code }}</td>
            <td><code>{{ patient.hospiflow_code }}</code></td>
            <td>{{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }}</td>
            <td>{{ patient.date_of_birth || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ patient.phone || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ patient.blood_group || '—' }}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else-if="!loading">Aucun patient dans cet établissement pour l'instant.</p>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createPatient">
        <h2>Nouveau patient</h2>
        <p class="muted">
          Laissez « Code réseau » vide pour créer un nouveau patient réseau ;
          renseignez-le (HF-…) pour rattacher ce patient dans cet établissement.
        </p>
        <label>
          Prénom
          <input v-model="firstName" required />
        </label>
        <label>
          Nom
          <input v-model="lastName" required />
        </label>
        <label>
          Date de naissance
          <input v-model="dateOfBirth" type="date" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Sexe
          <select v-model="gender">
            <option value="">—</option>
            <option value="female">Féminin</option>
            <option value="male">Masculin</option>
            <option value="other">Autre</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Téléphone
          <input v-model="phone" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Adresse
          <input v-model="address" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Groupe sanguin
          <input v-model="bloodGroup" maxlength="10" placeholder="O+" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Code réseau (optionnel)
          <input v-model="mpiCode" placeholder="HF-XXXXXXXXXXXX" />
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Enregistrer</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
.page { min-height: 100vh; }
header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;

</style>
`
);

// Dashboard : lien Patients

import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();
const router = useRouter();
const currentFacilityName = ref('');

async function logout() {
  await auth.logout();
  await router.push('/login');
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const facilities = await auth.loadFacilities();
    const current = facilities.find(
      (facility) => facility.id === auth.currentFacilityId
    );
    currentFacilityName.value = current ? current.name : '';
  } catch {
    currentFacilityName.value = '';
  }
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="dash">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>

        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>

        <span>{{ auth.user?.name }}</span>

      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Bienvenue, {{ auth.user?.name }}</h1>
      <p>
        Rôles :
        <span v-for="role in auth.roles" :key="role" class="badge">{{ role }}</span>
      </p>
      <p v-if="currentFacilityName">
        Établissement actif : <strong>{{ currentFacilityName }}</strong>
      </p>
      <p class="muted">Module suivant : M04 — Rendez-vous & consultations.</p>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
.dash { min-height: 100vh; }
header {

.content { padding: 2rem; }

ok('Fichiers frontend écrits');

// ---------- M03/5 : Test de bout en bout ----------
log('M03/5', 'Test de bout en bout — patients & MPI');

const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
const server = spawn(
  'php',
  ['artisan', 'serve', '--host=127.0.0.1', `--port=${PORT}`],
  { cwd: API, stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true }
);

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function stopServer() {
  if (server.exitCode === null) {
    server.kill();
    await sleep(300);
  }
}

function mustBe(response, expected, label) {
  if (response.status !== expected) {
    fail(`${label} : HTTP ${expected} attendu, reçu HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  }
  ok(label);
}

function authHeaders(token) {
  return {
    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  };
}

async function login(credentials) {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    fail(`Connexion impossible pour ${credentials.email} (HTTP ${response.status}).`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).token;
}

async function switchFacility(token, facilityId) {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities/${facilityId}/switch`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(token),
  });
  if (response.status !== 200) {
    fail(`Bascule d'établissement refusée (HTTP ${response.status}).`);
  }
}

try {
  let ready = false;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30 && !ready; attempt += 1) {
    if (server.exitCode !== null) {
      fail(`Le serveur Laravel s'est arrêté (code ${server.exitCode}).`);
    }
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/health`, {
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
      });
      ready = response.ok;
    } catch {
      await sleep(1000);
    }
  }
  if (!ready) fail(`L'API n'a pas répondu sur le port ${PORT}.`);
  ok('API en ligne');

const adminToken = await login(ADMIN);
  const secretaryToken = await login(SECRETARY);
  ok('Connexions admin et secrétaire OK');

// Récupérer les établissements pour la secrétaire (CSM) et l'admin (HREF)
  const secFacilities = await (await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
  })).json();
  const csm = secFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'CSM');
  if (!csm) fail('CSM introuvable pour la secrétaire.');

const adminFacilities = await (await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/facilities`, {
    headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
  })).json();
  const href = adminFacilities.facilities.find((f) => f.code === 'HREF');
  if (!href) fail('HREF introuvable pour l\'admin.');

// La secrétaire pointe sur CSM, l'admin sur HREF pour la suite.
  await switchFacility(secretaryToken, csm.id);
  await switchFacility(adminToken, href.id);
  ok('Établissements actifs définis (secrétaire → CSM, admin → HREF)');

// Test 1 : liste patients de la secrétaire = patients de CSM uniquement
  const listResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/patients`, {
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
  });
  await mustBe(listResponse, 200, 'Liste patients accessible (secrétaire)');

const listData = await listResponse.json();
  const localCodes = listData.patients.map((p) => p.local_code);
  if (!localCodes.includes('P-CSM-0001')) {
    fail(`Le patient de démonstration P-CSM-0001 manque. Reçu : ${localCodes.join(', ')}`);
  }
    for (const code of localCodes) {
    if (!code.startsWith('P-CSM-')) {
      fail(`Isolation rompue : code local inattendu ${code}.`);
    }
  }

  const demoPatient = listData.patients[0];
  if (!demoPatient.hospiflow_code) {
    fail('Le code réseau (hospiflow_code) manque dans la réponse patient.');
  }
  ok(`Patient démo visible : ${demoPatient.local_code} / ${demoPatient.hospiflow_code}`);

// Test 2 : création d'un patient par la secrétaire → 201 + code réseau généré
  const createResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/patients`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
    body: JSON.stringify({
      first_name: 'Koffi',
      last_name: 'Adjovi',
      date_of_birth: '1985-03-02',
      gender: 'male',
      phone: '+229 96 11 22 33',
      address: 'Abomey-Calavi',
      blood_group: 'A+',
    }),
  });
  await mustBe(createResponse, 201, 'Création patient par la secrétaire (201)');

const created = (await createResponse.json()).patient;
  if (!created.hospiflow_code.startsWith('HF-')) {
    fail(`Le code réseau généré est invalide : ${created.hospiflow_code}`);
  }
  ok(`Code réseau généré automatiquement : ${created.hospiflow_code}`);

// Test 3 : recherche par nom dans l'établissement actif
  const searchResponse = await fetch(
    `${baseUrl}/api/patients/search?q=Adjovi`,
    { headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken) }
  );
  await mustBe(searchResponse, 200, 'Recherche patients (200)');

const searchResults = (await searchResponse.json()).results;
  if (searchResults.length !== 1) {
    fail(`La recherche « Adjovi » devrait renvoyer 1 patient, reçu ${searchResults.length}.`);
  }
  ok('Recherche par nom : patient trouvé');

// Test 4 : admin rattache le patient réseau de CSM dans HREF (MPI fédéré)
  const linkResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/patients`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(adminToken),
    body: JSON.stringify({
      mpi_code: demoPatient.hospiflow_code,
      first_name: demoPatient.first_name,
      last_name: demoPatient.last_name,
      date_of_birth: demoPatient.date_of_birth,
      gender: demoPatient.gender,
      phone: demoPatient.phone,
    }),
  });
  await mustBe(linkResponse, 201, 'Rattachement du patient réseau dans HREF (201)');

const linked = (await linkResponse.json()).patient;
  if (linked.hospiflow_code !== demoPatient.hospiflow_code) {
    fail('Le code réseau devrait être identique entre les deux établissements.');
  }
  if (linked.local_code === demoPatient.local_code) {
    fail('Le code local devrait être différent dans chaque établissement.');
  }
  ok(`Fédération OK : ${linked.local_code} (HREF) partage le code ${linked.hospiflow_code} avec ${demoPatient.local_code} (CSM)`);

// Test 5 : isolation — la secrétaire ne peut PAS voir le patient de HREF
  const forbiddenShow = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/patients/${linked.id}`, {
    headers: authHeaders(secretaryToken),
  });
  await mustBe(forbiddenShow, 404, 'Accès au patient d\'un autre établissement rejeté (404)');

// Test 6 : accès non authentifié rejeté
  const anonymousShow = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/patients`, {
    headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
  });
  await mustBe(anonymousShow, 401, 'Accès anonyme rejeté (401)');
} finally {
  await stopServer();
}

log('M03', 'Terminé');
console.log('');
console.log('Patients : identifiant réseau HF-… partagé entre établissements,');
console.log('codes locaux distincts par établissement, isolation vérifiée.');

===== scripts/m09-audit.mjs ===== (193 lignes, 6 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — audit du module M09 : vérification + export du code réel
// Usage : node scripts/m09-audit.mjs
// Sortie : M09-audit.txt à la racine (à uploader pour analyse)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
const API = path.join(ROOT, 'api');
const WEB = path.join(ROOT, 'web');

function walk(dir, exts) {
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      out.push(...walk(full, exts));
    } else if (exts.some((e) => entry.name.endsWith(e))) {
      out.push(full);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function read(file) {
  try {
    return fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

const checks = [];

function check(label, ok, detail) {
  checks.push({ label, ok: ok === true, detail: detail || '' });
}

// ---------- 1. Backend ----------
const controller = path.join(API, 'app', 'Http', 'Controllers', 'ReportController.php');
const controllerSrc = read(controller);
check('ReportController.php présent', controllerSrc.length > 0, path.relative(ROOT, controller));

if (controllerSrc.length > 0) {
  for (const method of ['function overview', 'function revenue', 'function stock']) {
    check('ReportController : ' + method, controllerSrc.includes(method));
  }
  check('Anti-corruption backslash', controllerSrc.includes('IlluminateSupport') === false);
  const lint = spawnSync('php', ['-l', controller], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  check('php -l ReportController.php', lint.status === 0, (lint.stdout || lint.stderr || '').trim());
}

const routesFile = path.join(API, 'routes', 'api.php');
const routesSrc = read(routesFile);
check('routes/api.php présent', routesSrc.length > 0);
check('Route reports/overview', routesSrc.includes('overview'));
check('Route reports/revenue', routesSrc.includes('revenue'));
check('Route reports/stock', routesSrc.includes('stock'));

if (routesSrc.length > 0) {
  const lintRoutes = spawnSync('php', ['-l', routesFile], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  check('php -l routes/api.php', lintRoutes.status === 0, (lintRoutes.stdout || lintRoutes.stderr || '').trim());
}

// ---------- 2. Frontend ----------
const webSources = walk(path.join(WEB, 'src'), ['.vue', '.js']);
const reportsVue = webSources.find((f) => path.basename(f).toLowerCase() === 'reports.vue');
check('Reports.vue présent', !!reportsVue, reportsVue ? path.relative(ROOT, reportsVue) : '');

let reportsSrc = '';
if (reportsVue) {
  reportsSrc = read(reportsVue);
  check('Reports.vue appelle /reports/overview', reportsSrc.includes('/reports/overview'));
  check('Reports.vue sans corruption <mark', reportsSrc.includes('<mark') === false);
}

const routerFile = webSources.find(
  (f) => path.basename(f) !== 'Reports.vue' && read(f).includes('Reports.vue')
);
check('Route câblée vers Reports.vue', !!routerFile,
  routerFile ? path.relative(ROOT, routerFile) : 'aucun fichier n\'importe Reports.vue');

let billingStillThere = false;
let menuLink = null;

for (const file of webSources) {
  const src = read(file);
  if (src.includes('BillingView') || src.includes('facturation')) {
    billingStillThere = true;
  }
  if (src.includes('Rapports') && (src.includes('/rapports') || src.includes('/reports'))) {
    menuLink = menuLink || path.relative(ROOT, file);
  }
}

check('Facturation toujours présente (non remplacée)', billingStillThere);
check('Lien/menu « Rapports » présent', !!menuLink, menuLink || '');

// ---------- 3. Scripts M09 ----------
const scriptFiles = [path.join(HERE, 'm09-reports.mjs')];
if (fs.existsSync(path.join(HERE, 'm09'))) {
  scriptFiles.push(...walk(path.join(HERE, 'm09'), ['.mjs']));
}

for (const file of scriptFiles) {
  const rel = path.relative(ROOT, file);

if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
    check('Script ' + rel, false, 'introuvable');
    continue;
  }
  const lint = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--check', file], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  check('node --check ' + rel, lint.status === 0, (lint.stderr || '').trim().slice(0, 200));
  check('Sans corruption <mark : ' + rel, read(file).includes('<mark') === false);
}

const pkg = read(path.join(ROOT, 'package.json'));
check('Script npm m09 présent', pkg.includes('"m09"'));

// ---------- Rapport console ----------
console.log('');
console.log('=== AUDIT M09 ===');

let failures = 0;

for (const c of checks) {
  const icon = c.ok ? '✓' : '✗';

if (c.ok === false) {
    failures += 1;
  }
  console.log('  ' + icon + ' ' + c.label + (c.detail ? ' — ' + c.detail : ''));
}

console.log('');

if (failures === 0) {
  console.log('M09 : toutes les vérifications passent');
} else {
  console.log('M09 : ' + failures + ' vérification(s) en échec');
}

// ---------- Export du code réel ----------
const dumpFiles = [];

if (controllerSrc.length > 0) {
  dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, controller), controllerSrc]);
}
if (routesSrc.length > 0) {
  dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, routesFile), routesSrc]);
}
if (reportsSrc.length > 0) {
  dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, reportsVue), reportsSrc]);
}
if (routerFile) {
  dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, routerFile), read(routerFile)]);
}
for (const f of scriptFiles) {
  if (fs.existsSync(f)) {
    dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, f), read(f)]);
  }
}
const billingVue = webSources.find((f) => path.basename(f).toLowerCase().includes('billing'));

if (billingVue) {
  dumpFiles.push([path.relative(ROOT, billingVue), read(billingVue)]);
}

let out = 'HOSPIFLOW — AUDIT M09 — ' + new Date().toISOString() + '\n\n';
out += '=== RÉSULTATS DES VÉRIFICATIONS ===\n';

for (const c of checks) {
  const tag = c.ok ? '[OK]    ' : '[ECHEC] ';
  out += tag + c.label + (c.detail ? ' — ' + c.detail : '') + '\n';
}

out += '\n=== CONTENU INTÉGRAL DES FICHIERS ===\n';

for (const entry of dumpFiles) {
  out += '\n[FICHIER: ' + entry[0] + ']\n---8<---\n' + entry[1] + '\n---8<---\n[FIN: ' + entry[0] + ']\n';
}

const auditPath = path.join(ROOT, 'M09-audit.txt');
fs.writeFileSync(auditPath, out, 'utf8');

console.log('Code réel exporté : M09-audit.txt ('
  + Math.round(fs.statSync(auditPath).size / 1024) + ' Ko) — à uploader pour analyse');

process.exit(failures === 0 ? 0 : 1);

===== scripts/update-context-m09.mjs ===== (75 lignes, 3 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — context.md : M09 terminé + conventions consolidées
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const FILE = path.join(ROOT, "context.md");
let md = fs.readFileSync(FILE, "utf8");
let changed = false;

fs.copyFileSync(FILE, FILE + ".bak");

// --- 1. Tableau : marquer M09 Terminé (ligne par ligne, sans regex) ---
const lines = md.split("\n");
let foundRow = false;

for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  const line = lines[i];
  if (line.startsWith("| M09 |")) {
    foundRow = true;
    if (line.includes("Terminé")) {
      console.log("= M09 déjà marqué Terminé");
      break;
    }
    if (line.includes("À faire")) {
      lines[i] = line.replace("À faire", "Terminé");
      changed = true;
      console.log("✓ M09 marqué Terminé");
      break;
    }
    console.log("⚠ Ligne M09 trouvée mais sans « À faire » — vérifiez :");
    console.log("  " + line);
    break;
  }
}

if (!foundRow) {
  console.log("⚠ Aucune ligne « | M09 | » dans le tableau — statut à corriger à la main");
}

md = lines.join("\n");

// --- 2. Retirer M09 de la section « Reste à faire » ---
const before = md;
md = md.split("\n").filter((l) => !/^- M09 :/.test(l)).join("\n");

if (md !== before) {

  console.log("✓ M09 retiré de « Reste à faire »");
}

// --- 3. Ajouter les conventions M09 ---
const MARK = "## Conventions techniques consolidées (M09)";

if (!md.includes(MARK)) {
  md += "\n\n" + MARK + "\n\n"
    + "- Rapports : GET /api/reports/overview, /api/reports/revenue, /api/reports/stock (permission reports.view).\n"

    + "- Correctifs acquis : appels api(chemin) sans enveloppe (p), jeton d'authentification sur tous les appels /reports/*.\n";
  changed = true;
  console.log("✓ Conventions M09 ajoutées");
} else {
  console.log("= Conventions M09 déjà présentes");
}

if (changed) {
  fs.writeFileSync(FILE, md, "utf8");
  console.log("context.md à jour (sauvegarde : context.md.bak).");
} else {

===== scripts/update-context-m10.mjs ===== (73 lignes, 3 Ko)
#!/usr/bin/env node
// HOSPIFLOW — context.md : M10 terminé + MVP complet
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const FILE = path.join(ROOT, "context.md");
let md = fs.readFileSync(FILE, "utf8");
let changed = false;

fs.copyFileSync(FILE, FILE + ".bak");

// --- 1. Tableau : marquer M10 Terminé ---
const lines = md.split("\n");
let foundRow = false;

for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  const line = lines[i];
  if (line.startsWith("| M10 |")) {
    foundRow = true;
    if (line.includes("Terminé")) {
      console.log("= M10 déjà marqué Terminé");
    } else if (line.includes("À faire") || line.includes("En cours")) {
      lines[i] = line.replace(/À faire|En cours/, "Terminé");
      changed = true;
      console.log("✓ M10 marqué Terminé");
    } else {
      console.log("⚠ Ligne M10 trouvée mais statut non reconnu :");
      console.log("  " + line);
    }
    break;
  }
}

if (!foundRow) {
  console.log("⚠ Aucune ligne « | M10 | » dans le tableau — statut à corriger à la main");
}

md = lines.join("\n");

// --- 2. Retirer M10 de « Reste à faire » ---
const before = md;
md = md.split("\n").filter((l) => !/^- M10 :/.test(l)).join("\n");
if (md !== before) {
  changed = true;
  console.log("✓ M10 retiré de « Reste à faire »");
}

// --- 3. Section MVP complet + conventions M10 ---
const MARK = "## Conventions techniques consolidées (M10)";

if (!md.includes(MARK)) {
  md += "\n\n" + MARK + "\n\n"
    + "- MVP COMPLET : M00 → M10 tous terminés (" + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + ").\n"
    + "- PWA : web/public/manifest.webmanifest + web/public/sw.js (précache shell, cache-first assets, /api non intercepté).\n"
    + "- Hors ligne : web/src/api/offline.js + offline-db.js — file IndexedDB (store ops, clé X-Idempotency-Key).\n"
    + "- client.js v2 : écritures hors ligne mises en file ({ queued: true }), rejeu à la reconnexion (online + load).\n"
    + "- Backend : middleware IdempotencyKey (cache 24 h, X-Idempotent-Replay: true sur rejeu).\n"
    + "- En production : HTTPS obligatoire (Service Worker), npm run build, serveur web (Nginx/Apache) pour web/dist.\n";
  changed = true;
  console.log("✓ Conventions M10 + MVP complet ajoutés");
} else {
  console.log("= Conventions M10 déjà présentes");
}

if (changed) {
  fs.writeFileSync(FILE, md, "utf8");
  console.log("context.md à jour (sauvegarde : context.md.bak).");
} else {
  console.log("= context.md déjà à jour — aucune modification.");
}


===== tools/fixes/lib/fs-utils.mjs ===== (29 lignes, 1 Ko)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
const BAK_SUFFIX = '.hospiflow-vague1.bak';
export function readFile(absPath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(absPath)) throw new Error(`Fichier introuvable: ${absPath}`);
  return fs.readFileSync(absPath, 'utf8');
}
export function backupOnce(absPath) {
  const bak = absPath + BAK_SUFFIX;
  if (!fs.existsSync(bak)) fs.copyFileSync(absPath, bak);
  return bak;
}
export function writeFile(absPath, content) {
  const dir = path.dirname(absPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(absPath, content, 'utf8');
}
export function lineDelta(before, after) {
  const b = before.split('\n'); const a = after.split('\n');
  const maxLen = Math.max(b.length, a.length);
  let added = 0, removed = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < maxLen; i++) {
    if (b[i] === undefined) added++;
    else if (a[i] === undefined) removed++;
    else if (b[i] !== a[i]) { added++; removed++; }
  }
  return { added, removed };
}


===== tools/fixes/lib/logger.mjs ===== (29 lignes, 1 Ko)
const C = {
  reset: '\x1b[0m', bold: '\x1b[1m', dim: '\x1b[2m',
  red: '\x1b[31m', green: '\x1b[32m', yellow: '\x1b[33m',
  blue: '\x1b[34m', magenta: '\x1b[35m', cyan: '\x1b[36m',
};

export const log = {
  title: (s) => console.log(`\n${C.bold}${C.magenta}▶ ${s}${C.reset}`),
  info:  (s) => console.log(`${C.cyan}ℹ${C.reset} ${s}`),
  ok:    (s) => console.log(`${C.green}✔${C.reset} ${s}`),
  warn:  (s) => console.log(`${C.yellow}⚠${C.reset} ${s}`),
  err:   (s) => console.log(`${C.red}✖${C.reset} ${s}`),
  skip:  (s) => console.log(`${C.dim}○ ${s}${C.reset}`),
};

export function summary(results) {
  const applied = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'applied').length;
  const skipped = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'skipped').length;
  const failed  = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'failed').length;
  log.title('Résumé');
  for (const r of results) {
    const icon = { applied: '✔', skipped: '○', failed: '✖' }[r.status];
    const color = { applied: C.green, skipped: C.dim, failed: C.red }[r.status];
    console.log(`  ${color}${icon}${C.reset} ${r.id.padEnd(35)} ${r.message ?? ''}`);
  }
  console.log(`\n${C.bold}Total${C.reset}: ${C.green}${applied} appliqués${C.reset}, ${C.dim}${skipped} ignorés${C.reset}, ${C.red}${failed} échoués${C.reset}\n`);
  if (failed > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
}


===== tools/fixes/lib/patcher.mjs ===== (82 lignes, 3 Ko)
import { readFile, writeFile, backupOnce, lineDelta } from './fs-utils.mjs';

function escapeRegex(s) {
  return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

function applyOps(content, ops) {
  let mutated = content;
  for (const op of ops) {
    if (op.type === 'replace') {
      if (mutated.includes(op.from)) {
        mutated = mutated.split(op.from).join(op.to);
        continue;
      }
      const flexibleFrom = op.from.replace(/^ +/gm, '');
      if (mutated.includes(flexibleFrom)) {
        mutated = mutated.split(flexibleFrom).join(op.to.replace(/^ +/gm, ''));
        continue;
      }
      const fromLines = op.from.split('\n');
      const toLines = op.to.split('\n');
      const firstLine = fromLines[0].trimStart();
      const regex = new RegExp('^( *)' + escapeRegex(firstLine), 'm');
      const m = mutated.match(regex);
      if (m) {
        const indent = m[1];
        const normalizedFrom = fromLines.map((l) => l.trimStart()).join('\n');
        const normalizedTo = toLines.map((l) => indent + l.trimStart()).join('\n');
        if (mutated.includes(normalizedFrom)) {
          mutated = mutated.split(normalizedFrom).join(normalizedTo);
          continue;
        }
      }
      throw new Error('motif introuvable: ' + op.from.slice(0, 60) + '...');
    } else if (op.type === 'insertBefore') {
      if (mutated.includes(op.anchor)) {
        mutated = mutated.replace(op.anchor, op.text + op.anchor);
        continue;
      }
      const flexible = op.anchor.replace(/^ +/gm, '');
      if (mutated.includes(flexible)) {
        mutated = mutated.replace(flexible, op.text.replace(/^ +/gm, '') + flexible);
        continue;
      }
      throw new Error('ancre introuvable: ' + op.anchor.slice(0, 60) + '...');
    } else {
      throw new Error('type d\'op inconnu: ' + op.type);
    }
  }
  return mutated;
}

export function applyPatch(rootDir, patch, { dryRun = false } = {}) {
  const absPath = (rootDir + '/' + patch.file).replace(/\\\\/g, '/');
  let content;
  try { content = readFile(absPath); }
  catch (e) { return { id: patch.id, status: 'failed', message: e.message }; }
  if (patch.marker && content.includes(patch.marker)) {
    return { id: patch.id, status: 'skipped', message: 'déjà appliqué' };
  }
  let mutated;
  try {
    mutated = patch.customApply ? patch.newContent : applyOps(content, patch.ops);
  } catch (e) {
    return { id: patch.id, status: 'failed', message: e.message };
  }
  if (mutated === content) {
    return { id: patch.id, status: 'skipped', message: 'aucun changement' };
  }
  const delta = lineDelta(content, mutated);
  if (!dryRun) { backupOnce(absPath); writeFile(absPath, mutated); }
  if (patch.extraFiles) {
    for (const ef of patch.extraFiles) {
      const abs = (rootDir + '/' + ef.file).replace(/\\\\/g, '/');
      const current = (() => { try { return readFile(abs); } catch { return ''; } })();
      if (current.includes(ef.marker)) continue;
      if (!dryRun) { try { backupOnce(abs); } catch {} writeFile(abs, ef.content); }
    }
  }
  return { id: patch.id, status: 'applied', message: '+' + delta.added + '/-' + delta.removed + (dryRun ? ' (dry)' : ''), delta };
}

      to: "$w->whereRaw($this->portableLike('first_name'), [$this->likePattern($q)])\n                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('last_name'), [$this->likePattern($q)])\n                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('local_code'), [$this->likePattern($q)])\n                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('phone'), [$this->likePattern($q)])\n                    ->orWhereHas('mpi', function ($m) use ($q) {\n                        $m->whereRaw('LOWER(hospiflow_code) LIKE LOWER(?)', [$this->likePattern($q)]);\n                    });",

===== tools/fixes/patches/06-clean-agents-md.mjs ===== (59 lignes, 2 Ko)
const NEW_AGENTS = `# HOSPIFLOW — Conventions projet

> Ce fichier remplace le bootstrap Laravel Boost (Vague 1).

## Stack

- Laravel 13 (API REST), PHP 8.3+, Sanctum (auth), Spatie Permissions (RBAC).
- Base : SQLite en dev, PostgreSQL en prod.
- Frontend SPA séparé (hors de api/).

## Architecture

- Multi-tenant par établissement : toute requête métier scopée via currentFacility().
- Un contrôleur = un module (M00→M10).
- Permissions : patients., appointments., consultations., lab., pharmacy., billing., reports., facilities.

## Conventions

- Messages utilisateur en français, code en anglais.
- Validation dans \`$request->validate([...])\` (Form Requests en Vague 2).
- Toujours répondre en JSON. Erreurs via \`abort(4xx, 'message FR')\`.
- Pas d'appel \`request()\` global dans les closures de transaction.

## Base de données

- Portabilité SQLite/PostgreSQL obligatoire : pas de ilike, to_char, extract.
- Utiliser LOWER(col) LIKE LOWER(?) et calculer les dérivés en PHP.
- Index sur facility_id, patient_id, status, scheduled_at.

## Sécurité

- Jamais de \`exists:users,email\` ni de message permettant d'énumérer les comptes.
- Routes protégées par auth:sanctum (sauf /login).
- Chaque contrôleur vérifie currentFacility()->is_active.

## Tests

- php artisan test avant chaque commit.
- Chaque contrôleur : Feature test happy path + 403 + 422.
`;

Voir AGENTS.md pour les conventions projet.

===== tools/fixes/run.mjs ===== (41 lignes, 2 Ko)
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { log, summary } from './lib/logger.mjs';
import { applyPatch } from './lib/patcher.mjs';
import p01 from './patches/01-patient-search-portable.mjs';
import p02 from './patches/02-report-portable.mjs';
import p03 from './patches/03-patient-update-fix.mjs';
import p04 from './patches/04-pharmacy-notes-fix.mjs';
import p05 from './patches/05-attach-user-secure.mjs';
import p06 from './patches/06-clean-agents-md.mjs';
import p07 from './patches/07-appointment-indent.mjs';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const dryRun = args.includes('--dry-run');
const onlyIdx = args.indexOf('--only');
const only = onlyIdx !== -1 ? args[onlyIdx + 1] : null;
const ALL = [p01, p02, p03, p04, p05, p06, p07];
const patches = only ? ALL.filter((p) => p.id === only) : ALL;

log.title(`HOSPIFLOW — Vague 1 ${dryRun ? '(DRY-RUN)' : ''}`);
log.info(`Racine projet : ${ROOT}`);
log.info(`Patches à exécuter : ${patches.length}`);

const results = [];
for (const patch of patches) {
  try {
    const r = applyPatch(ROOT, patch, { dryRun });
    results.push(r);
    if (r.status === 'applied') log.ok(`${patch.id} — ${r.message}`);
    else if (r.status === 'skipped') log.skip(`${patch.id} — ${r.message}`);
    else log.err(`${patch.id} — ${r.message}`);
  } catch (e) {
    results.push({ id: patch.id, status: 'failed', message: e.message });
    log.err(`${patch.id} — exception: ${e.message}`);
  }
}
summary(results);
if (dryRun) log.warn('DRY-RUN : aucun fichier modifié.');


===== web/src/api/client.js ===== (27 lignes, 1 Ko)
const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api';

export async function api(path, { method = 'GET', body, token, facilityId } = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...(facilityId ? { 'X-Facility-Id': String(facilityId) } : {}),
    },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });

const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.message ||
        (data.errors && Object.values(data.errors).flat().join(' ')) ||
        `Erreur HTTP ${response.status}`
    );
  }

return data;
}

import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';
import LoginView from '../views/LoginView.vue';

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView },
    {
      path: '/',
      component: () => import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facilities',
      component: () => import('../views/FacilitiesView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/patients',
      component: () => import('../views/PatientsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/appointments',
      component: () => import('../views/AppointmentsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/consultations',
      component: () => import('../views/ConsultationsView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/laboratoire',
      component: () => import('../views/LabView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/pharmacie',
      component: () => import('../views/PharmacyView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/facturation',
      component: () => import('../views/BillingView.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/rapports',
      component: () => import('../views/Reports.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    {
      path: '/rapports',
      component: () => import('../views/Reports.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
        {
      path: '/rapports',
      component: () => import('../views/Reports.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
    },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
});

router.beforeEach((to) => {
  const auth = useAuthStore();

if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/login';
  }

if (to.path === '/login' && auth.isAuthenticated) {
    return '/';
  }
});

export default router;


===== web/src/stores/auth.js ===== (68 lignes, 2 Ko)
import { defineStore } from 'pinia';
import { api } from '../api/client';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'hospiflow_token';
const USER_KEY = 'hospiflow_user';
const FACILITY_KEY = 'hospiflow_facility';

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', {
  state: () => ({
    token: localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY),
    user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY) || 'null'),
    currentFacilityId: localStorage.getItem(FACILITY_KEY),
  }),

getters: {
    isAuthenticated: (state) => Boolean(state.token),
    roles: (state) => state.user?.roles || [],
    canManageFacilities: (state) =>
      (state.user?.permissions || []).includes('facilities.manage'),
  },

actions: {
    async login(email, password) {
      const data = await api('/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { email, password },
      });

this.token = data.token;
      this.user = data.user;

localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, this.token);
      localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.user));
    },

async loadFacilities() {
      const data = await api('/facilities', { token: this.token });
      this.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
      localStorage.setItem(FACILITY_KEY, this.currentFacilityId || '');
      return data.facilities;
    },

async switchFacility(facilityId) {
      const data = await api('/facilities/' + facilityId + '/switch', {
        method: 'POST',
        token: this.token,
      });
      this.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
      localStorage.setItem(FACILITY_KEY, this.currentFacilityId || '');
    },

async logout() {
      try {
        await api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', token: this.token });
      } catch {
        // Token peut être déjà révoqué ou expiré.
      }

this.token = null;
      this.user = null;
      this.currentFacilityId = null;
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(FACILITY_KEY);
    },
  },
});


===== web/src/views/AppointmentsView.vue ===== (272 lignes, 8 Ko)
<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const appointments = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const practitioners = ref([]);
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const filterDate = ref(today);

const patientId = ref('');
const practitionerId = ref('');
const scheduledAt = ref('');
const durationMinutes = ref(30);
const reason = ref('');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  scheduled: 'Planifié',
  confirmed: 'Confirmé',
  done: 'Réalisé',
  cancelled: 'Annulé',
  no_show: 'Absent',
};

async function loadAppointments() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = filterDate.value
      ? '?date=' + encodeURIComponent(filterDate.value)
      : '';
    const data = await api('/appointments' + params, { token: auth.token });
    appointments.value = data.appointments;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function loadReferences() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
  try {
    const practitionersData = await api('/appointments/practitioners', {
      token: auth.token,
    });
    practitioners.value = practitionersData.practitioners;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createAppointment() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/appointments', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        practitioner_id: Number(practitionerId.value),
        scheduled_at: scheduledAt.value,
        duration_minutes: Number(durationMinutes.value),
        reason: reason.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    practitionerId.value = '';
    scheduledAt.value = '';
    durationMinutes.value = 30;
    reason.value = '';
    await loadAppointments();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(appointmentId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/appointments/' + appointmentId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadAppointments();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadReferences();
  await loadAppointments();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Rendez-vous</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadAppointments">
        <label>
          Date
          <input v-model="filterDate" type="date" />
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
        <button type="button" @click="filterDate = ''">Tout</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="appointments.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Heure</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Praticien</th>
            <th>Motif</th>
            <th>Durée</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="appointment in appointments" :key="appointment.id">
            <td>{{ appointment.scheduled_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ appointment.patient.last_name }} {{ appointment.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ appointment.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ appointment.practitioner.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ appointment.reason || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ appointment.duration_minutes }} min</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="appointment.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[appointment.status] || appointment.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="appointment.status === 'scheduled'"
                @click="setStatus(appointment.id, 'confirmed')"
              >
                Confirmer
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="appointment.status === 'confirmed'"
                @click="setStatus(appointment.id, 'done')"
              >
                Réalisé
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="appointment.status !== 'cancelled'"
                @click="setStatus(appointment.id, 'cancelled')"
              >
                Annuler
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else-if="!loading">Aucun rendez-vous pour cette date.</p>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createAppointment">
        <h2>Nouveau rendez-vous</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Praticien
          <select v-model="practitionerId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un praticien —</option>
            <option
              v-for="practitioner in practitioners"
              :key="practitioner.id"
              :value="practitioner.id"
            >
              {{ practitioner.name }}
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Date et heure
          <input v-model="scheduledAt" type="datetime-local" required />
        </label>

<label>
          Durée (minutes)
          <input v-model="durationMinutes" type="number" min="5" max="480" step="5" />
        </label>

<label>
          Motif
          <input v-model="reason" placeholder="Consultation générale…" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Enregistrer</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

===== web/src/views/BillingView.vue ===== (354 lignes, 11 Ko)
<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const invoices = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const filterPatientId = ref('');
const filterStatus = ref('');

const patientId = ref('');
const dueDate = ref('');
const lines = ref([{ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 }]);

const payTarget = ref(null);
const payAmount = ref('');
const payMethod = ref('cash');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  draft: 'Brouillon',
  issued: 'Émise',
  paid: 'Payée',
  cancelled: 'Annulée',
};

const METHOD_LABELS = {
  cash: 'Espèces',
  mobile_money: 'Mobile Money',
  transfer: 'Virement',
};

function addLine() {
  lines.value.push({ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 });
}

function removeLine(index) {
  if (lines.value.length > 1) {
    lines.value.splice(index, 1);
  }
}

function formatXof(amount) {
  return Number(amount).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' XOF';
}

async function loadInvoices() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

if (filterPatientId.value) params.set('patient_id', filterPatientId.value);
    if (filterStatus.value) params.set('status', filterStatus.value);

const suffix = params.toString() ? '?' + params.toString() : '';
    const data = await api('/invoices' + suffix, { token: auth.token });
    invoices.value = data.invoices;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createInvoice() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        due_date: dueDate.value || null,
        items: lines.value
          .filter((line) => line.label.trim() && line.quantity > 0)
          .map((line) => ({
            label: line.label.trim(),
            quantity: Number(line.quantity),
            unit_price: Number(line.unit_price),
          })),
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    dueDate.value = '';
    lines.value = [{ label: '', quantity: 1, unit_price: 0 }];
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(invoiceId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices/' + invoiceId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

function openPayForm(invoice) {
  payTarget.value = invoice;
  payAmount.value = invoice.remaining_amount;
  payMethod.value = 'cash';
}

async function submitPayment() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/invoices/' + payTarget.value.id + '/payments', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        amount: Number(payAmount.value),
        method: payMethod.value,
      },
    });
    payTarget.value = null;
    await loadInvoices();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadInvoices();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/laboratoire">Laboratoire</router-link>
        <router-link to="/pharmacie">Pharmacie</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Facturation</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadInvoices">
        <label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="filterPatientId">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Statut
          <select v-model="filterStatus">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option value="draft">Brouillon</option>
            <option value="issued">Émise</option>
            <option value="paid">Payée</option>
            <option value="cancelled">Annulée</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="invoices.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>N°</th>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Total</th>
            <th>Payé</th>
            <th>Reste</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="invoice in invoices" :key="invoice.id">
            <td>{{ invoice.number }}</td>
            <td>{{ invoice.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ invoice.patient.last_name }} {{ invoice.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ invoice.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.total_amount) }}</td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.paid_amount) }}</td>
            <td>{{ formatXof(invoice.remaining_amount) }}</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="invoice.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[invoice.status] || invoice.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'draft'"
                @click="setStatus(invoice.id, 'issued')"
              >
                Émettre
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'issued'"
                @click="openPayForm(invoice)"
              >
                Encaisser
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="invoice.status === 'draft' || invoice.status === 'issued'"
                @click="setStatus(invoice.id, 'cancelled')"
              >
                Annuler
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else>Aucune facture pour ce filtre.</p>

<form v-if="payTarget" class="card" @submit.prevent="submitPayment">
        <h2>Encaissement — {{ payTarget.number }} (reste {{ formatXof(payTarget.remaining_amount) }})</h2>

<label>
          Montant (XOF)
          <input v-model="payAmount" type="number" min="1" required />
        </label>

<label>
          Mode
          <select v-model="payMethod">
            <option value="cash">Espèces</option>
            <option value="mobile_money">Mobile Money</option>
            <option value="transfer">Virement</option>
          </select>
        </label>

<button type="submit">Enregistrer le paiement</button>
        <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="payTarget = null">
          Annuler
        </button>
      </form>

<form class="card" @submit.prevent="createInvoice">
        <h2>Nouvelle facture</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Échéance
          <input v-model="dueDate" type="date" />
        </label>

<fieldset class="lines">
          <legend>Lignes de facture</legend>

<div v-for="(line, index) in lines" :key="index" class="line">
            <label class="grow">
              Libellé
              <input v-model="line.label" placeholder="Consultation générale" />
            </label>
            <label>
              Qté
              <input v-model="line.quantity" type="number" min="1" />
            </label>
            <label>
              Prix unitaire
              <input v-model="line.unit_price" type="number" min="0" />
            </label>
            <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="removeLine(index)">×</button>
          </div>

<button type="button" class="secondary" @click="addLine">Ajouter une ligne</button>
        </fieldset>

<button type="submit">Créer la facture</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>
